================================================================================
FILE: .\adapters\base.py
================================================================================
"""Common interface for platform adapters (implemented from phase 2)."""
from __future__ import annotations

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from dataclasses import dataclass, field


@dataclass
class PostPayload:
    text: str
    image_path: str | None = None
    image_url: str | None = None
    title: str | None = None
    link: str | None = None
    extra: dict = field(default_factory=dict)


@dataclass
class PostResult:
    platform: str
    ok: bool
    post_id: str | None = None
    url: str | None = None
    error_code: str | None = None  # AUTH, RATE_LIMIT, VALIDATION, NETWORK, PLATFORM, UNKNOWN
    error_message: str | None = None
    retryable: bool = False
    needs_check: bool = False  # outcome unknown: the post may have gone through


class PlatformAdapter(ABC):
    name: str
    needs_public_image: bool = False  # True if the platform fetches the image from a public URL
    last_account: str | None = None
    last_error: str | None = None

    @abstractmethod
    def validate(self, payload: PostPayload) -> list[str]:
        """Return problems; empty list means the payload can be posted."""

    @abstractmethod
    def post(self, payload: PostPayload) -> PostResult: ...

    def prepare_image(self, path: str) -> str:
        return path

    def check_auth(self) -> bool:
        return False




================================================================================
FILE: .\adapters\facebook.py
================================================================================
from __future__ import annotations

import os

import requests

from promo_studio.adapters.base import PlatformAdapter, PostPayload, PostResult
from promo_studio.adapters.graph import GraphError, call
from promo_studio.core.validators import validate_post


class FacebookAdapter(PlatformAdapter):
    """Posts to a Facebook Page (personal profiles are not supported by the API)."""

    name = "facebook"
    HOST = "https://graph.facebook.com"

    def __init__(self, page_id: str, token: str, version: str = "v25.0", http=requests):
        self.page_id, self.token, self.version, self.http = page_id, token, version, http

    def _url(self, path: str) -> str:
        return f"{self.HOST}/{self.version}/{path}"

    def validate(self, payload: PostPayload) -> list[str]:
        problems = validate_post("facebook", payload.text)
        if payload.image_path and not os.path.exists(payload.image_path):
            problems.append("Image file not found.")
        return problems

    def check_auth(self) -> bool:
        try:
            body = call(self.http, "GET", self._url(self.page_id),
                        params={"fields": "name", "access_token": self.token})
            self.last_account, self.last_error = body.get("name") or self.page_id, None
            return True
        except GraphError as exc:
            self.last_account, self.last_error = None, exc.message
            return False

    def post(self, payload: PostPayload) -> PostResult:
        try:
            if payload.image_path:
                with open(payload.image_path, "rb") as fh:
                    body = call(self.http, "POST", self._url(f"{self.page_id}/photos"),
                                data={"message": payload.text, "published": "true", "access_token": self.token},
                                files={"source": fh}, timeout=90)
            else:
                data = {"message": payload.text, "access_token": self.token}
                if payload.link:
                    data["link"] = payload.link
                body = call(self.http, "POST", self._url(f"{self.page_id}/feed"), data=data)
        except GraphError as exc:
            return exc.to_result(self.name)
        except OSError:
            return PostResult(self.name, False, error_code="VALIDATION", error_message="Could not read the image file.")
        post_id = str(body.get("post_id") or body.get("id") or "")
        if not post_id:
            return PostResult(self.name, False, error_code="UNKNOWN", error_message="Facebook returned no post id.",
                              needs_check=True)
        return PostResult(self.name, True, post_id=post_id, url=f"https://www.facebook.com/{post_id}")




================================================================================
FILE: .\adapters\graph.py
================================================================================
"""Shared HTTP + error handling for Meta Graph API calls (Facebook and Instagram)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import requests

from promo_studio.adapters.base import PostResult

_RATE_CODES = {4, 17, 32, 613, 80001, 80002, 80003, 80004, 80005, 80006, 80008}


class GraphError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, error_code: str, message: str, retryable: bool = False):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.error_code, self.message, self.retryable = error_code, message, retryable

    def to_result(self, platform: str) -> PostResult:
        return PostResult(platform, False, error_code=self.error_code, error_message=self.message,
                          retryable=self.retryable)


def classify(status: int, body) -> GraphError:
    err = body.get("error") if isinstance(body, dict) else None
    if isinstance(err, dict):
        code, text = err.get("code"), str(err.get("message", "Unknown error"))[:240]
        if code in (102, 190):
            return GraphError("AUTH", "Access token expired or invalid. Reconnect or refresh it in Settings.")
        if code in _RATE_CODES:
            return GraphError("RATE_LIMIT", f"Rate limit reached: {text}", retryable=True)
        if code == 10 or (isinstance(code, int) and 200 <= code <= 299):
            return GraphError("AUTH", f"Missing permission: {text}")
        if code in (1, 2, 9007):
            return GraphError("PLATFORM", f"Temporary platform problem: {text}", retryable=True)
        if code == 100:
            return GraphError("VALIDATION", f"Rejected by the platform: {text}")
        return GraphError("PLATFORM", text, retryable=status >= 500)
    if status == 429:
        return GraphError("RATE_LIMIT", "Too many requests.", retryable=True)
    if status in (401, 403):
        return GraphError("AUTH", "Not authorized. Check the token in Settings.")
    if status >= 500:
        return GraphError("PLATFORM", f"Platform error (HTTP {status}).", retryable=True)
    return GraphError("UNKNOWN", f"Unexpected response (HTTP {status}).")


def call(http, method: str, url: str, *, timeout: int = 30, **kwargs) -> dict:
    """Make a request and return the JSON body, or raise GraphError. Never leaks the URL in messages."""
    try:
        response = http.request(method, url, timeout=timeout, **kwargs)
    except requests.Timeout:
        raise GraphError("NETWORK", "The request timed out.", retryable=True) from None
    except requests.RequestException as exc:
        raise GraphError("NETWORK", f"Network problem ({type(exc).__name__}).", retryable=True) from None
    try:
        body = response.json()
    except ValueError:
        body = {}
    if response.status_code >= 400 or (isinstance(body, dict) and "error" in body):
        raise classify(response.status_code, body)
    return body if isinstance(body, dict) else {}




================================================================================
FILE: .\adapters\instagram.py
================================================================================
from __future__ import annotations

import time

import requests

from promo_studio.adapters.base import PlatformAdapter, PostPayload, PostResult
from promo_studio.adapters.graph import GraphError, call
from promo_studio.core.validators import validate_post


class InstagramAdapter(PlatformAdapter):
    """Instagram API with Instagram Login (no Facebook Page needed). Business/Creator accounts only.

    Flow: create a media container from a public image URL, wait until it is FINISHED, publish it.
    """

    name = "instagram"
    needs_public_image = True
    HOST = "https://graph.instagram.com"

    def __init__(self, ig_user_id: str, token: str, version: str = "v25.0", http=requests,
                 sleep=time.sleep, poll_attempts: int = 5, poll_interval: float = 3.0):
        self.ig_user_id, self.token, self.version, self.http = ig_user_id, token, version, http
        self._sleep, self.poll_attempts, self.poll_interval = sleep, poll_attempts, poll_interval

    def _url(self, path: str) -> str:
        return f"{self.HOST}/{self.version}/{path}"

    @property
    def _headers(self) -> dict:
        return {"Authorization": f"Bearer {self.token}"}

    def validate(self, payload: PostPayload) -> list[str]:
        return validate_post("instagram", payload.text, has_image=bool(payload.image_path or payload.image_url))

    def check_auth(self) -> bool:
        try:
            body = call(self.http, "GET", self._url(self.ig_user_id), headers=self._headers,
                        params={"fields": "username"})
            name = body.get("username")
            self.last_account, self.last_error = f"@{name}" if name else self.ig_user_id, None
            return True
        except GraphError as exc:
            self.last_account, self.last_error = None, exc.message
            return False

    def refresh_token(self) -> tuple[str, int]:
        """Extend a long-lived token (about 60 days). Returns (new_token, seconds_valid)."""
        body = call(self.http, "GET", f"{self.HOST}/refresh_access_token",
                    params={"grant_type": "ig_refresh_token", "access_token": self.token})
        return str(body["access_token"]), int(body.get("expires_in", 0))

    def post(self, payload: PostPayload) -> PostResult:
        if not payload.image_url:
            return PostResult(self.name, False, error_code="VALIDATION",
                              error_message="The image must be uploaded to a public URL first.")
        try:
            container = call(self.http, "POST", self._url(f"{self.ig_user_id}/media"), headers=self._headers,
                             json={"image_url": payload.image_url, "caption": payload.text})["id"]
        except GraphError as exc:
            return exc.to_result(self.name)
        except KeyError:
            return PostResult(self.name, False, error_code="UNKNOWN", error_message="Instagram returned no container id.")

        not_ready = self._wait_until_ready(container)
        if not_ready:
            return not_ready
        try:
            media_id = call(self.http, "POST", self._url(f"{self.ig_user_id}/media_publish"),
                            headers=self._headers, json={"creation_id": container})["id"]
        except GraphError as exc:
            if exc.retryable:  # the request may have reached Instagram: never auto-retry a publish
                return PostResult(self.name, False, error_code=exc.error_code, needs_check=True,
                                  error_message="Could not confirm whether Instagram published it. "
                                                "Check the profile before posting again.")
            return exc.to_result(self.name)
        except KeyError:
            return PostResult(self.name, False, error_code="UNKNOWN", needs_check=True,
                              error_message="Instagram gave no media id. Check the profile before posting again.")
        return PostResult(self.name, True, post_id=str(media_id), url=self._permalink(str(media_id)))

    def _wait_until_ready(self, container: str) -> PostResult | None:
        for attempt in range(self.poll_attempts):
            try:
                status = call(self.http, "GET", self._url(container), headers=self._headers,
                              params={"fields": "status_code"}).get("status_code")
            except GraphError:
                return None  # cannot read the status: try publishing anyway
            if status == "FINISHED":
                return None
            if status in ("ERROR", "EXPIRED"):
                return PostResult(self.name, False, error_code="PLATFORM",
                                  error_message=f"Instagram could not process the image ({status}). Check that the "
                                                "image URL is reachable and the file is a JPEG.")
            if attempt < self.poll_attempts - 1:
                self._sleep(self.poll_interval)
        return PostResult(self.name, False, error_code="PLATFORM", retryable=True,
                          error_message="Instagram is still processing the image.")

    def _permalink(self, media_id: str) -> str:
        try:
            body = call(self.http, "GET", self._url(media_id), headers=self._headers, params={"fields": "permalink"})
            return body.get("permalink") or "https://www.instagram.com/"
        except GraphError:
            return "https://www.instagram.com/"

================================================================================
FILE: .\ai\base.py
================================================================================
from __future__ import annotations

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod


class AIError(Exception):
    """Any failure talking to an AI provider."""


class RateLimitError(AIError):
    """Quota or rate limit reached: worth retrying after a wait."""


class AIProvider(ABC):
    name: str = "base"

    @abstractmethod
    def generate(self, system: str, user: str, temperature: float = 0.8) -> str:
        """Return the model's raw text (expected to be JSON)."""

    def health_check(self) -> bool:
        try:
            return bool(self.generate("Reply with the JSON {\"ok\": true} only.", "ping", 0))
        except AIError:
            return False




================================================================================
FILE: .\ai\gemini.py
================================================================================
from __future__ import annotations

from promo_studio.ai.base import AIError, AIProvider, RateLimitError


class GeminiProvider(AIProvider):
    name = "gemini"

    def __init__(self, api_key: str, model: str):
        if not api_key:
            raise AIError("Gemini API key is missing. Add it in Settings.")
        try:
            from google import genai
            from google.genai import types
        except ImportError as exc:
            raise AIError("Package 'google-genai' is not installed (pip install google-genai).") from exc
        self._types = types
        self._client = genai.Client(api_key=api_key)
        self.model = model

    def generate(self, system: str, user: str, temperature: float = 0.8) -> str:
        config = self._types.GenerateContentConfig(
            system_instruction=system, temperature=temperature,
            response_mime_type="application/json")
        try:
            response = self._client.models.generate_content(
                model=self.model, contents=user, config=config)
        except Exception as exc:  # SDK raises several exception types
            text = str(exc)
            if "429" in text or "RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED" in text or "quota" in text.lower():
                raise RateLimitError(text) from exc
            raise AIError(text) from exc
        return response.text or ""




================================================================================
FILE: .\ai\ollama.py
================================================================================
from __future__ import annotations

import requests

from promo_studio.ai.base import AIError, AIProvider


class OllamaProvider(AIProvider):
    name = "ollama"

    def __init__(self, model: str, base_url: str = "http://localhost:11434", timeout: int = 180):
        self.model, self.base_url, self.timeout = model, base_url.rstrip("/"), timeout

    def generate(self, system: str, user: str, temperature: float = 0.8) -> str:
        payload = {
            "model": self.model, "stream": False, "format": "json",
            "options": {"temperature": temperature},
            "messages": [{"role": "system", "content": system}, {"role": "user", "content": user}],
        }
        try:
            r = requests.post(f"{self.base_url}/api/chat", json=payload, timeout=self.timeout)
            r.raise_for_status()
            return r.json().get("message", {}).get("content", "")
        except requests.RequestException as exc:
            raise AIError(f"Ollama request failed: {exc}") from exc




================================================================================
FILE: .\ai\prompts.py
================================================================================
from __future__ import annotations

import json

ANGLES = {
    "feature_spotlight": "Spotlight one feature and the concrete benefit it gives the user.",
    "problem_solution": "Name a real user pain point, then show how the app helps.",
    "tip": "Give a genuinely useful tip related to the app's topic; mention the app lightly.",
    "behind_the_scenes": "Share a short, honest development story or decision.",
    "update": "Announce an update or release in plain language, using only the facts in the note.",
    "social_proof": "Use only real user feedback supplied in the note. Never invent quotes.",
}

SYSTEM_PROMPT = """You are a careful app-marketing copywriter for an independent developer.
Rules:
- Use only facts found in the app profile or the note. Never invent features, statistics, awards, reviews or prices.
- No medical, health-outcome or guaranteed-result claims.
- Write naturally and specifically. Avoid filler like "game-changer" and "revolutionary".
- Give each variant a genuinely different hook, not a reword of the same sentence.
- Hashtags: specific and relevant, without the # symbol.
- Return ONLY valid JSON matching the requested shape. No markdown fences, no commentary.

Platform guidance:
- x: under 250 characters before hashtags. One idea, one call to action.
- facebook: 300 to 500 characters, friendly, short paragraphs, include the store link if given.
- instagram: strong first line, caption under 600 characters, link-in-bio style call to action (links are not clickable).
- reddit: honest title under 100 characters, plain body, disclose that you are the developer, no hype, no hashtags.
"""

_SHAPES = {
    "x": '"x": {"text": "...", "hashtags": ["..."]}',
    "facebook": '"facebook": {"text": "...", "hashtags": ["..."]}',
    "instagram": '"instagram": {"caption": "...", "hashtags": ["..."]}',
    "reddit": '"reddit": {"title": "...", "body": "...", "suggested_subreddits": ["..."]}',
}


def build_user_prompt(profile, angle: str, base_text: str, note: str, n: int, platforms) -> str:
    facts = {
        "name": profile.name, "pitch": profile.pitch, "description": profile.description,
        "audience": profile.audience, "key_features": profile.features, "tone": profile.tone,
        "store_links": profile.store_links, "seed_keywords": profile.seed_keywords,
    }
    shape = ", ".join(_SHAPES[p] for p in platforms)
    example = '{"variants": [{"angle": "%s", %s}]}' % (angle, shape)
    parts = [
        "APP PROFILE:", json.dumps(facts, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),
        f"CONTENT ANGLE: {angle} - {ANGLES.get(angle, angle)}",
    ]
    if base_text.strip():
        parts += ["BASE IDEA FROM THE DEVELOPER (build on it):", base_text.strip()]
    if note.strip():
        parts += ["EXTRA NOTE:", note.strip()]
    parts += [
        f"Write {n} distinct variants. Use this exact JSON shape (repeat the variant object {n} times):",
        example,
    ]
    return "\n\n".join(parts)

FILE: .\assets\promo_studio\assets\brand\README.md

# Siddhanta brand mark

Place the approved Siddhanta brand mark at:

`promo_studio/assets/brand/siddhanta_mark.png`

The renderer will use it automatically. It intentionally does **not** draw a
fake Om, circle, or placeholder mark when the real asset is absent.




================================================================================
FILE: .\assets\promo_studio\assets\festivals\README.md
================================================================================
# Festival artwork

Only names listed in `promo_studio/festival_assets.py` may resolve to artwork.
Artwork must exist locally under this directory (or under the directory named
by `SIDDHANTA_FESTIVAL_ASSET_DIR`). Remote URLs and upstream `file_name` values
are never fetched or trusted by the renderer.

Use the allowlist to map each festival name to the **correct** local asset;
do not reuse a visually similar festival image merely because the upstream
record points at it.

================================================================================
FILE: .\assets\promo_studio\assets\fonts\README.md

# Siddhanta Promo Studio fonts

The package includes the Devanagari roles required for reliable Hindi shaping:

- `NotoSansDevanagari-Regular.ttf`
- `NotoSansDevanagari-Bold.ttf`
- `NotoSerifDevanagari-Regular.ttf`
- `NotoSerifDevanagari-Bold.ttf`

The bundled Noto Sans/Noto Serif family is the deterministic Latin fallback used by the release renderer.
If the final Siddhanta brand font is selected later, replace/bundle that licensed family and update the golden hashes.

**CI requirement:** Pillow must report `features.check("raqm") == True` so
HarfBuzz/FriBiDi/libraqm shaping is available. Without it, Devanagari can render
incorrectly even though the font itself is present.




================================================================================
FILE: .\assets\promo_studio\graphics\background.py
================================================================================
"""Procedural backgrounds (NumPy). Deterministic, offline, no AI generation.

Design rules that keep output byte-reproducible across machines:
  * only + - * / sqrt and polynomial falloffs (no sin/cos/exp: libm results can differ);
  * no random numbers: bokeh positions come from an R2 low-discrepancy sequence;
  * quantise once, at the end, with rint.
Backgrounds stay light so the maroon/ink text and white cards keep their contrast
(enforced by tests/test_studio.py).
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Sequence, Tuple
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
from ..theme import W, H, CREAM, GOLD_LIGHT


def _rgb(hex_color: str) -> np.ndarray:
    h = hex_color.lstrip("#")
    return np.array([int(h[i:i + 2], 16) for i in (0, 2, 4)], dtype=np.float64)


def _smooth(t: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    t = np.clip(t, 0.0, 1.0)
    return t * t * (3.0 - 2.0 * t)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Glow:
    cx: float; cy: float; radius: float; color: str; strength: float


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Theme:
    stops: Sequence[Tuple[float, str]] = ()          # vertical gradient: (0..1, colour)
    glows: Sequence[Glow] = ()
    bokeh: int = 0                                   # number of faint gold discs
    bokeh_strength: float = 0.10


THEMES = {
    "cream": Theme(),                                # flat brand cream (identical to v3)
    # Panchang: cream sky warming towards a low sun.
    "sunrise": Theme(
        stops=((0.0, CREAM), (0.55, CREAM), (1.0, "#F2DFBE")),
        glows=(Glow(540, 1350, 950, GOLD_LIGHT, 0.34),),
    ),
    # Muhurta: a soft high sun behind the header.
    "solar": Theme(
        stops=((0.0, "#F9F1DF"), (0.5, CREAM), (1.0, "#F3E7D0")),
        glows=(Glow(540, 70, 760, GOLD_LIGHT, 0.38),),
    ),
    # Festival: warm glow with a sparse scatter of bokeh.
    "festival_glow": Theme(
        stops=((0.0, CREAM), (1.0, "#F4E4C6")),
        glows=(Glow(540, 520, 820, GOLD_LIGHT, 0.26),),
        bokeh=36,
    ),
}


def _frac(x: float) -> float:
    return x - float(int(x))


class BackgroundEngine:
    @staticmethod
    def create(theme: str = "cream", width: int = W, height: int = H) -> Image.Image:
        try:
            spec = THEMES[theme]
        except KeyError:
            raise ValueError(f"unknown background theme {theme!r}; available: {sorted(THEMES)}") from None
        if not (spec.stops or spec.glows or spec.bokeh):
            return Image.new("RGB", (width, height), CREAM)

        ys = np.arange(height, dtype=np.float64)
        xs = np.arange(width, dtype=np.float64)
        if spec.stops:
            pos = np.array([p for p, _ in spec.stops], dtype=np.float64)
            cols = np.stack([_rgb(c) for _, c in spec.stops])
            t = ys / max(1, height - 1)
            column = np.stack([np.interp(t, pos, cols[:, k]) for k in range(3)], axis=1)   # (H, 3)
            img = np.repeat(column[:, None, :], width, axis=1)
        else:
            img = np.empty((height, width, 3), dtype=np.float64); img[:] = _rgb(CREAM)

        for g in spec.glows:
            d = np.sqrt((xs[None, :] - g.cx) ** 2 + (ys[:, None] - g.cy) ** 2) / g.radius
            a = g.strength * (1.0 - _smooth(d))
            img = img * (1.0 - a[..., None]) + _rgb(g.color) * a[..., None]

        for i in range(spec.bokeh):
            # R2 sequence: deterministic, evenly spread, no RNG.
            bx = _frac((i + 1) * 0.7548776662466927) * width
            by = _frac((i + 1) * 0.5698402909980532) * height
            br = 14.0 + 40.0 * _frac((i + 1) * 0.6180339887498949)
            x0, x1 = max(0, int(bx - br)), min(width, int(bx + br) + 1)
            y0, y1 = max(0, int(by - br)), min(height, int(by + br) + 1)
            if x0 >= x1 or y0 >= y1:
                continue
            d = np.sqrt((xs[None, x0:x1] - bx) ** 2 + (ys[y0:y1, None] - by) ** 2) / br
            a = spec.bokeh_strength * (1.0 - _smooth(d))
            img[y0:y1, x0:x1] = img[y0:y1, x0:x1] * (1.0 - a[..., None]) + _rgb(GOLD_LIGHT) * a[..., None]

        return Image.fromarray(np.rint(np.clip(img, 0, 255)).astype(np.uint8), "RGB")

"""Reusable Pillow components for Siddhanta promotional posts."""
from __future__ import annotations
import logging, math, warnings
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional, Sequence
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
from ..theme import *
from .background import BackgroundEngine
from .ornaments import paste_ornament

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
MAX_ASSET_PIXELS = 40_000_000
MAX_ASSET_BYTES = 15 * 1024 * 1024


def canvas(theme: str = "cream"):
    """Blank post canvas. ``theme`` selects a procedural background (see background.py)."""
    im = BackgroundEngine.create(theme, W, H)
    return im, ImageDraw.Draw(im)


def fit_size(text, max_w, size, min_size, make_font):
    while size > min_size and make_font(size).getlength(str(text)) > max_w:
        size -= 1
    return max(min_size, size)


def _ellipsize(text, font, max_w):
    text = str(text)
    if font.getlength(text) <= max_w:
        return text
    ellipsis = "…"
    if font.getlength(ellipsis) > max_w:
        return ""
    out = []
    for ch in text:
        candidate = "".join(out) + ch + ellipsis
        if font.getlength(candidate) > max_w:
            break
        out.append(ch)
    return "".join(out).rstrip() + ellipsis


def wrap(text, font, max_w, max_lines):
    lines, cur = [], ""
    for word in str(text).split():
        trial = f"{cur} {word}".strip()
        if font.getlength(trial) <= max_w or not cur:
            cur = trial
        else:
            lines.append(cur); cur = word
    if cur or not lines: lines.append(cur)
    if len(lines) > max_lines:
        lines = lines[:max_lines]
        lines[-1] = _ellipsize(lines[-1], font, max_w)
    return lines


def text_fit(d, xy, s, max_w, size, min_size=TYPE["minimum"], fill=INK, bold=False, anchor=None, face="sans"):
    mk = (lambda z: serif(z)) if face == "serif" else (lambda z: sans(z, bold))
    final_size = fit_size(str(s), max_w, size, min_size, mk)
    actual = mk(final_size)
    text = _ellipsize(str(s), actual, max_w)
    d.text(xy, text, font=actual, fill=fill, anchor=anchor)


def centered_wrapped(d, y, s, size=TYPE["title"], fill=MAROON, max_lines=2, face="serif", min_size=TYPE["minimum"]):
    mk = (lambda z: serif(z)) if face == "serif" else (lambda z: sans(z, True))
    size = fit_size(str(s), (W - 2 * M), size, min_size, mk)
    f = mk(size); lines = wrap(str(s), f, W - 2 * M, max_lines)
    for i, ln in enumerate(lines):
        d.text((W / 2, y + i * int(size * 1.15)), ln, font=f, fill=fill, anchor="ma")
    return y + len(lines) * int(size * 1.15)


def _brand_mark_path() -> Optional[Path]:
    p = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "assets" / "brand" / "siddhanta_mark.png"
    return p if p.is_file() else None


def header(d, title, im=None, hindi_title=None):
    mark = _brand_mark_path(); x = M
    if mark and im is not None:
        logo = Image.open(mark).convert("RGBA")
        logo.thumbnail((40, 40), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)
        im.paste(logo, (x, 54), logo); x += 54
    d.text((x, 58), "SIDDHANTA", font=sans(26, True), fill=MAROON)
    title_end = centered_wrapped(d, 118, title, TYPE["title"], MAROON, max_lines=2, min_size=TYPE["body"])
    if hindi_title:
        d.text((W / 2, title_end + 18), hindi_title, font=devanagari(TYPE["hindi_subtitle"], True), fill=MAROON, anchor="ma")


def footer(d, cta="Explore Siddhanta"):
    d.line((M, H - 112, W - M, H - 112), fill=CREAM_DARK, width=2)
    text_fit(d, (M, H - 78), cta, 640, TYPE["footer"], TYPE["minimum"], MAROON, True)
    d.text((W - M, H - 78), "Offline Panchang", font=sans(TYPE["footer"]), fill=MUTED_DEEP, anchor="ra")


def card(d, box, title, value, subtitle=None, accent=MAROON):
    x1, y1, x2, y2 = box; inner = x2 - x1 - 50
    d.rounded_rectangle(box, 22, fill=WHITE, outline=CREAM_DARK, width=2)
    d.text((x1 + 25, y1 + 20), title.upper(), font=sans(TYPE["label"], True), fill=MUTED)
    text_fit(d, (x1 + 25, y1 + 62), value, inner, TYPE["hero"], TYPE["minimum"], accent, True)
    if subtitle: text_fit(d, (x1 + 25, y2 - 34), subtitle, inner, TYPE["body"], TYPE["minimum"], MUTED)


def metric_card(d, box, label, value, detail=None, accent=MAROON):
    x1, y1, x2, y2 = box
    d.rounded_rectangle(box, 24, fill=WHITE, outline=CREAM_DARK, width=2)
    d.text((x1 + 26, y1 + 20), label.upper(), font=sans(TYPE["label"], True), fill=MUTED)
    text_fit(d, (x1 + 26, y1 + 60), value, x2 - x1 - 52, TYPE["hero"], TYPE["minimum"], accent, True)
    if detail: text_fit(d, (x1 + 26, y2 - 34), detail, x2 - x1 - 52, TYPE["body"], TYPE["minimum"], MUTED)


def mandala(d, cx, cy, r=150, spokes=12, color=GOLD_LIGHT):
    for rr in (r, r * .78, r * .56, r * .34): d.ellipse((cx - rr, cy - rr, cx + rr, cy + rr), outline=color, width=2)
    for i in range(spokes):
        a = math.tau * i / spokes; d.line((cx, cy, cx + math.cos(a) * r, cy + math.sin(a) * r), fill=color, width=2)


def _minutes(value: str) -> int:
    import datetime as _dt
    parsed = _dt.datetime.strptime(value.strip().upper(), "%I:%M %p")
    return parsed.hour * 60 + parsed.minute


# Timeline geometry. Everything the card needs vertically is derived from the number of
# lanes, so the card can never be shorter than its content (v3 drew the sunrise/sunset
# row outside the card when two lanes were needed).
_TL_TITLE_H = 64      # card title row
_TL_LABEL_H = 30      # label row sitting directly above each lane's bar
_TL_BAR_GAP = 2       # gap between a lane's label row and its bar
_TL_BAR_H = 24
_TL_LANE_GAP = 18     # empty space between one lane's bar and the next lane's label
_TL_TIMES_H = 52      # sunrise / sunset row (gap + 28 px text)
_TL_BOTTOM_PAD = 14
_TL_MIN_H = 188
_TL_SWATCH = 14


def _timeline_height_for(lane_count: int) -> int:
    n = max(1, lane_count)
    lane_block = n * (_TL_LABEL_H + _TL_BAR_GAP + _TL_BAR_H) + (n - 1) * _TL_LANE_GAP
    return max(_TL_MIN_H, _TL_TITLE_H + lane_block + _TL_TIMES_H + _TL_BOTTOM_PAD)


def _plan_timeline(sunrise, sunset, bands, bar_x1, bar_x2):
    """Place bands on the daylight axis and group them into lanes.

    Two bands share a lane only if neither their bars nor their labels collide.
    Returns a list of lanes; each lane is a list of dicts with pixel geometry.
    """
    start_m, end_m = _minutes(sunrise), _minutes(sunset)
    if end_m <= start_m: end_m += 24 * 60
    usable = bar_x2 - bar_x1
    label_font = sans(TYPE["label"], True)

    prepared = []
    for label, s, e, fill in bands:
        sm, em = _minutes(s), _minutes(e)
        if sm < start_m: sm += 24 * 60
        if em < start_m: em += 24 * 60
        if em <= sm or sm > end_m or em < start_m:
            continue
        left = bar_x1 + usable * max(0, min(1, (sm - start_m) / (end_m - start_m)))
        right = bar_x1 + usable * max(0, min(1, (em - start_m) / (end_m - start_m)))
        if right <= left: continue
        label_w = _TL_SWATCH + 8 + label_font.getlength(str(label))
        lx = min(max((left + right) / 2 - label_w / 2, bar_x1), bar_x2 - label_w)
        prepared.append({"label": label, "start": sm, "end": em, "left": left, "right": right,
                         "fill": fill, "label_x": lx, "label_x2": lx + label_w})

    def collides(a, b):
        bars = a["left"] < b["right"] - 0.5 and a["right"] > b["left"] + 0.5
        labels = a["label_x"] < b["label_x2"] + 14 and a["label_x2"] + 14 > b["label_x"]
        return bars or labels

    lanes: list[list[dict]] = []
    for item in sorted(prepared, key=lambda z: (z["start"], z["end"])):
        for lane in lanes:
            if not any(collides(item, other) for other in lane):
                lane.append(item); break
        else:
            lanes.append([item])
    return lanes


def timeline_height(sunrise, sunset, bands: Sequence[tuple[str, str, str, str]], width: int = W - 2 * M) -> int:
    """Height the timeline card needs for these bands (1 lane, or 2+ when windows overlap)."""
    return _timeline_height_for(len(_plan_timeline(sunrise, sunset, bands, 28, width - 28)))


def timeline_bar(d, box, sunrise, sunset, bands: Sequence[tuple[str, str, str, str]], title="Daylight"):
    """Draw the daylight timeline card and return its bottom y.

    Overlapping windows (e.g. Abhijit inside Rahu Kaal) get separate lanes. Every lane
    has its own label row directly above its bar, drawn in dark text with a colour
    swatch, so each bar is unambiguously named. The card grows to fit its lanes; use
    ``timeline_height`` to reserve the right amount of space before calling.
    """
    x1, y1, x2, y2 = box
    bar_x1, bar_x2 = x1 + 28, x2 - 28
    lanes = _plan_timeline(sunrise, sunset, bands, bar_x1, bar_x2)
    y2 = y1 + max(y2 - y1, _timeline_height_for(len(lanes)))
    d.rounded_rectangle((x1, y1, x2, y2), 22, fill=WHITE, outline=CREAM_DARK, width=2)
    d.text((x1 + 24, y1 + 20), title.upper(), font=sans(TYPE["label"], True), fill=MUTED)

    label_font = sans(TYPE["label"], True)
    lane_pitch = _TL_LABEL_H + _TL_BAR_GAP + _TL_BAR_H + _TL_LANE_GAP
    top = y1 + _TL_TITLE_H
    for i, lane in enumerate(lanes or [[]]):
        row_y = top + i * lane_pitch
        bar_y = row_y + _TL_LABEL_H + _TL_BAR_GAP
        d.rounded_rectangle((bar_x1, bar_y, bar_x2, bar_y + _TL_BAR_H), 12, fill=CREAM_DARK)
        for item in lane:
            d.rounded_rectangle((item["left"], bar_y, item["right"], bar_y + _TL_BAR_H), 12, fill=item["fill"])
            cy = row_y + _TL_LABEL_H / 2
            sw = item["label_x"]
            d.rounded_rectangle((sw, cy - _TL_SWATCH / 2, sw + _TL_SWATCH, cy + _TL_SWATCH / 2), 4, fill=item["fill"])
            d.text((sw + _TL_SWATCH + 8, cy), str(item["label"]), font=label_font, fill=MAROON, anchor="lm")

    n = max(1, len(lanes))
    times_y = top + n * (_TL_LABEL_H + _TL_BAR_GAP + _TL_BAR_H) + (n - 1) * _TL_LANE_GAP + 16
    d.text((bar_x1, times_y), sunrise, font=sans(TYPE["body"], True), fill=MUTED)
    d.text((bar_x2, times_y), sunset, font=sans(TYPE["body"], True), fill=MUTED, anchor="ra")
    return y2


def choghadiya_list(d, box, slots: Sequence[dict], title="Auspicious Choghadiya") -> bool:
    """Draw the Choghadiya card. Returns False (drawing nothing) when there is no slot to list.

    An empty card would read as a statement about the day, so the caller decides what
    to show instead; see ``posts/muhurta.py``.
    """
    good = list(slots)[:4]
    if not good:
        return False
    x1, y1, x2, y2 = box
    d.rounded_rectangle(box, 24, fill=WHITE, outline=CREAM_DARK, width=2)
    d.text((x1 + 24, y1 + 22), title.upper(), font=sans(TYPE["label"], True), fill=MUTED)
    top = y1 + 78
    row_h = (y2 - top - 22) / max(1, len(good))
    for i, slot in enumerate(good):
        ry = top + i * row_h
        d.rounded_rectangle((x1 + 24, ry, x2 - 24, ry + row_h - 12), 18, fill=GOLD_LIGHT, outline=CREAM_DARK, width=1)
        name = str(slot.get("name_en", "")); times = f"{slot.get('start','')} – {slot.get('end','')}"
        text_fit(d, (x1 + 48, ry + (row_h - 12) / 2), name, 300, TYPE["body"], TYPE["minimum"], MAROON, True, "lm")
        text_fit(d, (x2 - 48, ry + (row_h - 12) / 2), times, 350, TYPE["body"], TYPE["minimum"], INK, True, "rm")
    return True


def _safe_open_image(path: Path):
    try:
        size_bytes = path.stat().st_size
        if size_bytes > MAX_ASSET_BYTES:
            raise ValueError(f"asset exceeds {MAX_ASSET_BYTES // (1024*1024)} MB")
        with warnings.catch_warnings():
            warnings.simplefilter("error", Image.DecompressionBombWarning)
            with Image.open(path) as probe:
                width, height = probe.size
                if width * height > MAX_ASSET_PIXELS:
                    raise ValueError(f"asset exceeds {MAX_ASSET_PIXELS:,} pixels")
                # Decode only after both limits have passed.
                return probe.convert("RGB")
    except Exception as exc:
        log.warning("Festival asset rejected (%s): %s", path.name, exc)
        return None


def festival_hero(im, d, box, title, image_path=None, max_title_lines=3):
    x1, y1, x2, y2 = box
    d.rounded_rectangle(box, 28, fill=NAVY, outline=CREAM_DARK, width=2)
    if image_path and Path(image_path).is_file():
        src = _safe_open_image(Path(image_path))
        if src is not None:
            ratio = max((x2-x1)/src.width, (y2-y1)/src.height)
            src = src.resize((max(1, int(src.width*ratio)), max(1, int(src.height*ratio))), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)
            left = (src.width-(x2-x1))//2; top = (src.height-(y2-y1))//2
            crop = src.crop((left, top, left+x2-x1, top+y2-y1)).convert("RGBA")
            # Strong lower scrim protects white title text over bright deity artwork.
            scrim = Image.new("RGBA", crop.size, (0, 0, 0, 0)); sd = ImageDraw.Draw(scrim)
            for yy in range(crop.height):
                t = yy / max(1, crop.height - 1)
                alpha = int(34 + 175 * max(0, (t - .35) / .65))
                sd.line((0, yy, crop.width, yy), fill=(16, 35, 63, alpha))
            crop = Image.alpha_composite(crop, scrim).convert("RGB")
            mask = Image.new("L", crop.size, 0); md = ImageDraw.Draw(mask)
            md.rounded_rectangle((0, 0, crop.width - 1, crop.height - 1), 28, fill=255)
            im.paste(crop, (x1, y1), mask)
        else:
            paste_ornament(im, "mandala_full", ((x1+x2)//2, y1 + int((y2-y1)*.34)), 300)
    else:
        # Quiet fallback: keep the mandala high and compact so the title has its own zone.
        paste_ornament(im, "mandala_full", ((x1+x2)//2, y1 + int((y2-y1)*.34)), 300)
    title_y = y2 - 155 if image_path and Path(image_path).is_file() else y2 - 105
    centered_wrapped(d, title_y, title, 52, WHITE, max_lines=max_title_lines, min_size=TYPE["body"])




================================================================================
FILE: .\assets\promo_studio\graphics\ornaments.py
================================================================================
"""Pre-rendered vector ornaments.

SVG sources live in assets/ornaments/src/ and are rasterised ONCE, offline, by
tools/build_ornaments.py (CairoSVG, a dev-only dependency). The resulting PNGs are
committed, so production needs only Pillow: no libcairo, no runtime SVG parsing.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import re
from functools import lru_cache
from pathlib import Path
from PIL import Image

_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "assets" / "ornaments"
_NAME = re.compile(r"^[a-z0-9_]+$")


class OrnamentError(RuntimeError):
    pass


@lru_cache(maxsize=32)
def _load(name: str) -> Image.Image:
    if not _NAME.match(name):
        raise OrnamentError(f"invalid ornament name {name!r}")
    path = _DIR / f"{name}.png"
    if not path.is_file():
        raise OrnamentError(f"ornament {name!r} not found; run tools/build_ornaments.py")
    with Image.open(path) as im:
        return im.convert("RGBA")


@lru_cache(maxsize=64)
def ornament(name: str, size: int) -> Image.Image:
    src = _load(name)
    return src.resize((int(size), max(1, round(src.height * size / src.width))), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)


def paste_ornament(im: Image.Image, name: str, center, size: int, opacity: float = 1.0) -> None:
    art = ornament(name, size)
    if opacity < 1.0:
        art = art.copy(); art.putalpha(art.getchannel("A").point(lambda v: int(v * opacity)))
    im.paste(art, (int(center[0] - art.width / 2), int(center[1] - art.height / 2)), art)

from ..draw import *
from ..festival_assets import resolve as festival_asset
from ..spec import PostSpec
from ..models import post_warnings
from . import register

def build(day):
    if not day.festivals_today:
        return None
    names = [x.name_en for x in day.festivals_today]
    f = {"date": day.date.isoformat(), "festival": names[0],
         "tithi": f"{day.paksha.title()} {day.tithi}", "nakshatra": day.nakshatra}
    if day.location: f["location"] = day.location
    if day.location_display: f["location_display"] = day.location_display
    if len(names) > 1: f["also_today"] = " • ".join(names[1:])
    return PostSpec("festival", f["date"], f, warnings=post_warnings(day))

@register("festival", build)
def render(s):
    f = s.facts; im, d = canvas("festival_glow"); header(d, "Festival Today", im, "आज का पर्व")
    hero_path = festival_asset(f["festival"])
    festival_hero(im, d, (M, 245, W - M, 760), f["festival"], hero_path)
    y = 790
    if f.get("location_display"):
        d.text((W / 2, y), f"Times for {f['location_display']}", font=sans(TYPE["label"], True), fill=MAROON, anchor="ma"); y += 48
    metric_card(d, (M, y, 510, y + 160), "Tithi", f["tithi"])
    metric_card(d, (570, y, W - M, y + 160), "Nakshatra", f["nakshatra"])
    if "also_today" in f:
        centered_wrapped(d, y + 185, "Also today: " + f["also_today"], TYPE["body"], INK, max_lines=2, face="sans", min_size=TYPE["minimum"])
    footer(d, "See the festival calendar in Siddhanta"); return im




================================================================================
FILE: .\assets\promo_studio\posts\muhurta.py
================================================================================
from ..draw import *
from ..spec import PostSpec
from ..models import post_warnings
from . import register

def _minutes(value: str) -> int:
    from datetime import datetime
    parsed = datetime.strptime(value.strip().upper(), "%I:%M %p")
    return parsed.hour * 60 + parsed.minute


def _overlaps(start: str, end: str, other_start: str, other_end: str) -> bool:
    a, b = _minutes(start), _minutes(end)
    c, d = _minutes(other_start), _minutes(other_end)
    if b <= a: b += 1440
    if d <= c: d += 1440
    return max(a, c) < min(b, d)


# Presentation-only policy (documentation, NOT a caption fact). Never claim that an
# auspicious Choghadiya window overrides Rahu Kaal: featured slots that overlap Rahu
# are omitted from the card. This sentence must never be passed to the caption writer.
CHOGHADIYA_POLICY = (
    "Auspicious Choghadiya windows overlapping Rahu Kaal are omitted from the featured list; "
    "this is a presentation filter, not a Jyotish judgement."
)


def build(day):
    if not (day.abhijit or day.rahu_kaal or day.choghadiya):
        return None
    f = {"weekday": day.weekday, "date": day.date.isoformat(),
         "tithi": f"{day.paksha.title()} {day.tithi}", "nakshatra": day.nakshatra}
    if day.location: f["location"] = day.location
    if day.location_display: f["location_display"] = day.location_display
    if day.abhijit: f["abhijit"] = f"{day.abhijit.start} – {day.abhijit.end}"
    if day.rahu_kaal: f["rahu"] = f"{day.rahu_kaal.start} – {day.rahu_kaal.end}"

    if day.choghadiya and isinstance(day.choghadiya.get("day"), list):
        candidates = [dict(x) for x in day.choghadiya["day"]
                      if isinstance(x, dict) and x.get("is_auspicious")]
        excluded = []
        if day.rahu_kaal:
            kept = []
            for slot in candidates:
                if slot.get("start") and slot.get("end") and _overlaps(
                    str(slot["start"]), str(slot["end"]), day.rahu_kaal.start, day.rahu_kaal.end
                ):
                    excluded.append({"name_en": slot.get("name_en", ""), "start": slot.get("start", ""), "end": slot.get("end", "")})
                else:
                    kept.append(slot)
            candidates = kept
        f["choghadiya_day"] = candidates[:4]
        if excluded:
            f["choghadiya_excluded_overlapping_rahu"] = excluded

    if not ("abhijit" in f or "rahu" in f or f.get("choghadiya_day")):
        return None          # nothing Muhurta-specific left to show: selector falls back to Panchang
    return PostSpec("muhurta", f["date"], f, warnings=post_warnings(day))

@register("muhurta", build)
def render(s):
    f = s.facts; im, d = canvas("solar"); header(d, "Plan Your Day Wisely", im, "आज का शुभ समय")
    d.text((W / 2, 252), f"{f['weekday']} • {f['date']}", font=sans(TYPE["body"], True), fill=MUTED, anchor="ma")
    if f.get("location_display"):
        d.text((W / 2, 292), f"Times for {f['location_display']}", font=sans(TYPE["label"], True), fill=MAROON, anchor="ma")
    # The Choghadiya card is drawn only when at least one slot survives the Rahu filter.
    # If every auspicious slot overlapped Rahu Kaal there is nothing honest to feature,
    # so the card is omitted and the remaining cards use the freed space.
    slots = list(f.get("choghadiya_day", ()))
    has_chog = bool(slots)
    card_h, pitch = (155, 174) if has_chog else (190, 220)
    y = 325
    if "abhijit" in f:
        metric_card(d, (M, y, W - M, y + card_h), "Auspicious Window · Abhijit", f["abhijit"], accent=GOLD); y += pitch
    if "rahu" in f:
        metric_card(d, (M, y, W - M, y + card_h), "Caution Window · Rahu Kaal", f["rahu"], accent=MAROON); y += pitch
    if has_chog:
        choghadiya_list(d, (M, y, W - M, min(y + 390, 1125)), slots)
        y += 410
    metric_card(d, (M, y, 510, y + (145 if has_chog else card_h)), "Tithi", f["tithi"])
    metric_card(d, (570, y, W - M, y + (145 if has_chog else card_h)), "Nakshatra", f["nakshatra"])
    footer(d, "Use the calculated windows in Siddhanta"); return im

FILE: .\assets\promo_studio\posts\panchang.py

from ..draw import *
from ..spec import PostSpec
from ..models import post_warnings
from . import register

def build(day):
    f = {"weekday": day.weekday, "date": day.date.isoformat(),
         "tithi": f"{day.paksha.title()} {day.tithi}", "nakshatra": day.nakshatra}
    if day.location: f["location"] = day.location
    if day.location_display: f["location_display"] = day.location_display
    for k, v in (("yoga", day.yoga), ("karana", day.karana), ("masa", day.masa),
                 ("sunrise", day.sunrise), ("sunset", day.sunset)):
        if v: f[k] = v
    if day.abhijit: f["abhijit"] = f"{day.abhijit.start} – {day.abhijit.end}"
    if day.rahu_kaal: f["rahu"] = f"{day.rahu_kaal.start} – {day.rahu_kaal.end}"
    return PostSpec("panchang", f["date"], f, warnings=post_warnings(day))

@register("panchang", build)
def render(s):
    f = s.facts; im, d = canvas("sunrise"); header(d, "Today's Panchang", im, "दैनिक पंचांग")
    d.text((W / 2, 252), f"{f['weekday']} • {f['date']}", font=sans(TYPE["body"], True), fill=MUTED, anchor="ma")
    if f.get("location_display"):
        d.text((W / 2, 292), f"Times for {f['location_display']}", font=sans(TYPE["label"], True), fill=MAROON, anchor="ma")
    grid = [("Tithi", "tithi"), ("Nakshatra", "nakshatra"), ("Yoga", "yoga"), ("Karana", "karana"),
            ("Sunrise", "sunrise"), ("Sunset", "sunset")]
    items = [(t, f[k]) for t, k in grid if k in f]
    y = 330
    for i in range(0, len(items), 2):
        row = items[i:i + 2]
        xs = [(M, 510), (570, W - M)] if len(row) == 2 else [(M, W - M)]
        for (t, v), (x1, x2) in zip(row, xs): metric_card(d, (x1, y, x2, y + 150), t, v)
        y += 176
    if "masa" in f:
        d.text((W / 2, y + 4), f"Masa • {f['masa']} · Purnimanta", font=sans(TYPE["label"], True), fill=MAROON, anchor="ma")
        y += 50
    if "sunrise" in f and "sunset" in f and ("abhijit" in f or "rahu" in f):
        bands = []
        if "abhijit" in f:
            a, b = [x.strip() for x in f["abhijit"].split("–", 1)]
            bands.append(("Abhijit", a, b, GOLD))
        if "rahu" in f:
            a, b = [x.strip() for x in f["rahu"].split("–", 1)]
            bands.append(("Rahu Kaal", a, b, MAROON))
        top = y + 8
        h = timeline_height(f["sunrise"], f["sunset"], bands)
        timeline_bar(d, (M, top, W - M, top + h), f["sunrise"], f["sunset"], bands)
    footer(d); return im

"""Static, person-agnostic feature showcases. Always labelled illustrative."""
import math
from ..draw import *
from ..spec import PostSpec
from ..models import post_warnings
from . import register

def _spec(kind): return lambda day: PostSpec(kind, day.date.isoformat(), {}, illustrative=True, warnings=post_warnings(day))

@register("kundli", _spec("kundli"))
def kundli(s):
    im, d = canvas(); header(d, "Your Kundli"); cx, cy, r = W // 2, 620, 250
    d.ellipse((cx - r, cy - r, cx + r, cy + r), outline=MAROON, width=4)
    for i in range(12):
        a = math.radians(i * 30); d.line((cx, cy, cx + math.cos(a) * r, cy + math.sin(a) * r), fill=CREAM_DARK, width=3)
    d.ellipse((cx - 80, cy - 80, cx + 80, cy + 80), fill=CREAM_DARK, outline=GOLD, width=3)
    d.text((W / 2, 930), "D1 • D9 • Dashas • Yogas", font=sans(30, True), fill=MAROON, anchor="ma")
    d.text((W / 2, 985), "Illustrative feature preview", font=sans(22), fill=MUTED, anchor="ma")
    footer(d, "Explore Kundli in Siddhanta"); return im

@register("chakra", _spec("chakra"))
def chakra(s):
    im, d = canvas(); header(d, "Balance Your Inner Energy")
    names = ["Sahasrara", "Ajna", "Vishuddha", "Anahata", "Manipura", "Svadhisthana", "Muladhara"]
    radii = [46, 42, 38, 34, 30, 26, 22]               # uniform-ish radii: no overlap, labels beside circles
    top, step, cx = 280, 120, W // 2 - 120
    for i, (n, r) in enumerate(zip(names, radii)):
        y = top + i * step
        d.ellipse((cx - r, y - r, cx + r, y + r), outline=GOLD, width=4)
        d.text((cx + 90, y), n, font=sans(28, True), fill=MAROON if i % 2 == 0 else MUTED, anchor="lm")
    d.text((W / 2, 1090), "Illustrative feature preview", font=sans(22), fill=MUTED, anchor="ma")
    footer(d, "Explore the seven chakras in Siddhanta"); return im




================================================================================
FILE: .\assets\promo_studio\posts\transit.py
================================================================================
from datetime import date, datetime
from ..draw import *
from ..spec import PostSpec
from ..models import post_warnings
from . import register


def _iso(value):
    try:
        return date.fromisoformat(str(value))
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None


def _featured_major(major, today):
    candidates = []
    for key, item in major.items():
        if not isinstance(item, dict) or not item.get("current_rashi_en"):
            continue
        ingress = _iso(item.get("ingress_date")); egress = _iso(item.get("egress_date"))
        if ingress == today or egress == today:
            return key, item
        latest_ingress = ingress if ingress and ingress <= today else None
        candidates.append((latest_ingress or date.min, key, item))
    if not candidates:
        return None
    # Most recently entered current sign first; stable key breaks ties. This removes
    # the old hard-coded Jupiter-first behaviour while remaining deterministic.
    candidates.sort(key=lambda x: (x[0], x[1]), reverse=True)
    return candidates[0][1], candidates[0][2]


def build(day):
    if not day.raw_transit or not isinstance(day.raw_transit.get("major_transits"), dict):
        return None
    chosen = _featured_major(day.raw_transit["major_transits"], day.date)
    if not chosen:
        return None
    key, item = chosen
    planet = str(item.get("planet_en") or key.title())
    facts = {
        "date": day.date.isoformat(),
        "planet": planet,
        "rashi": str(item["current_rashi_en"]),
    }
    if day.location:
        facts["location"] = day.location
    if day.location_display:
        facts["location_display"] = day.location_display
    # Node motion is already normalized by the Siddhanta adapter. Do not expose the
    # upstream Direct value. For other planets, preserve the authoritative motion.
    if key in ("rahu", "ketu"):
        facts["motion"] = "Retrograde"
    elif item.get("motion_en"):
        facts["motion"] = str(item["motion_en"])
    if item.get("ingress_date"):
        facts["most_recent_ingress"] = str(item["ingress_date"])
    # Egress is intentionally omitted because retrograde re-entry can make a single
    # period card misleading.
    return PostSpec("transit", facts["date"], facts, warnings=post_warnings(day, include_node_motion=True))


@register("transit", build)
def render(s):
    f = s.facts
    im, d = canvas(); header(d, "Jyotish Insight", im, "ग्रह गोचर")
    d.text((W / 2, 252), f["date"], font=sans(TYPE["body"], True), fill=MUTED, anchor="ma")
    if f.get("location_display"):
        d.text((W / 2, 292), f"Sky reference: {f['location_display']}", font=sans(TYPE["label"], True), fill=MAROON, anchor="ma")
    centered_wrapped(d, 335, f"{f['planet']} Transit", 58, MAROON, max_lines=2)
    metric_card(d, (M, 535, W - M, 705), "Current Rashi", f["rashi"])
    if "motion" in f:
        metric_card(d, (M, 735, W - M, 880), "Motion", f["motion"])
    if f.get("most_recent_ingress"):
        try:
            ingress_label = datetime.strptime(f["most_recent_ingress"], "%Y-%m-%d").strftime("%d %b %Y").lstrip("0")
        except ValueError:
            ingress_label = f["most_recent_ingress"]
        metric_card(d, (M, 910, W - M, 1055), "Most Recent Ingress", ingress_label)
    footer(d, "Explore Jyotish in Siddhanta")
    return im

"""Post registry. Add a post type = add one module with @register. Nothing else changes."""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Callable, Dict, Optional
from ..models import Day
from ..spec import PostSpec

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PostType:
    kind: str
    build: Callable[[Day], Optional[PostSpec]]   # None => prerequisites unmet, selector falls back
    render: Callable[[PostSpec], "Image.Image"]

REGISTRY: Dict[str, PostType] = {}

def register(kind, build):
    def deco(render):
        REGISTRY[kind] = PostType(kind, build, render); return render
    return deco

from . import panchang, muhurta, festival, transit, showcases  # noqa: E402,F401  (populate registry)




================================================================================
FILE: .\assets\promo_studio\adapter.py
================================================================================
"""Adapter from the real Siddhanta workflow schema to Promo Studio's internal schema.

Only this module knows the external Siddhanta JSON layout. Renderers and selectors
consume the validated internal model and never inspect the upstream JSON directly.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from copy import deepcopy
from typing import Any, Mapping, Optional
from .models import DataError


def adapt_siddhanta(daily_panchang: Mapping[str, Any], muhurta_data: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None) -> dict:
    if not isinstance(daily_panchang, Mapping):
        raise TypeError("daily_panchang must be an object")
    today = daily_panchang.get("today")
    if not isinstance(today, Mapping):
        raise ValueError("daily_panchang.today is required")

    en = today.get("english")
    if not isinstance(en, Mapping):
        raise DataError("not a Siddhanta payload: today.english is required")

    # The English block is the canonical Promo Studio language binding.
    metadata = daily_panchang.get("metadata")
    if not isinstance(metadata, Mapping):
        raise DataError("not a Siddhanta payload: metadata is required")
    location = metadata.get("location")
    if not isinstance(location, Mapping) or not isinstance(location.get("name"), str) or not location.get("name").strip():
        raise DataError("Siddhanta metadata.location.name is required")

    binding_warnings: list[str] = []
    normalised_nodes: set[str] = set()
    out_today = {
        "date": today.get("date"),
        "day_of_week": today.get("day_of_week_en"),
        "location": location.get("name").strip(),
        "location_display": (location.get("display_name") or location.get("name").strip().split(",", 1)[0].split(" (", 1)[0]).strip(),
        "tithi": en.get("tithi_name"),
        "paksha": en.get("paksha"),
        "nakshatra": en.get("nakshatra"),
        "yoga": en.get("yoga"),
        "karana": en.get("karana"),
        "masa_purnimanta": en.get("masa"),
        "sunrise": en.get("sunrise"),
        "sunset": en.get("sunset"),
        # Explicitly authoritative: [] means no festival today.
        "festivals_today": deepcopy(today.get("festivals_today", [])),
        # Kept for the upcoming-festival adapter/tests, never used as today's festival
        # when festivals_today is explicitly present.
        "upcoming_festivals": deepcopy(daily_panchang.get("upcoming_festivals", [])),
    }

    # Prefer the dedicated muhurta file. Its structure is authoritative for windows.
    if isinstance(muhurta_data, Mapping):
        mu_date = muhurta_data.get("date")
        day_date = today.get("date")
        if mu_date != day_date:
            raise DataError(f"muhurta_data.date {mu_date!r} does not match today.date {day_date!r}")
        mh = muhurta_data.get("muhurtas", {})
        ch = muhurta_data.get("choghadiya", {})
    else:
        # Backward-compatible fallback to the duplicated values inside daily_panchang.
        hi = today.get("hindi", {}) if isinstance(today.get("hindi"), Mapping) else {}
        sh = hi.get("shubh_muhurta", {}) if isinstance(hi.get("shubh_muhurta"), Mapping) else {}
        ah = hi.get("ashubh_muhurta", {}) if isinstance(hi.get("ashubh_muhurta"), Mapping) else {}
        mh = {
            "abhijit_muhurta": _window_from_string(sh.get("abhijit")),
            "brahma_muhurta": _window_from_string(sh.get("brahma_muhurta")),
            "godhuli_muhurta": _window_from_string(sh.get("godhuli_muhurta")),
            "rahu_kaal": _window_from_string(ah.get("rahu_kaal")),
            "gulika_kaal": _window_from_string(ah.get("gulika_kaal")),
            "yamaghanta": _window_from_string(ah.get("yamaghanta")),
        }
        ch = today.get("choghadiya", {})

    for source_key, target_key in (
        ("abhijit_muhurta", "abhijit_muhurta"),
        ("brahma_muhurta", "brahma_muhurta"),
        ("godhuli_muhurta", "godhuli_muhurta"),
        ("rahu_kaal", "rahu_kaal"),
        ("gulika_kaal", "gulika_kaal"),
        ("yamaghanta", "yamaghanta"),
    ):
        if isinstance(mh, Mapping) and mh.get(source_key) is not None:
            out_today[target_key] = _normalise_window(mh[source_key])

    if isinstance(ch, Mapping):
        out_today["choghadiya"] = deepcopy(ch)

    # Transits live at the top level in the real workflow. Normalize node motion
    # here so no renderer/caption can accidentally publish the upstream Direct flag.
    transits = deepcopy(daily_panchang.get("transits", {}))
    if isinstance(transits, Mapping):
        for section in ("positions", "major_transits"):
            group = transits.get(section)
            if isinstance(group, Mapping):
                for node in ("rahu", "ketu"):
                    item = group.get(node)
                    if isinstance(item, Mapping):
                        item["is_retrograde"] = True
                        item["motion_en"] = "Retrograde"
                        item["motion_hi"] = "वक्री"
                        normalised_nodes.add(node)
    binding_warnings.extend(f"{node} motion normalised to Retrograde" for node in sorted(normalised_nodes))
    out_today["warnings"] = binding_warnings
    return {"today": out_today, "transits": transits}


def _window_from_string(value: Any):
    if not isinstance(value, str) or "–" not in value:
        return None
    start, end = [x.strip() for x in value.split("–", 1)]
    return {"start": start, "end": end}


def _normalise_window(value: Any):
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return {"start": value.get("start"), "end": value.get("end")}
    return _window_from_string(value)

FILE: .\assets\promo_studio\caption.py

"""Factual hand-off contract for an external caption writer such as Gemini."""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Mapping
from .spec import PostSpec, _freeze, _thaw

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CaptionInput:
    post_type: str
    date: str
    facts: Mapping[str, Any]
    illustrative: bool

    def __post_init__(self):
        # Keep the hand-off object itself immutable; to_dict() returns fresh JSON-safe copies.
        object.__setattr__(self, "facts", _freeze(dict(self.facts)))

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {"post_type": self.post_type, "date": self.date,
                "facts": _thaw(self.facts), "illustrative": self.illustrative}

def caption_input(spec: PostSpec) -> CaptionInput:
    """Create the only content contract that should be supplied to Gemini."""
    return CaptionInput(spec.kind, spec.date, spec.to_dict()["facts"], spec.illustrative)

def system_prompt() -> str:
    return (
        "Write a social caption using ONLY the supplied facts. "
        "Do not invent or infer dates, times, names, scores, astrological claims, "
        "predictions, benefits, or other factual details. Do not turn an upcoming "
        "event into an event happening today. Keep illustrative feature previews "
        "clearly illustrative. If a location is supplied, identify time-sensitive "
        "windows as being for that location. Do not describe Rahu or Ketu as Direct."
    )

"""Allowlisted local festival artwork resolver.

Upstream file names/URLs are never trusted. Add approved local files under
assets/festivals/ or configure SIDDHANTA_FESTIVAL_ASSET_DIR.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import os
from pathlib import Path

ALLOWLIST = {
    "Indira Ekadashi": "vishnu.webp",
    "Papankusha Ekadashi": "vishnu.webp",
    "Masik Shivratri": "masik_shivratri.webp",
    "Pradosh Vrat (Krishna)": "pradosh_krishna.webp",
    "Pradosh Vrat (Shukla)": "pradosh_shukla.webp",
    "Ashwin Amavasya": "amavasya.webp",
    "Navratri (Start)": "navratri.webp",
    "Durga Puja": "durga_puja.webp",
    "Durga Maha Ashtami": "durga.webp",
    "Durga Maha Navami": "durga.webp",
    "Vijay dashami": "vijayadashmi.webp",
    "Durga Visarjan": "durga.webp",
    "Sharad Purnima": "sharad_purnima.webp",
    "Karwa Chauth": "karwa_chauth.webp",
    "Ahoi Ashtami": "ahoi_ashtami.webp",
}


def resolve(name: str):
    filename = ALLOWLIST.get(name)
    if not filename:
        return None
    root = Path(os.environ.get("SIDDHANTA_FESTIVAL_ASSET_DIR", Path(__file__).parent / "assets" / "festivals"))
    path = (root / filename).resolve()
    try:
        path.relative_to(root.resolve())
    except ValueError:
        return None
    return path if path.is_file() else None




================================================================================
FILE: .\assets\promo_studio\models.py
================================================================================
"""Strict, validated view of the upstream Siddhanta JSON.

Rules enforced here (never in the renderer):
  * required facts missing/invalid  -> DataError (no post is produced)
  * optional facts missing/invalid  -> dropped + recorded in Day.warnings
  * nothing is ever computed or guessed; values are only checked and normalised
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import re, unicodedata
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import date as _date, datetime
from typing import Any, Mapping, Optional, Tuple


class DataError(ValueError):
    """Upstream payload is unusable or self-contradictory. Do not post."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Window:
    start: str
    end: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Festival:
    name_en: str
    date: _date


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Day:
    date: _date
    weekday: str
    location: Optional[str]
    location_display: Optional[str]
    tithi: str
    paksha: str
    nakshatra: str
    yoga: Optional[str] = None
    karana: Optional[str] = None
    masa: Optional[str] = None
    sunrise: Optional[str] = None
    sunset: Optional[str] = None
    rahu_kaal: Optional[Window] = None
    gulika_kaal: Optional[Window] = None
    yamaghanta: Optional[Window] = None
    abhijit: Optional[Window] = None
    brahma_muhurta: Optional[Window] = None
    godhuli_muhurta: Optional[Window] = None
    choghadiya: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None
    festivals_today: Tuple[Festival, ...] = ()
    raw_transit: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None  # opaque; only a transit post may read it
    warnings: Tuple[str, ...] = field(default=())


_CTRL = re.compile(r"[\x00-\x1f\x7f]")
_TIME = re.compile(r"^\s*(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})\s*([AaPp][Mm])\s*$")
MAX_NAME = 80


def _text(v: Any, maxlen: int = 60) -> Optional[str]:
    if not isinstance(v, str):
        return None
    s = unicodedata.normalize("NFC", _CTRL.sub(" ", v)).strip()
    s = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", s)
    return s[:maxlen] if s else None


def _time(v: Any) -> Optional[str]:
    if not isinstance(v, str):
        return None
    m = _TIME.match(v)
    if not m:
        return None
    h, mi = int(m.group(1)), int(m.group(2))
    if not (1 <= h <= 12 and 0 <= mi <= 59):
        return None
    return f"{h}:{mi:02d} {m.group(3).upper()}"


def _parse_date(v: Any, what: str) -> _date:
    try:
        return datetime.strptime(str(v), "%Y-%m-%d").date()
    except ValueError:
        raise DataError(f"{what}: expected YYYY-MM-DD, got {v!r}") from None


def parse_day(payload: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Day:
    if not isinstance(payload, Mapping) or not isinstance(payload.get("today"), Mapping):
        raise DataError("payload must be an object with a 'today' object")
    raw = payload["today"]
    warnings: list[str] = list(raw.get("warnings") or []) if isinstance(raw.get("warnings"), list) else []

    d = _parse_date(raw.get("date"), "today.date")
    weekday = d.strftime("%A")
    claimed = _text(raw.get("day_of_week"))
    if claimed and claimed.lower() != weekday.lower():
        raise DataError(f"day_of_week {claimed!r} contradicts date {d} ({weekday})")

    location = _text(raw.get("location"), MAX_NAME)
    location_display = _text(raw.get("location_display"), MAX_NAME)
    tithi, nak = _text(raw.get("tithi")), _text(raw.get("nakshatra"))
    paksha = (_text(raw.get("paksha")) or "").lower()
    if not tithi:
        raise DataError("today.tithi missing")
    if not nak:
        raise DataError("today.nakshatra missing")
    if paksha not in ("shukla", "krishna"):
        raise DataError(f"today.paksha must be shukla|krishna, got {raw.get('paksha')!r}")

    def opt_text(key):
        v = _text(raw.get(key))
        if raw.get(key) is not None and v is None:
            warnings.append(f"{key}: ignored invalid value")
        return v

    def opt_time(key):
        if raw.get(key) is None:
            return None
        t = _time(raw.get(key))
        if t is None:
            warnings.append(f"{key}: ignored invalid time {raw.get(key)!r}")
        return t

    def opt_window(key):
        w = raw.get(key)
        if w is None:
            return None
        if not isinstance(w, Mapping):
            warnings.append(f"{key}: ignored (not an object)")
            return None
        s, e = _time(w.get("start")), _time(w.get("end"))
        if s is None or e is None:
            warnings.append(f"{key}: ignored invalid window {dict(w)!r}")
            return None
        return Window(s, e)

    festivals = _festivals_today(raw, d, warnings)
    transit = payload.get("transits") if isinstance(payload.get("transits"), Mapping) else None

    return Day(d, weekday, location, location_display, tithi, paksha, nak,
               opt_text("yoga"), opt_text("karana"), opt_text("masa_purnimanta"),
               opt_time("sunrise"), opt_time("sunset"),
               opt_window("rahu_kaal"), opt_window("gulika_kaal"), opt_window("yamaghanta"),
               opt_window("abhijit_muhurta"), opt_window("brahma_muhurta"), opt_window("godhuli_muhurta"),
               raw.get("choghadiya") if isinstance(raw.get("choghadiya"), Mapping) else None,
               festivals, transit, tuple(warnings))


def _festivals_today(raw: Mapping[str, Any], today: _date, warnings: list) -> Tuple[Festival, ...]:
    """A festival is 'today' only on explicit upstream evidence.

    Preferred: raw['festivals_today'] = [{"name_en": ...}] (ask upstream to add this).
    Fallback : an entry in upcoming_festivals whose date == today AND days_away == 0.
    Conflicting evidence (one true, the other false) is rejected, never resolved by guessing.
    """
    out: list[Festival] = []
    explicit = raw.get("festivals_today")
    if explicit is not None:
        if not isinstance(explicit, list):
            raise DataError("today.festivals_today must be a list when supplied")
        for x in explicit:
            name = _text(x.get("name_en") if isinstance(x, Mapping) else None, MAX_NAME)
            if name:
                out.append(Festival(name, today))
            else:
                warnings.append("festivals_today: skipped entry without name_en")
        # An explicit list is authoritative, including an explicit empty list.
        return tuple(out)
    for x in raw.get("upcoming_festivals") or []:
        if not isinstance(x, Mapping):
            warnings.append("upcoming_festivals: skipped non-object entry"); continue
        name = _text(x.get("name_en"), MAX_NAME)
        try:
            fd = datetime.strptime(str(x.get("date")), "%Y-%m-%d").date()
        except ValueError:
            continue  # unparseable upcoming entries can never become a 'today' festival
        days_away = x.get("days_away")
        is_today_by_date = fd == today
        is_today_by_count = days_away == 0 and not isinstance(days_away, bool)
        if is_today_by_date and is_today_by_count and name:
            out.append(Festival(name, fd))
        elif is_today_by_date != is_today_by_count:
            warnings.append(f"festival {name!r}: date/days_away disagree - NOT treated as today")
    return tuple(out)


def post_warnings(day: Day, *, include_node_motion: bool = False) -> Tuple[str, ...]:
    """Return warnings relevant to a post type. Node normalization is transit-only."""
    if include_node_motion:
        return tuple(day.warnings)
    return tuple(w for w in day.warnings if "motion normalised to Retrograde" not in w)

FILE: .\assets\promo_studio\pipeline.py
================================================================================
from __future__ import annotations
import hashlib, io, os, tempfile
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Mapping
from .models import parse_day, DataError
from .adapter import adapt_siddhanta
from .posts import REGISTRY
from .selector import select
from .caption import caption_input, system_prompt
from .theme import require_raqm

class PromoStudio:
    def __init__(self, output_dir="output"):
        require_raqm()
        self.output_dir = Path(output_dir)

    def generate(self, payload: Mapping[str, Any], overwrite: bool = True, muhurta_data: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None) -> dict:
        # Accept either the internal normalized payload or the real Siddhanta workflow output.
        today = payload.get("today") if isinstance(payload, Mapping) else None
        looks_like_siddhanta = isinstance(payload, Mapping) and ("metadata" in payload or (isinstance(today, Mapping) and "day_of_week_en" in today))
        if looks_like_siddhanta:
            if not isinstance(today, Mapping) or not isinstance(today.get("english"), Mapping):
                raise DataError("not a Siddhanta payload: today.english is required")
            payload = adapt_siddhanta(payload, muhurta_data)
        day = parse_day(payload)                        # raises DataError on bad data
        spec = select(day)
        path = self.output_dir / f"{spec.date}_{spec.kind}_1080x1350.png"   # date is validated ISO
        if path.exists() and not overwrite:
            return self._result(spec, path, None, skipped=True)
        img = REGISTRY[spec.kind].render(spec)
        buf = io.BytesIO(); img.save(buf, "PNG", optimize=True)                # no metadata => reproducible
        data = buf.getvalue()
        self.output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        fd, tmp = tempfile.mkstemp(dir=self.output_dir, suffix=".tmp")         # atomic: no half-written PNGs
        try:
            with os.fdopen(fd, "wb") as fh: fh.write(data)
            os.replace(tmp, path)
        except BaseException:
            try: os.unlink(tmp)
            except OSError: pass
            raise
        return self._result(spec, path, hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest())

    @staticmethod
    def _result(spec, path, sha, skipped=False):
        return {"post_type": spec.kind, "path": str(path), "width": 1080, "height": 1350,
                "sha256": sha, "skipped": skipped, "illustrative": spec.illustrative,
                "warnings": list(spec.warnings),
                "caption_facts": spec.to_dict()["facts"],
                "caption_input": caption_input(spec).to_dict(),
                "caption_system_prompt": system_prompt()}




================================================================================
FILE: .\assets\promo_studio\selector.py
================================================================================
"""Which post? Pure function of validated Day + registry. No Pillow, no I/O."""
from __future__ import annotations
from typing import Callable, List, Optional
from .models import Day, DataError
from .posts import REGISTRY
from .spec import PostSpec

# Ordered candidate kinds per rule; first one that is registered AND can build a spec wins.
def _festival(day): return ["festival"] if day.festivals_today else []
WEEKDAY_RULES = {
    "Tuesday": ["muhurta"], "Thursday": ["muhurta"], "Saturday": ["kundli"], "Wednesday": ["chakra"],
    "Friday": ["transit"],
    "Sunday": ["panchang"], "Monday": ["panchang"],
}
FALLBACK = "panchang"

def candidates(day: Day) -> List[str]:
    return _festival(day) + WEEKDAY_RULES.get(day.weekday, []) + [FALLBACK]

def select(day: Day) -> PostSpec:
    for kind in candidates(day):
        pt = REGISTRY.get(kind)
        if pt is None:
            continue                                   # e.g. 'transit' not implemented yet
        spec = pt.build(day)
        if spec is not None:
            return spec
    raise DataError("no post type could be built from this payload")




================================================================================
FILE: .\assets\promo_studio\spec.py
================================================================================
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from types import MappingProxyType
from typing import Any, Mapping, Tuple


def _freeze(value: Any) -> Any:
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return MappingProxyType({k: _freeze(v) for k, v in value.items()})
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
        return tuple(_freeze(v) for v in value)
    if isinstance(value, set):
        return frozenset(_freeze(v) for v in value)
    return value


def _thaw(value: Any) -> Any:
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return {k: _thaw(v) for k, v in value.items()}
    if isinstance(value, tuple):
        return [_thaw(v) for v in value]
    if isinstance(value, frozenset):
        return [_thaw(v) for v in value]
    return value


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PostSpec:
    """Validated content boundary shared by renderers and caption generation."""
    kind: str
    date: str
    facts: Mapping[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    illustrative: bool = False
    warnings: Tuple[str, ...] = ()

    def __post_init__(self):
        object.__setattr__(self, "facts", _freeze(dict(self.facts)))
        object.__setattr__(self, "warnings", tuple(self.warnings))

    def to_dict(self):
        return {"kind": self.kind, "date": self.date, "facts": _thaw(self.facts),
                "illustrative": self.illustrative, "warnings": list(self.warnings)}
