"""Deterministic design tokens and bundled font resolution."""
from __future__ import annotations
import os
from functools import lru_cache
from pathlib import Path
from PIL import ImageFont, features

CREAM = "#F7F0E3"; CREAM_DARK = "#E9DDC9"; MAROON = "#6F2638"; GOLD = "#B88A3B"
GOLD_LIGHT = "#D9B86C"; NAVY = "#10233F"; INK = "#2B2522"; MUTED = "#746B63"; WHITE = "#FFFDF8"
MUTED_DEEP = "#5E554D"   # small muted text drawn directly on the (warm) backgrounds: >= 4.5:1 on every theme
W, H, M = 1080, 1350, 76

# Social-first type scale. Nothing intended for the final artwork falls below 20 px.
TYPE = {
    "title": 64,
    "hero": 44,
    "body": 28,
    "label": 22,
    "minimum": 20,
    "meta": 22,
    "footer": 22,
    "hindi_subtitle": 26,
}

class FontError(RuntimeError):
    pass

_BUNDLED = Path(__file__).parent / "assets" / "fonts"
_DIRS = [_BUNDLED]
# Bundled fonts are the production source of truth. External directories remain only
# as an explicit developer override, never as a silent production dependency.
if os.environ.get("SIDDHANTA_FONT_DIR"):
    _DIRS.insert(0, Path(os.environ["SIDDHANTA_FONT_DIR"]))

_ROLES = {
    "sans": ["NotoSans-Regular.ttf"],
    "sans_bold": ["NotoSans-Bold.ttf"],
    "serif_bold": ["NotoSerif-Bold.ttf"],
    "devanagari": ["NotoSansDevanagari-Regular.ttf"],
    "devanagari_bold": ["NotoSansDevanagari-Bold.ttf"],
    "devanagari_serif": ["NotoSerifDevanagari-Regular.ttf"],
    "devanagari_serif_bold": ["NotoSerifDevanagari-Bold.ttf"],
}

@lru_cache(maxsize=None)
def _path(role: str) -> str:
    for d in _DIRS:
        for name in _ROLES[role]:
            p = d / name
            if p.is_file():
                return str(p)
    raise FontError(f"no bundled font for role {role!r}; expected {_ROLES[role]} in {_BUNDLED}")

@lru_cache(maxsize=256)
def get_font(role: str, size: int):
    return ImageFont.truetype(_path(role), int(size))

def sans(size, bold=False): return get_font("sans_bold" if bold else "sans", int(size))
def serif(size): return get_font("serif_bold", int(size))
def devanagari(size, bold=False): return get_font("devanagari_bold" if bold else "devanagari", int(size))
def devanagari_serif(size, bold=False): return get_font("devanagari_serif_bold" if bold else "devanagari_serif", int(size))

def require_raqm() -> None:
    if not features.check("raqm"):
        raise FontError("Pillow libraqm/Harfbuzz/FriBiDi support is required for Devanagari shaping")

================================================================================
FILE: .\assets\tests\test_studio.py
================================================================================
import copy, json, pathlib, pytest
from PIL import Image
from promo_studio import PromoStudio, DataError
from promo_studio.models import parse_day

ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).parent.parent
FIXTURES = pathlib.Path(__file__).parent / "fixtures"
BASE = json.load(open(ROOT / "sample_payload.json", encoding="utf-8"))
DAILY = json.load(open(FIXTURES / "daily_panchang.json", encoding="utf-8"))
MUHURTA = json.load(open(FIXTURES / "muhurta_data.json", encoding="utf-8"))
def P(**over):
    p = copy.deepcopy(BASE); p["today"].update(over); return p
def kind(tmp_path, p): return PromoStudio(tmp_path).generate(p)["post_type"]

def test_sample_is_panchang_1080x1350(tmp_path):
    r = PromoStudio(tmp_path).generate(BASE); assert r["post_type"] == "panchang"
    assert Image.open(r["path"]).size == (1080, 1350)

def test_upcoming_festival_is_not_today(tmp_path):
    assert kind(tmp_path, BASE) != "festival"

def test_festival_today_requires_date_and_days_away(tmp_path):
    ok = P(); ok["today"]["upcoming_festivals"][0].update(date="2026-10-04", days_away=0)
    assert kind(tmp_path, ok) == "festival"
    bad = P(); bad["today"]["upcoming_festivals"][0].update(date="2026-10-04", days_away=2)
    assert kind(tmp_path, bad) != "festival"
    assert any("disagree" in w for w in parse_day(bad).warnings)

def test_explicit_festivals_today_wins(tmp_path):
    assert kind(tmp_path, P(festivals_today=[{"name_en": "Maha Navami"}])) == "festival"

@pytest.mark.parametrize("day,date,expect", [("Tuesday","2026-10-06","muhurta"),("Thursday","2026-10-08","muhurta"),
    ("Saturday","2026-10-10","kundli"),("Wednesday","2026-10-07","chakra"),("Friday","2026-10-09","panchang"),
    ("Monday","2026-10-05","panchang")])
def test_weekday_rules(tmp_path, day, date, expect):
    p = P(day_of_week=day, date=date, upcoming_festivals=[]); assert kind(tmp_path, p) == expect

def test_muhurta_falls_back_without_windows(tmp_path):
    p = P(day_of_week="Tuesday", date="2026-10-06", upcoming_festivals=[], rahu_kaal=None, abhijit_muhurta=None)
    assert kind(tmp_path, p) == "panchang"

@pytest.mark.parametrize("over", [{"tithi": None}, {"nakshatra": ""}, {"paksha": "???"}, {"date": "04/10/2026"},
                                  {"day_of_week": "Tuesday"}])
def test_bad_core_data_refuses(tmp_path, over):
    with pytest.raises(DataError): PromoStudio(tmp_path).generate(P(**over))
    assert not list(pathlib.Path(tmp_path).glob("*"))          # nothing written

def test_missing_today_refuses(tmp_path):
    with pytest.raises(DataError): PromoStudio(tmp_path).generate({})

def test_invalid_optional_time_dropped_not_drawn(tmp_path):
    d = parse_day(P(sunrise="25:99 PM", rahu_kaal={"start": "x", "end": "y"}))
    assert d.sunrise is None and d.rahu_kaal is None and len(d.warnings) == 2

def test_long_festival_name_renders(tmp_path):
    p = P(); p["today"]["upcoming_festivals"][0].update(date="2026-10-04", days_away=0,
        name_en="Sharad Navratri Ghatasthapana Pratipada Kalash Sthapana Muhurta Observance")
    assert Image.open(PromoStudio(tmp_path).generate(p)["path"]).size == (1080, 1350)

def test_deterministic_bytes(tmp_path):
    a = PromoStudio(tmp_path / "a").generate(BASE)["sha256"]; b = PromoStudio(tmp_path / "b").generate(BASE)["sha256"]
    assert a == b

def test_showcases_flagged_illustrative(tmp_path):
    p = P(day_of_week="Saturday", date="2026-10-10", upcoming_festivals=[])
    assert PromoStudio(tmp_path).generate(p)["illustrative"] is True

def test_no_overwrite_skips(tmp_path):
    s = PromoStudio(tmp_path); s.generate(BASE); assert s.generate(BASE, overwrite=False)["skipped"]


def test_postspec_facts_are_immutable(tmp_path):
    from promo_studio.models import parse_day
    from promo_studio.selector import select
    spec = select(parse_day(BASE))
    with pytest.raises(TypeError):
        spec.facts["invented"] = "x"


def test_explicit_empty_festivals_today_is_authoritative(tmp_path):
    p = P(festivals_today=[])
    p["today"]["upcoming_festivals"][0].update(date="2026-10-04", days_away=0)
    assert kind(tmp_path, p) != "festival"


def test_invalid_festivals_today_shape_refuses(tmp_path):
    with pytest.raises(DataError):
        PromoStudio(tmp_path).generate(P(festivals_today={"name_en": "bad"}))


def test_caption_handshake_contains_only_spec_facts(tmp_path):
    r = PromoStudio(tmp_path).generate(BASE)
    assert set(r["caption_input"]) == {"post_type", "date", "facts", "illustrative"}
    assert r["caption_input"]["facts"] == r["caption_facts"]
    assert "ONLY the supplied facts" in r["caption_system_prompt"]

def test_real_siddhanta_binding(tmp_path):
    daily = copy.deepcopy(DAILY)
    muhurta = copy.deepcopy(MUHURTA)
    muhurta["date"] = daily["today"]["date"]
    r = PromoStudio(tmp_path).generate(daily, muhurta_data=muhurta)
    assert r['post_type'] == 'panchang'
    assert r['caption_facts']['tithi'] == 'Krishna Navami'
    assert r['caption_facts']['nakshatra'] == 'Punarvasu'
    assert r['caption_facts']['abhijit'] == '12:03 PM – 12:51 PM'
    assert r['caption_facts']['location'] == 'Ujjain, India (Standard Astronomical Baseline)'


def test_real_siddhanta_explicit_empty_festival_is_authoritative(tmp_path):
    daily = copy.deepcopy(DAILY)
    daily['today']['date'] = '2026-10-06'
    daily['today']['day_of_week_en'] = 'Tuesday'
    muhurta = copy.deepcopy(MUHURTA)
    muhurta["date"] = daily["today"]["date"]
    r = PromoStudio(tmp_path).generate(daily, muhurta_data=muhurta)
    assert r['post_type'] == 'muhurta'


def test_real_siddhanta_transit_binding(tmp_path):
    daily = copy.deepcopy(DAILY)
    daily['today']['date'] = '2026-10-09'
    daily['today']['day_of_week_en'] = 'Friday'
    daily['today']['festivals_today'] = []
    muhurta = copy.deepcopy(MUHURTA)
    muhurta["date"] = daily["today"]["date"]
    r = PromoStudio(tmp_path).generate(daily, muhurta_data=muhurta)
    assert r['post_type'] == 'transit'
    assert r['caption_facts']['planet'] == 'Mars'
    assert r['caption_facts']['rashi'] == 'Cancer'
    assert 'phase_start' not in r['caption_facts']
    assert 'phase_end' not in r['caption_facts']


def test_real_siddhanta_muhurta_windows_are_bound(tmp_path):
    daily = copy.deepcopy(DAILY)
    daily['today']['date'] = '2026-10-06'
    daily['today']['day_of_week_en'] = 'Tuesday'
    daily['today']['festivals_today'] = []
    muhurta = copy.deepcopy(MUHURTA)
    muhurta["date"] = daily["today"]["date"]
    r = PromoStudio(tmp_path).generate(daily, muhurta_data=muhurta)
    assert r['post_type'] == 'muhurta'
    assert r['caption_facts']['abhijit'] == '12:03 PM – 12:51 PM'
    assert r['caption_facts']['location'] == 'Ujjain, India (Standard Astronomical Baseline)'
    assert r['caption_facts']['rahu'] == '4:42 PM – 6:12 PM'


def test_real_siddhanta_muhurta_date_mismatch_refuses(tmp_path):
    daily = copy.deepcopy(DAILY)
    muhurta = copy.deepcopy(MUHURTA)
    muhurta["date"] = "2026-10-03"
    with pytest.raises(DataError, match="muhurta_data.date"):
        PromoStudio(tmp_path).generate(daily, muhurta_data=muhurta)
    assert not list(pathlib.Path(tmp_path).glob("*"))


def test_siddhanta_nodes_are_normalized_retrograde(tmp_path):
    daily = copy.deepcopy(DAILY)
    daily["today"]["date"] = "2026-10-09"
    daily["today"]["day_of_week_en"] = "Friday"
    daily["today"]["festivals_today"] = []
    muh = copy.deepcopy(MUHURTA); muh["date"] = daily["today"]["date"]
    r = PromoStudio(tmp_path).generate(daily, muhurta_data=muh)
    from promo_studio.adapter import adapt_siddhanta
    muh = copy.deepcopy(MUHURTA); muh["date"] = daily["today"]["date"]
    adapted = adapt_siddhanta(daily, muh)
    assert adapted["transits"]["positions"]["rahu"]["is_retrograde"] is True
    assert adapted["transits"]["positions"]["rahu"]["motion_en"] == "Retrograde"
    assert adapted["transits"]["major_transits"]["ketu"]["motion_en"] == "Retrograde"


def test_siddhanta_location_reaches_facts(tmp_path):
    daily = copy.deepcopy(DAILY)
    muh = copy.deepcopy(MUHURTA); muh["date"] = daily["today"]["date"]
    r = PromoStudio(tmp_path).generate(daily, muhurta_data=muh)
    assert r["caption_facts"]["location"] == "Ujjain, India (Standard Astronomical Baseline)"


def test_siddhanta_missing_english_has_specific_error(tmp_path):
    daily = copy.deepcopy(DAILY)
    del daily["today"]["english"]
    with pytest.raises(DataError, match="not a Siddhanta payload"):
        PromoStudio(tmp_path).generate(daily, muhurta_data=copy.deepcopy(MUHURTA))


def test_raqm_is_available_for_devanagari_ci():
    from PIL import features
    assert features.check("raqm"), "Pillow libraqm/Harfbuzz/FriBiDi support is required"

def test_devanagari_fonts_are_resolvable():
    from promo_studio.theme import devanagari, devanagari_serif
    assert devanagari(24).getlength("पंचांग") > 0
    assert devanagari_serif(24).getlength("पंचांग") > 0


def test_muhurta_graphics_render_with_choghadiya(tmp_path):
    daily = copy.deepcopy(DAILY)
    daily["today"]["date"] = "2026-10-06"
    daily["today"]["day_of_week_en"] = "Tuesday"
    daily["today"]["festivals_today"] = []
    muh = copy.deepcopy(MUHURTA); muh["date"] = daily["today"]["date"]
    r = PromoStudio(tmp_path).generate(daily, muhurta_data=muh)
    assert r["post_type"] == "muhurta"
    assert Image.open(r["path"]).size == (1080, 1350)

def test_corrupt_festival_asset_falls_back(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    asset_dir = tmp_path / "assets"; asset_dir.mkdir()
    (asset_dir / "vishnu.webp").write_bytes(b"not-an-image")
    monkeypatch.setenv("SIDDHANTA_FESTIVAL_ASSET_DIR", str(asset_dir))
    daily = copy.deepcopy(DAILY)
    daily["today"]["festivals_today"] = [{"name_en": "Indira Ekadashi"}]
    muh = copy.deepcopy(MUHURTA); muh["date"] = daily["today"]["date"]
    r = PromoStudio(tmp_path / "out").generate(daily, muhurta_data=muh)
    assert Image.open(r["path"]).size == (1080, 1350)


def test_oversized_festival_asset_falls_back(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    asset_dir = tmp_path / "assets"; asset_dir.mkdir()
    # Valid image header with >40M pixels; renderer must reject before full decode.
    from PIL import Image
    huge = Image.new("RGB", (7000, 6000), "white")
    huge.save(asset_dir / "vishnu.webp", format="WEBP", quality=1)
    monkeypatch.setenv("SIDDHANTA_FESTIVAL_ASSET_DIR", str(asset_dir))
    daily = copy.deepcopy(DAILY)
    daily["today"]["festivals_today"] = [{"name_en": "Indira Ekadashi"}]
    muh = copy.deepcopy(MUHURTA); muh["date"] = daily["today"]["date"]
    r = PromoStudio(tmp_path / "out").generate(daily, muhurta_data=muh)
    assert Image.open(r["path"]).size == (1080, 1350)


def test_location_display_is_separate_from_metadata_name(tmp_path):
    daily = copy.deepcopy(DAILY)
    daily["metadata"]["location"]["display_name"] = "Ujjain"
    muh = copy.deepcopy(MUHURTA); muh["date"] = daily["today"]["date"]
    r = PromoStudio(tmp_path).generate(daily, muhurta_data=muh)
    assert r["caption_facts"]["location"] == "Ujjain, India (Standard Astronomical Baseline)"
    assert r["caption_facts"]["location_display"] == "Ujjain"


def test_node_normalisation_is_recorded_as_warning(tmp_path):
    daily = copy.deepcopy(DAILY)
    daily["today"]["date"] = "2026-10-09"
    daily["today"]["day_of_week_en"] = "Friday"
    daily["today"]["festivals_today"] = []
    muh = copy.deepcopy(MUHURTA); muh["date"] = daily["today"]["date"]
    r = PromoStudio(tmp_path).generate(daily, muhurta_data=muh)
    assert any("motion normalised" in w for w in r["warnings"])


def test_real_panchang_render_exercises_devanagari(tmp_path):
    daily = copy.deepcopy(DAILY)
    muh = copy.deepcopy(MUHURTA); muh["date"] = daily["today"]["date"]
    r = PromoStudio(tmp_path).generate(daily, muhurta_data=muh)
    assert Image.open(r["path"]).size == (1080, 1350)
    from promo_studio.theme import devanagari
    assert devanagari(24).getbbox("दैनिक पंचांग") is not None


def test_location_display_falls_back_to_city(tmp_path):
    daily = copy.deepcopy(DAILY)
    muh = copy.deepcopy(MUHURTA); muh["date"] = daily["today"]["date"]
    r = PromoStudio(tmp_path).generate(daily, muhurta_data=muh)
    assert r["caption_facts"]["location_display"] == "Ujjain"

GOLDEN_HASHES = {
    "panchang": "2158c1d50737586ae1876eeeb79d9de3f99db4d51ff5460dc49b3f18ac987b68",
    "muhurta": "e25d0924f0b43322c66711de7b74dc3460ae2a89406e89afb67a1b1f08b000d8",
    "transit": "e2e7a16bce96b7f7c44ad514d220b592c696501823087979274f23e820331ccc",
    "festival": "65f2f4b156f50f94b518cd2e9ce54a7649dbff278c320fe3c2a5759c4a017147",
    "kundli": "010ce47764f7e7540c68ff7143691d5765b080d3002db0542d80027dd28a7c04",
    "chakra": "efc2f0d4f86306cb65672c640c18c709a1354c5fbf0281a5afc50b831036bdc5",
}


def _real_post_payload(kind):
    d = copy.deepcopy(DAILY)
    dates = {
        "panchang": ("2026-10-04", "Sunday"),
        "muhurta": ("2026-10-06", "Tuesday"),
        "transit": ("2026-10-09", "Friday"),
        "festival": ("2026-10-11", "Sunday"),
        "kundli": ("2026-10-10", "Saturday"),
        "chakra": ("2026-10-07", "Wednesday"),
    }
    date_value, weekday = dates[kind]
    d["today"]["date"] = date_value
    d["today"]["day_of_week_en"] = weekday
    d["today"]["festivals_today"] = [{"name_en": "Navratri (Start)"}] if kind == "festival" else []
    m = copy.deepcopy(MUHURTA)
    m["date"] = date_value
    return d, m


def test_golden_hash_per_post_type(tmp_path):
    for kind, expected in GOLDEN_HASHES.items():
        daily, muh = _real_post_payload(kind)
        result = PromoStudio(tmp_path / kind).generate(daily, muhurta_data=muh)
        assert result["post_type"] == kind
        assert result["sha256"] == expected


def test_nested_postspec_facts_are_immutable(tmp_path):
    from promo_studio.models import parse_day
    from promo_studio.selector import select
    spec = select(parse_day(BASE))
    # The real sample does not contain Choghadiya; use a direct PostSpec to exercise nesting.
    from promo_studio.spec import PostSpec
    nested = PostSpec("x", "2026-10-04", {"items": [{"name": "A"}]})
    with pytest.raises(TypeError):
        nested.facts["items"][0]["name"] = "B"
    with pytest.raises(TypeError):
        nested.facts["items"] += ("x",)


def test_node_warnings_only_reach_transit(tmp_path):
    daily, muh = _real_post_payload("panchang")
    daily["today"]["date"] = "2026-10-04"
    muh["date"] = "2026-10-04"
    p = PromoStudio(tmp_path / "p").generate(daily, muhurta_data=muh)
    assert not any("motion normalised" in w for w in p["warnings"])
    daily, muh = _real_post_payload("transit")
    t = PromoStudio(tmp_path / "t").generate(daily, muhurta_data=muh)
    assert any("motion normalised" in w for w in t["warnings"])

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# v3.1: JSON-safe caption hand-off, Choghadiya/Rahu policy, timeline lanes
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
import json

def _muhurta_payload(rahu=None, choghadiya=None, abhijit=None, drop_abhijit=False):
    daily, muh = _real_post_payload("muhurta")
    if rahu: muh["muhurtas"]["rahu_kaal"].update(start=rahu[0], end=rahu[1])
    if abhijit: muh["muhurtas"]["abhijit_muhurta"].update(start=abhijit[0], end=abhijit[1])
    if choghadiya is not None: muh["choghadiya"]["day"] = choghadiya
    return daily, muh

def _slot(name, start, end, good=True):
    return {"name_en": name, "start": start, "end": end, "is_auspicious": good}


@pytest.mark.parametrize("post_kind", list(GOLDEN_HASHES))
def test_caption_hand_off_is_json_serialisable_for_every_post_type(tmp_path, post_kind):
    daily, muh = _real_post_payload(post_kind)
    r = PromoStudio(tmp_path).generate(daily, muhurta_data=muh)
    assert r["post_type"] == post_kind
    for key in ("caption_input", "caption_facts"):
        encoded = json.dumps(r[key])                      # mappingproxy/tuple regressions fail here
        assert json.loads(encoded) == r[key]              # plain dict/list/str all the way down


def test_muhurta_caption_input_with_nested_choghadiya_serialises(tmp_path):
    daily, muh = _muhurta_payload()
    r = PromoStudio(tmp_path).generate(daily, muhurta_data=muh)
    facts = json.loads(json.dumps(r["caption_input"]))["facts"]
    assert isinstance(facts["choghadiya_day"], list) and facts["choghadiya_day"]
    assert all(isinstance(x, dict) for x in facts["choghadiya_day"])


def test_caption_dicts_are_independent_copies_of_the_frozen_spec(tmp_path):
    from promo_studio.caption import caption_input
    from promo_studio.selector import select
    daily, muh = _muhurta_payload()
    from promo_studio.adapter import adapt_siddhanta
    spec = select(parse_day(adapt_siddhanta(daily, muh)))
    ci = caption_input(spec)
    first = ci.to_dict(); first["facts"]["choghadiya_day"].append({"name_en": "invented"})
    assert ci.to_dict()["facts"]["choghadiya_day"] == spec.to_dict()["facts"]["choghadiya_day"]
    with pytest.raises(TypeError):
        spec.facts["choghadiya_day"][0]["name_en"] = "tampered"      # frozen spec unaffected


def test_overlapping_choghadiya_is_omitted_and_listed_as_excluded(tmp_path):
    daily, muh = _muhurta_payload(rahu=("10:00 AM", "11:30 AM"), choghadiya=[
        _slot("Labh", "9:17 AM", "10:46 AM"), _slot("Amrit", "10:46 AM", "12:15 PM"),
        _slot("Shubh", "1:44 PM", "3:13 PM")])
    f = PromoStudio(tmp_path).generate(daily, muhurta_data=muh)["caption_facts"]
    assert [x["name_en"] for x in f["choghadiya_day"]] == ["Shubh"]
    assert [x["name_en"] for x in f["choghadiya_excluded_overlapping_rahu"]] == ["Labh", "Amrit"]


def test_slot_touching_rahu_boundary_is_not_an_overlap(tmp_path):
    daily, muh = _muhurta_payload(rahu=("12:15 PM", "1:44 PM"), choghadiya=[
        _slot("Amrit", "10:46 AM", "12:15 PM"), _slot("Shubh", "1:44 PM", "3:13 PM")])
    f = PromoStudio(tmp_path).generate(daily, muhurta_data=muh)["caption_facts"]
    assert [x["name_en"] for x in f["choghadiya_day"]] == ["Amrit", "Shubh"]
    assert "choghadiya_excluded_overlapping_rahu" not in f


def test_internal_policy_note_is_never_sent_to_the_caption_writer(tmp_path):
    from promo_studio.posts import muhurta
    daily, muh = _muhurta_payload(rahu=("10:00 AM", "11:30 AM"))
    r = PromoStudio(tmp_path).generate(daily, muhurta_data=muh)
    assert "choghadiya_policy" not in r["caption_facts"]
    assert "Jyotish judgement" not in json.dumps(r["caption_input"], ensure_ascii=False)
    assert "Jyotish judgement" not in r["caption_system_prompt"]
    assert muhurta.CHOGHADIYA_POLICY.startswith("Auspicious Choghadiya")   # kept in code


def test_muhurta_hides_choghadiya_card_when_every_slot_overlaps_rahu(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    from promo_studio.posts import muhurta
    calls = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(muhurta, "choghadiya_list", lambda *a, **k: calls.append(a) or True)

# -- timeline lanes --------------------------------------------------------
from promo_studio.graphics import components as gc
from promo_studio.theme import GOLD, MAROON, WHITE, H

SUNRISE, SUNSET = "6:19 AM", "6:12 PM"
BANDS_SEPARATE = [("Abhijit", "12:03 PM", "12:51 PM", GOLD), ("Rahu Kaal", "4:42 PM", "6:12 PM", MAROON)]
BANDS_OVERLAP = [("Abhijit", "12:03 PM", "12:51 PM", GOLD), ("Rahu Kaal", "12:00 PM", "1:30 PM", MAROON)]


def _lane_count(bands):
    return len(gc._plan_timeline(SUNRISE, SUNSET, bands, 104, 976))


def test_separate_windows_share_one_lane_and_overlapping_windows_get_two():
    assert _lane_count(BANDS_SEPARATE) == 1
    assert _lane_count(BANDS_OVERLAP) == 2


def test_adjacent_windows_with_colliding_labels_are_split_into_lanes():
    touching = [("Abhijit", "12:03 PM", "12:51 PM", GOLD), ("Rahu Kaal", "12:51 PM", "2:21 PM", MAROON)]
    assert _lane_count(touching) == 2          # bars do not overlap but their labels would


def test_timeline_card_grows_with_lanes_and_contains_sunrise_sunset_row():
    assert gc.timeline_height(SUNRISE, SUNSET, BANDS_OVERLAP) > gc.timeline_height(SUNRISE, SUNSET, BANDS_SEPARATE)
    from PIL import ImageDraw
    for bands in (BANDS_SEPARATE, BANDS_OVERLAP):
        need = gc.timeline_height(SUNRISE, SUNSET, bands)
        im = Image.new("RGB", (1080, 700), "#F7F0E3")
        bottom = gc.timeline_bar(ImageDraw.Draw(im), (76, 20, 1004, 20 + need), SUNRISE, SUNSET, bands)
        assert bottom == 20 + need
        # sunrise/sunset text (MUTED) must lie inside the card: nothing muted below its bottom edge
        muted = (0x74, 0x6B, 0x63)
        below = im.crop((76, bottom + 4, 1004, 700))
        assert muted not in {c for _, c in below.getcolors(maxcolors=1_000_000)}


def test_a_card_that_is_too_short_is_grown_not_overflowed():
    from PIL import ImageDraw
    im = Image.new("RGB", (1080, 700), "#F7F0E3")
    need = gc.timeline_height(SUNRISE, SUNSET, BANDS_OVERLAP)
    bottom = gc.timeline_bar(ImageDraw.Draw(im), (76, 20, 1004, 20 + 120), SUNRISE, SUNSET, BANDS_OVERLAP)
    assert bottom == 20 + need


def _ink_rows(im, color, x0, x1, y0, y1, tol=40):
    hits = []
    for y in range(y0, y1):
        for x in range(x0, x1):
            px = im.getpixel((x, y))
            if all(abs(px[i] - color[i]) <= tol for i in range(3)):
                hits.append((x, y))
    return hits


@pytest.mark.parametrize("bands,expected_labels", [(BANDS_SEPARATE, 2), (BANDS_OVERLAP, 2)])
def test_every_band_label_is_visible_on_the_white_card(bands, expected_labels):
    """v3 drew maroon-band labels in white-on-white, so 'Rahu Kaal' was invisible."""
    from PIL import ImageDraw
    need = gc.timeline_height(SUNRISE, SUNSET, bands)
    im = Image.new("RGB", (1080, 700), "#F7F0E3")
    gc.timeline_bar(ImageDraw.Draw(im), (76, 20, 1004, 20 + need), SUNRISE, SUNSET, bands)
    lanes = gc._plan_timeline(SUNRISE, SUNSET, bands, 104, 976)
    pitch = gc._TL_LABEL_H + gc._TL_BAR_GAP + gc._TL_BAR_H + gc._TL_LANE_GAP
    seen = 0
    for i, lane in enumerate(lanes):
        row_top = 20 + gc._TL_TITLE_H + i * pitch
        for item in lane:
            text_x0 = int(item["label_x"]) + gc._TL_SWATCH + 8
            ink = _ink_rows(im, (0x6F, 0x26, 0x38), text_x0, int(item["label_x2"]), row_top, row_top + gc._TL_LABEL_H)
            assert len(ink) > 40, f"label {item['label']!r} has no visible ink"
            seen += 1
    assert seen == expected_labels


def test_panchang_overlap_case_fits_above_the_footer(tmp_path):
    daily, muh = _real_post_payload("panchang")
    muh["muhurtas"]["rahu_kaal"].update(start="12:00 PM", end="1:30 PM")
    r = PromoStudio(tmp_path).generate(daily, muhurta_data=muh)
    assert r["post_type"] == "panchang" and Image.open(r["path"]).size == (1080, 1350)
    # panchang layout: timeline top is y=916 with Masa line present
    assert 916 + gc.timeline_height(SUNRISE, SUNSET, BANDS_OVERLAP) <= H - 112


# Edge layouts, reviewed visually before pinning (see V3_1_UPDATE.md).
GOLDEN_EDGE_HASHES = {
    "panchang_overlap": "e6078409816a9773dba24d46d820d4c9fc9e752addadbe2c5d31f7fd41a45212",
    "muhurta_all_excluded": "9bf83c83c471cd38bb0d63d5e98a8a976938ed4b5df76b6672da89a1ec0814e3",
}


def test_golden_hash_edge_layouts(tmp_path):
    cases = {
        "panchang_overlap": ("panchang", {"rahu": ("12:00 PM", "1:30 PM")}),
        "muhurta_all_excluded": ("muhurta", {"rahu": ("9:00 AM", "6:00 PM")}),
    }
    for name, (kind, over) in cases.items():
        daily, muh = _real_post_payload(kind)
        if "rahu" in over: muh["muhurtas"]["rahu_kaal"].update(start=over["rahu"][0], end=over["rahu"][1])
        got = PromoStudio(tmp_path / name).generate(daily, muhurta_data=muh)["sha256"]
        assert got == GOLDEN_EDGE_HASHES[name], f"{name}: {got}"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# v3.2: procedural backgrounds (NumPy) and pre-rendered SVG ornaments
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
import hashlib, subprocess, sys
import numpy as np
from promo_studio.graphics.background import BackgroundEngine, THEMES
from promo_studio.graphics import ornaments as orn

GOLDEN_BACKGROUND_HASHES = {
    "cream": "6034c262d08942d5874fcdd580c6e9a3dd8522cd046a44a3fbbd4c4d4902ccaf",
    "festival_glow": "cc05b087ba9dbba4c089bc41acf8b7232252ac41d24bd940686ec031142a0d21",
    "solar": "f3a31151ef15ae7c3fb9cdd9d3b255c763c8ac10c0b277dbbb29feebcaafc446",
    "sunrise": "97e50161611cb8838e39c00d8db8a9a0b225a8b2bf0288dac49c2f9c3a3aa79a",
}   # reviewed visually


def _lum(rgb):
    c = np.asarray(rgb, float) / 255
    c = np.where(c <= 0.03928, c / 12.92, ((c + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4)
    return 0.2126 * c[..., 0] + 0.7152 * c[..., 1] + 0.0722 * c[..., 2]


def _contrast(fg, bg):
    hi, lo = sorted((float(_lum(fg)), float(_lum(bg))), reverse=True)
    return (hi + 0.05) / (lo + 0.05)


def _hexrgb(h): return tuple(int(h[i:i + 2], 16) for i in (1, 3, 5))


def test_background_cream_is_the_flat_brand_colour():
    im = BackgroundEngine.create("cream")
    assert im.size == (1080, 1350) and im.getcolors() == [(1080 * 1350, _hexrgb("#F7F0E3"))]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("theme", sorted(THEMES))
def test_backgrounds_are_deterministic_and_correctly_sized(theme):
    a, b = BackgroundEngine.create(theme), BackgroundEngine.create(theme)
    assert a.size == (1080, 1350) and a.mode == "RGB" and a.tobytes() == b.tobytes()


def test_unknown_background_theme_is_rejected():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="unknown background theme"):
        BackgroundEngine.create("neon")


@pytest.mark.parametrize("theme", sorted(THEMES))
def test_text_stays_readable_on_the_darkest_background_pixel(theme):
    px = np.asarray(BackgroundEngine.create(theme)).reshape(-1, 3)
    darkest = px[int(np.argmin(_lum(px)))]
    assert _contrast(_hexrgb("#6F2638"), darkest) >= 7       # maroon titles
    assert _contrast(_hexrgb("#2B2522"), darkest) >= 10      # ink
    assert _contrast(_hexrgb("#5E554D"), darkest) >= 4.5     # footer text (MUTED_DEEP)


def test_golden_hash_per_background_theme():
    got = {t: hashlib.sha256(BackgroundEngine.create(t).tobytes()).hexdigest() for t in sorted(THEMES)}
    assert got == GOLDEN_BACKGROUND_HASHES


def test_post_to_theme_mapping(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    from promo_studio.graphics import components
    seen = []
    real = components.BackgroundEngine.create
    monkeypatch.setattr(components.BackgroundEngine, "create", staticmethod(lambda theme="cream", *a, **k: seen.append(theme) or real(theme, *a, **k)))
    for kind in ("panchang", "muhurta", "festival", "transit"):
        seen.clear()
        daily, muh = _real_post_payload(kind)
        PromoStudio(tmp_path / kind).generate(daily, muhurta_data=muh)
        assert seen == [{"panchang": "sunrise", "muhurta": "solar", "festival": "festival_glow", "transit": "cream"}[kind]]

    assert art.mode == "RGBA" and art.width == 300
    alpha = art.getchannel("A"); assert alpha.getextrema()[0] == 0 and alpha.getextrema()[1] == 255

FILE: .\assets\tools\build_ornaments.py
================================================================================
"""DEV TOOL: regenerate SVG ornament sources and rasterise them to committed PNGs.

    pip install -r requirements-dev.txt
    python tools/build_ornaments.py

Not imported by the runtime. Re-run, review the PNGs, then commit both SVG and PNG.
"""
import math, pathlib
import cairosvg

OUT = pathlib.Path(__file__).parent.parent / "promo_studio" / "assets" / "ornaments"
SRC = OUT / "src"; SRC.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
GOLD = "#D9B86C"; GOLD_DEEP = "#B88A3B"
N = 1024; C = N / 2


def f(x): return f"{x:.2f}"


def mandala(petals=24):
    parts = [f'<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 {N} {N}" width="{N}" height="{N}" fill="none">']
    for r, w, col in ((500, 5, GOLD_DEEP), (470, 2, GOLD), (390, 3, GOLD), (300, 2, GOLD), (160, 3, GOLD_DEEP), (60, 4, GOLD)):
        parts.append(f'<circle cx="{C}" cy="{C}" r="{r}" stroke="{col}" stroke-width="{w}"/>')
    for ring, (length, width, col) in enumerate(((150, 56, GOLD), (110, 38, GOLD_DEEP))):
        n = petals if ring == 0 else petals // 2
        r0 = 305 if ring == 0 else 170
        for i in range(n):
            a = 360.0 * i / n + (0 if ring == 0 else 180.0 / n)
            parts.append(
                f'<path transform="rotate({f(a)} {C} {C}) translate({C} {f(C - r0)})" '
                f'd="M0 0 C {f(width/2)} {f(-length*0.35)} {f(width/2)} {f(-length*0.75)} 0 {f(-length)} '
                f'C {f(-width/2)} {f(-length*0.75)} {f(-width/2)} {f(-length*0.35)} 0 0 Z" '
                f'stroke="{col}" stroke-width="3"/>')
    for i in range(petals):
        a = math.tau * i / petals
        x, y = C + 470 * math.cos(a), C + 470 * math.sin(a)
        parts.append(f'<circle cx="{f(x)}" cy="{f(y)}" r="7" fill="{GOLD}"/>')
    parts.append("</svg>")
    return "\n".join(parts)


def main():
    (SRC / "mandala_full.svg").write_text(mandala(), encoding="utf-8")
    for svg in sorted(SRC.glob("*.svg")):
        cairosvg.svg2png(url=str(svg), write_to=str(OUT / f"{svg.stem}.png"), output_width=N, output_height=N)
        print("rendered", svg.stem)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# P0.1 Siddhanta Workflow Binding + Graphics Update

Promo Studio remains downstream of Siddhanta. The Panchang workflow is not modified.

## P0.1 binding fixes

1. `muhurta_data.date` must exactly match `today.date`; stale windows raise `DataError` and no image is written.
2. `metadata.location.name` is bound into `PostSpec.facts` and time-based posts render `Times for <location>`.
3. Rahu/Ketu motion is normalized to `Retrograde` / `वक्री` in the adapter. The upstream Direct flag is never exposed to captions or graphics.
4. Siddhanta-shaped payloads missing `today.english` fail with a specific `not a Siddhanta payload` error.

Real JSON fixtures live under `tests/fixtures/`, so tests no longer depend on `/mnt/data`.

## Graphics foundation

The renderer now has reusable components under `promo_studio/graphics/components.py`:

- Header / Footer
- MetricCard
- TimelineBar (sunrise → sunset with Abhijit/Rahu bands)
- ChoghadiyaTimeline (8 daytime slots)
- FestivalHero

The old `draw.py` remains a compatibility re-export.

Devanagari fonts are bundled in `assets/fonts/`, and CI verifies Pillow `raqm` support.
A real Siddhanta brand mark can be dropped into `assets/brand/siddhanta_mark.png`.
Festival artwork is resolved only through the explicit allowlist in `festival_assets.py`.

## Post design direction

- Panchang: metric grid + daylight timeline.
- Muhurta: Abhijit/Rahu cards + eight-slot Choghadiya timeline.
- Festival: local allowlisted FestivalHero + compact facts.
- Transit: dynamic featured transit selection; no misleading generic Transit Period card.

No AI image generation is used.

"""Example adapter boundary; replace `call_gemini` with your existing client."""

from promo_studio import PromoStudio
from promo_studio.caption import CaptionInput

def build_caption_request(result: dict) -> dict:
    """Return exactly the two fields allowed across the AI boundary."""
    return {
        "system": result["caption_system_prompt"],
        "input": result["caption_input"],
    }

# P0.1 Graphics Design Pass

## P0 safety
- Festival artwork is rejected safely when corrupt, oversized, or too large to decode.
- Asset limits are checked before full pixel decode: 40M pixels and 15 MB.
- Timeline labels are positioned above the daylight bar to avoid sunset/end-cap collisions.
- No production artwork uses text below 20 px.

## Design tokens
- Title: 64 px
- Hero/value: 44 px
- Body: 28 px
- Label: 22 px
- Minimum: 20 px

## Components
- Editorial header/footer
- Metric cards with larger values
- Sunrise/sunset timeline with non-overlapping labels
- Vertical Choghadiya list showing the best 3–4 daytime windows
- Festival hero with a gradient lower scrim
- Explicit image passing into FestivalHero; no Pillow private `_image` access

## Fonts
Production uses bundled Noto Sans and Noto Serif for Latin plus Noto Sans/Serif Devanagari. Noto font licensing text is bundled under `promo_studio/assets/fonts/licenses/`.

The graphics layer now exercises Devanagari in the Panchang, Muhurta, Festival and Transit headers. CI continues to require Pillow `raqm` support.

## Location
The raw metadata location remains available in facts, while `location_display` is used for human-facing artwork. If upstream supplies `metadata.location.display_name`, it wins; otherwise the city portion is used.




================================================================================
FILE: .\assets\generate_post.py
================================================================================
import argparse, json, sys
from promo_studio import PromoStudio, DataError

ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(); ap.add_argument("payload"); ap.add_argument("--out", default="output")
ap.add_argument("--no-overwrite", action="store_true"); a = ap.parse_args()
try:
    with open(a.payload, encoding="utf-8") as f: payload = json.load(f)
    print(json.dumps(PromoStudio(a.out).generate(payload, overwrite=not a.no_overwrite), ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
except json.JSONDecodeError as e: sys.exit(f"malformed JSON: {e}")
except DataError as e:            sys.exit(f"REFUSING TO POST - bad upstream data: {e}")   # exit code != 0 stops the scheduler




================================================================================
FILE: .\assets\GRAPHICS_STACK.md
================================================================================
# Graphics stack: what was adopted from the proposal, and how

| Proposal | Decision | Why |
|---|---|---|
| Pillow as core renderer | **Kept** | Already the renderer; text always goes through Pillow + raqm so Devanagari shaping is uniform. |
| NumPy procedural backgrounds | **Adopted** (`graphics/background.py`) | Themes: `cream` (flat, = v3), `sunrise` (Panchang), `solar` (Muhurta), `festival_glow` (Festival). Exact arithmetic only (no sin/cos/exp, no RNG) so hashes are stable; verified with SIMD paths disabled. |
| SVG ornaments via CairoSVG | **Adapted** | SVG sources in `assets/ornaments/src/`, rasterised **offline** by `tools/build_ornaments.py` into committed PNGs. Runtime never touches Cairo. First ornament: `mandala_full` (festival fallback hero). |
| svgwrite | Skipped | The tool writes SVG strings directly; fewer dependencies. |
| aggdraw | Skipped | Pillow supersampling is enough; unverified wheel support for Pillow 12.3. |
| Matplotlib | Skipped for now | Font/AA drift between versions would break golden hashes. Draw the few charts in Pillow if needed. |
| OpenCV | Skipped | No current need. |
| Curated deity artwork | Already supported | `festival_assets.py` allowlist + size/pixel limits. Add only images you have rights to. |

## Adding a background theme
Add a `Theme(...)` to `THEMES`, map a post to it in `posts/<kind>.py` (`canvas("theme")`), keep
it light, then add its hash to `GOLDEN_BACKGROUND_HASHES`. Tests enforce text contrast on the darkest pixel.

## Adding an ornament
Add the generator/SVG in `tools/build_ornaments.py`, run it, review the PNG, commit SVG + PNG.
Names must match `[a-z0-9_]+`.

# Reproducible rendering stack

Release golden images are pinned to (asserted by the test suite):

- Pillow 12.3.0 · FreeType 2.14.3 · libraqm 0.10.5
- NumPy 2.4.4 (procedural backgrounds)

**Runtime needs only Pillow + NumPy.** CairoSVG / libcairo is a *development-only* tool
(`requirements-dev.txt`) used by `tools/build_ornaments.py` to rasterise SVG ornaments into
committed PNGs. A test asserts it is never imported by the pipeline.

If you intentionally change any pinned component, regenerate and review the golden hashes.

"""Production binding example for the real Siddhanta workflow outputs."""
import json
from pathlib import Path
from promo_studio import PromoStudio

root = Path(__file__).parent
with open(root / "daily_panchang.json", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    daily = json.load(f)
with open(root / "muhurta_data.json", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    muhurta = json.load(f)

result = PromoStudio(root / "output").generate(daily, muhurta_data=muhurta)
print(result)

## Fixed before graphics

- Muhurta uses the eight-slot daytime Choghadiya timeline.

`37 passed` with the supplied Siddhanta fixtures.

# v3.1 — P0 fixes and P1 items 3–4

## P0
1. **Caption hand-off no longer crashes on JSON.** `caption.py` and `pipeline.py` now build
   facts with `spec.to_dict()["facts"]` (fully thawed: plain dict/list/str). `CaptionInput`
   additionally freezes its own copy, and `to_dict()` returns a fresh JSON-safe copy each call,
   so the hand-off object cannot be mutated through a returned dict.
2. **Two-lane timeline.**
   - Each lane has its own label row directly above its bar: dark text plus a colour swatch.
   - The card sizes itself from the number of lanes (`timeline_height()`); `timeline_bar()`
     returns its bottom edge and grows rather than overflows. Sunrise/sunset stay inside the card.
   - Two windows share a lane only if neither their bars *nor their labels* collide.
   - **Latent v3 bug fixed on the way:** band labels on a maroon band were drawn white on the
     white card, so "Rahu Kaal" was invisible even in the normal one-lane Panchang post.
     The Panchang golden hash changes for that reason.

## P1
3. `choghadiya_policy` is no longer a caption fact. The sentence lives in code as
   `posts/muhurta.py::CHOGHADIYA_POLICY`. Only `choghadiya_excluded_overlapping_rahu` is passed
   when slots were dropped.
4. When every auspicious slot overlaps Rahu Kaal, the Choghadiya card is not drawn and the
   remaining cards use the freed space. `choghadiya_list()` returns `False` and draws nothing
   for an empty list. If nothing Muhurta-specific is left at all (no Abhijit, no Rahu, no slot),
   the selector falls back to Panchang.

## Tests (49 → 70)
- JSON round-trip of `caption_input` and `caption_facts` for **every** post type.
- Overlap exclusion, boundary-touching slots, policy never reaching the caption writer.
- All-excluded Muhurta, empty Choghadiya list, Muhurta -> Panchang fallback.
- Lane planning, card growth, sunrise/sunset inside the card, label ink present for every band,
  two-lane Panchang fits above the footer.
- Golden hashes: Panchang updated; two new edge-layout hashes (Panchang overlap, Muhurta all-excluded).
- Existing pins (Pillow 12.3.0 / FreeType 2.14.3 / libraqm 0.10.5) unchanged.

## Not done in this pass
P1 #5 (long festival names), P2 #8 (Festival whitespace), P2 #9 (Hindi strings — needs a native speaker).
Muhurta all-excluded layout still leaves blank space below the cards.

# v3.2 — procedural backgrounds + pre-rendered SVG ornaments

- New: `graphics/background.py` (BackgroundEngine), `graphics/ornaments.py`, `tools/build_ornaments.py`,
  `assets/ornaments/` (SVG source + committed PNG), `requirements-dev.txt`, `GRAPHICS_STACK.md`.
- Panchang -> `sunrise`, Muhurta -> `solar`, Festival -> `festival_glow`; Transit/Kundli/Chakra stay flat cream.
- Festival fallback hero uses the vector mandala instead of Pillow line-art.
- Footer right-hand text now uses `MUTED_DEEP` (#5E554D): the old muted tone fell to 3.5:1 on the warm
  backgrounds. This changes the footer on **all six** posts, so all six golden hashes were regenerated.
- NumPy pinned (2.4.4). CairoSVG is dev-only.
- Tests 70 -> 94: determinism, per-theme golden hashes, contrast floors, theme mapping, ornament safety
  (no path traversal), SVG/PNG sync, CairoSVG-not-imported, NumPy pin.

Known limits: hash stability verified on x86-64 only (SIMD paths disabled to simulate other CPUs); not tried
on ARM. The footer divider line is faint on the warm backgrounds. Items still open: festival long names (P1 #5),
Festival whitespace (P2 #8), Hindi string review (P2 #9).




================================================================================
FILE: .\automation\ai_blueprint.py
================================================================================
"""Gemini AI Copywriter & Composer for Siddhanta.

Acts strictly as Composer & Writer:
- Crafts concise, culturally rich, authentic Vedic captions tailored to the
  *single selected post type* (Panchang, Plan Day, Festival, Kundli, Chakra, or Transit)
- Generates 8-12 high-intent, targeted hashtags
- Does NOT act as designer or invent astrological claims
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from promo_studio.ai.gemini import GeminiProvider
from promo_studio.automation.panchang_engine import DailyPanchang

SYSTEM_PROMPT = """You are a reverent, knowledgeable Vedic astrology copywriter for 'Siddhanta', a modern Panchang and Kundli app rooted in authentic tradition.
Rules:
- Act strictly as a Composer and Writer: write an engaging, concise Instagram caption tailored specifically to the post type requested.
- Honor the sacred Vedic tradition with genuine cultural warmth and clarity.
- Never make superstitious fear-based claims, medical guarantees, or false promises.
- Frame astrological advice as mindfulness, spiritual reflection, and mindful timing (Muhurta).
- Keep the caption focused (under 220 words) with clean Devanagari Hindi and English sub-labels.
- Include 8-12 high-intent hashtags in Hindi and English.
- Return ONLY valid JSON matching the schema provided.
"""

def generate_cosmic_caption(
    p: DailyPanchang,
    gemini: GeminiProvider,
    api_key: str = "",
    post_type: str = "panchang"
) -> dict:
    """Prompts Gemini to draft the daily caption and hashtags tailored to post_type."""
    
    context = {
        "post_type": post_type,
        "date": p.date_formatted,
        "vara": f"{p.vara_sa} ({p.vara_en})",
        "deity": p.deity,
        "tithi": f"{p.tithi_name} ({p.tithi_name_hi})",
        "paksha": p.paksha,
        "nakshatra": f"{p.nakshatra_name} ({p.nakshatra_name_hi})",
        "moon_rashi": p.moon_rashi,
        "sunrise": p.sunrise_str,
        "sunset": p.sunset_str,
        "abhijit_muhurta": p.abhijit_muhurta,
        "rahu_kaal": p.rahu_kaal,
        "is_festival": p.is_festival,
        "festival_name": p.festival_name,
        "festival_note": p.festival_note,
        "daily_remedy": p.remedy,
    }

    user_prompt = f"""
Write an Instagram feed post caption for the following post type: '{post_type.upper()}'.
Astrological & Vedic context:
{json.dumps(context, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)}

Content Focus based on post_type:
- If 'festival': Celebrate {p.festival_name or 'the sacred festival'}, explain the spiritual significance ({p.festival_note}), puja muhurta, and fasting rules.
- If 'plan_day': Highlight mindful timing today: Abhijit Muhurta ({p.abhijit_muhurta}), Rahu Kaal ({p.rahu_kaal}), and Choghadiya principles.
- If 'kundli': Focus on how the birth chart (D1/D9), planetary positions, and active Vimshottari Dasha illuminate one's life purpose.
- If 'chakra': Discuss the seven energy centers, Jyotish balance, and inner alignment through pranayama and meditation.
- If 'jyotish': Highlight current planetary movements and transit wisdom for intellectual expansion and mindfulness.
- If 'panchang': Provide a clean, devotional overview of today's Tithi, Nakshatra, and Sun/Moon vitality.

Include:
1. Inspiring Devotional Opening
2. Key Vedic insights (2-3 bullet points)
3. Gentle call-to-action inviting users to explore in the Siddhanta app
4. 8-12 relevant hashtags (mix of Hindi & English)

Output JSON schema:
{{
  "instagram_caption": "Caption text with tasteful formatting and emojis...",
  "hashtags": ["Panchang", "SiddhantaApp", "VedicAstrology"]
}}
"""

    raw_json = None
    try:
        raw_json = gemini.generate(SYSTEM_PROMPT, user_prompt, temperature=0.7)
    except Exception:
        fallback_models = ["gemini-flash-latest", "gemini-flash-lite-latest", "gemini-2.5-flash"]
        for fb_model in fallback_models:
            if gemini.model != fb_model and api_key:
                try:
                    fb_provider = GeminiProvider(api_key, fb_model)
                    raw_json = fb_provider.generate(SYSTEM_PROMPT, user_prompt, temperature=0.7)
                    if raw_json:
                        break
                except Exception:
                    continue

    if raw_json:
        try:
            clean = raw_json.strip()
            if clean.startswith("```json"):
                clean = clean[7:]
            if clean.endswith("```"):
                clean = clean[:-3]
            parsed = json.loads(clean.strip())
            if isinstance(parsed, dict) and "instagram_caption" in parsed:
                return parsed
        except Exception:
            pass

    return get_devotional_fallback_copy(p, post_type=post_type)


def get_devotional_fallback_copy(p: DailyPanchang, post_type: str = "panchang") -> dict:
    """Returns a focused fallback caption tailored to post_type."""
    if post_type == "festival" or (p.is_festival and p.festival_name):
        fest_name = p.festival_name or "Jivitputrika Vrat"
        clean_name = fest_name.split("(")[0].strip().replace(" ", "")
        caption = (
            f"🕉️ {fest_name} • {p.date_formatted}\n\n"
            f"हार्दिक शुभकामनाएं एवं पावन बधाई।\n\n"
            f"✨ तिथि: {p.tithi_name_hi} ({p.tithi_name})\n"
            f"⭐ नक्षत्र: {p.nakshatra_name_hi} ({p.nakshatra_name})\n"
            f"🌟 शुभ मुहूर्त: {p.abhijit_muhurta}\n\n"
            f"📜 {p.festival_note or 'पावन पर्व की पूजा एवं संकल्प विधि का पालन करें।'}\n\n"
            f"विस्तृत मुहूर्त और व्रत कथा के लिए Siddhanta ऐप देखें। (Link in bio)"
        )
        tags = [clean_name, "VedicFestival", "HinduFestival", "Panchang", "SiddhantaApp", "Vrat", "Muhurta", "सनातन_धर्म"]
        return {"instagram_caption": caption, "hashtags": tags}

    elif post_type == "plan_day":
        caption = (
            f"⏳ Plan Your Day Wisely • {p.date_formatted}\n\n"
            f"महत्वपूर्ण कार्यों के लिए आज के वैदिक समय:\n\n"
            f"🌟 अभिजीत मुहूर्त: {p.abhijit_muhurta} (शुभ कार्यों की शुरुआत हेतु श्रेष्ठ)\n"
            f"⚠️ राहुकाल: {p.rahu_kaal} (नए कार्य एवं महत्वपूर्ण निर्णय टालें)\n"
            f"☀️ चौघड़िया: शुभ वेलाओं का सदुपयोग करें\n\n"
            f"दैनिक चौघड़िया और संपूर्ण मुहूर्त चक्र के लिए Siddhanta ऐप डाउनलोड करें। (Link in bio)"
        )
        tags = ["Muhurta", "AbhijitMuhurta", "RahuKaal", "Choghadiya", "SiddhantaApp", "VedicAstrology", "मुहूर्त", "पंचांग"]
        return {"instagram_caption": caption, "hashtags": tags}

    elif post_type == "kundli":
        caption = (
            f"📜 Your Kundli & Karmic Map • {p.date_formatted}\n\n"
            f"जन्म कुंडली केवल भविष्य की भविष्यवाणी नहीं, बल्कि आपकी चेतना और कर्म का दर्पण है।\n\n"
            f"🔹 D1 लग्न चक्र: आपकी शारीरिक संरचना और जीवन दिशा\n"
            f"🔹 D9 नवांश: आत्मिक सामर्थ्य और भाग्य की गहराई\n"
            f"🔹 विंशोत्तरी महादशा: जीवन के विभिन्न सोपान और समयावधि\n\n"
            f"अपनी सटीक वैदिक जन्म कुंडली और दशा विश्लेषण के लिए Siddhanta ऐप का अनुभव करें। (Link in bio)"
        )
        tags = ["Kundli", "VedicAstrology", "BirthChart", "Lagna", "Dashas", "SiddhantaApp", "कुंडली", "ज्योतिष"]
        return {"instagram_caption": caption, "hashtags": tags}

    elif post_type == "chakra":
        caption = (
            f"🌿 Balance Your Inner Energy • {p.date_formatted}\n\n"
            f"सात चक्र हमारे सूक्ष्म शरीर के ऊर्जा केंद्र हैं। जब चक्र संतुलित होते हैं, तो विचार, प्राण और स्वास्थ्य में सहज सामंजस्य स्थापित होता है।\n\n"
            f"✨ मूलाधार से सहस्रार तक — ध्यान और प्राणायाम द्वारा अपनी आंतरिक ऊर्जा को जागृत करें।\n\n"
            f"ज्योतिष और चक्र सामंजस्य की जानकारी के लिए Siddhanta ऐप देखें। (Link in bio)"
        )
        tags = ["ChakraBalance", "SevenChakras", "Meditation", "Pranayama", "InnerPeace", "SiddhantaApp", "Kundalini", "VedicWisdom"]
        return {"instagram_caption": caption, "hashtags": tags}

    elif post_type == "jyotish":
        caption = (
            f"🪐 Jyotish Transit Insight • {p.date_formatted}\n\n"
            f"ग्रह गोचर हमें समय के स्वभाव और ब्रह्मांडीय लय को समझने में सहायता करता है।\n\n"
            f"✨ बृहस्पति (गुरु) का मिथुन राशि में संचरण ज्ञान, संवाद और बौद्धिक विस्तार का सुंदर अवसर लाता है।\n"
            f"🌟 अध्ययन, शोध और सकारात्मक चिंतन हेतु यह समय विशेष रूप से फलदायी है।\n\n"
            f"अपने लग्न के अनुसार सटीक गोचर प्रभाव जानने के लिए Siddhanta ऐप देखें। (Link in bio)"
        )
        tags = ["TransitAstrology", "JupiterTransit", "GuruGochar", "VedicAstrology", "SiddhantaApp", "Jyotish", "ग्रहगोचर"]
        return {"instagram_caption": caption, "hashtags": tags}

    else:  # default panchang
        caption = (
            f"🕉️ Today's Panchang • {p.date_formatted}\n\n"
            f"आज {p.vara_hi} ({p.vara_en}) है, {p.deity_hi} की आराधना का दिन।\n\n"
            f"✨ तिथि: {p.tithi_name_hi} ({p.tithi_name})\n"
            f"⭐ नक्षत्र: {p.nakshatra_name_hi} ({p.nakshatra_name})\n"
            f"🌟 अभिजीत मुहूर्त: {p.abhijit_muhurta}\n"
            f"⚠️ राहुकाल: {p.rahu_kaal}\n\n"
            f"☀️ सूर्योदय: {p.sunrise_str} | सूर्यास्त: {p.sunset_str}\n"
            f"🙏 दैनिक संकल्प: {p.remedy}\n\n"
            f"सटीक दैनिक पंचांग और मुहूर्त के लिए Siddhanta ऐप का उपयोग करें। (Link in bio)"
        )
        tags = ["Panchang", "DailyPanchang", "VedicAstrology", "Tithi", "Nakshatra", "SiddhantaApp", "पंचांग", "शुभमुहूर्त"]
        return {"instagram_caption": caption, "hashtags": tags}




================================================================================

"""High-Precision Template Renderer for Siddhanta Social Graphics.

Directly composites daily dynamic astronomical data onto Siddhanta's
official master background templates matching the user's agency mockups:
- Slide 1: Panchang Temple Riverside Template ("Today's Panchang")
- Slide 2: Golden Temple Sunrise with Diyas and Lotuses ("Plan Your Day Wisely")
- Slide 3: Celestial Temple Astrology Poster Template ("Jyotish Insight / Planetary Transits")
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont

from promo_studio.automation.panchang_engine import DailyPanchang

ASSETS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "assets"
TEMPLATES_DIR = ASSETS_DIR / "templates"
FONTS_DIR = ASSETS_DIR / "fonts"

# ── Color Palette Matching Official Mockups ──────────────────────────────────
COLOR_CRIMSON = (122, 20, 20)          # #7A1414 (Headers & Primary Values)
COLOR_GOLD_TEXT = (180, 115, 30)       # #B4731E (Jyotish Panchang subtitle)
COLOR_DARK = (30, 25, 22)              # #1E1916 (Timings & Primary Dark Text)
COLOR_MUTED = (112, 102, 94)           # #70665E (Labels & Subtitles)
COLOR_CARD_BG = (255, 253, 248, 230)   # Translucent cream card
COLOR_CARD_BORDER = (235, 222, 202)
COLOR_GREEN_TITLE = (36, 105, 68)      # #246944 (Abhijit Muhurta)
COLOR_GREEN_CIRCLE = (220, 245, 230)
COLOR_RED_TITLE = (169, 50, 38)        # #A93226 (Rahu Kaal)
COLOR_RED_CIRCLE = (253, 230, 230)
COLOR_AMBER_TITLE = (183, 121, 31)     # #B7791F (Choghadiya)
COLOR_AMBER_CIRCLE = (254, 243, 220)
COLOR_BTN_RED = (122, 20, 20)          # Bottom action button
WHITE = (255, 255, 255)


def _load_font(name: str, size: int) -> ImageFont.ImageFont:
    p = FONTS_DIR / name
    if p.exists():
        try:
            return ImageFont.truetype(str(p), size)
        except Exception:
            pass
    return ImageFont.load_default()


def _font_serif(size: int, bold: bool = True) -> ImageFont.ImageFont:
    return _load_font("LibreBaskerville-Bold.ttf" if bold else "LibreBaskerville-Regular.ttf", size)


def _font_sans(size: int, bold: bool = True) -> ImageFont.ImageFont:
    return _load_font("DMSans-Bold.ttf" if bold else "DMSans-Medium.ttf", size)


def _font_hindi(size: int) -> ImageFont.ImageFont:
    return _load_font("NotoSansDevanagari-Regular.ttf", size)


def _draw_brand_header(canvas: Image.Image, draw: ImageDraw.ImageDraw, x: int = 75, y: int = 55) -> None:
    """Draws the official Siddhanta Jyotish Panchang logo & wordmark."""
    logo_path = ASSETS_DIR / "logo.png"
    if logo_path.exists():
        try:
            logo = Image.open(logo_path).convert("RGBA")
            logo = logo.resize((72, 72), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)
            canvas.alpha_composite(logo, (x, y))
        except Exception:
            pass

    font_title = _font_serif(34, bold=True)
    font_sub = _font_serif(20, bold=False)
    
    text_x = x + 90
    draw.text((text_x, y + 4), "Siddhanta", fill=COLOR_CRIMSON, font=font_title)
    draw.text((text_x, y + 42), "Jyotish Panchang", fill=COLOR_GOLD_TEXT, font=font_sub)


# ── Illustration Compositing Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────

def _paste_badge_icon(
    canvas: Image.Image,
    icon_rel_path: str,
    box: tuple[int, int, int, int],
    bg_color: tuple[int, int, int] | tuple[int, int, int, int],
    padding: int = 8,
) -> bool:
    """Renders a circular badge with background color and the illustration centered inside."""
    x1, y1, x2, y2 = box
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(canvas)
    draw.ellipse(box, fill=bg_color)

    p = ASSETS_DIR / "illustrations" / icon_rel_path
    if not p.exists():
        return False

    try:
        icon = Image.open(p).convert("RGBA")
        inner_w = (x2 - x1) - 2 * padding
        inner_h = (y2 - y1) - 2 * padding
        w, h = icon.size
        scale = min(inner_w / w, inner_h / h)
        nw, nh = max(1, int(w * scale)), max(1, int(h * scale))
        icon = icon.resize((nw, nh), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)

        corner_r, corner_g, corner_b, _ = icon.getpixel((0, 0))
        if corner_r > 190 and corner_g > 190 and corner_b > 170:
            getter = getattr(icon, "get_flattened_data", icon.getdata)
            datas = list(getter())
            tol_sq = 35 * 35
            new_data = []
            for r, g, b, a in datas:
                dist_sq = (r - corner_r) ** 2 + (g - corner_g) ** 2 + (b - corner_b) ** 2
                if dist_sq < tol_sq:
                    alpha = int(255 * (dist_sq / tol_sq))
                    new_data.append((r, g, b, min(a, alpha)))
                else:
                    new_data.append((r, g, b, a))
            icon.putdata(new_data)

        cx = (x1 + x2) // 2
        cy = (y1 + y2) // 2
        canvas.alpha_composite(icon, (cx - nw // 2, cy - nh // 2))
        return True
    except Exception:
        return False


# ── Card Renderers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def generate_slide_1_hook(p: DailyPanchang, output_path: str | Path) -> str:
    """Slide 1 (Today's Panchang): Matches Mockup 1 layout with pixel accuracy."""
    template_path = TEMPLATES_DIR / "panchang_template.png"
    canvas = Image.open(template_path).convert("RGBA")
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(canvas)

    # 1. Official Header
    _draw_brand_header(canvas, draw, x=75, y=55)

    # 2. Main Title: Today's Panchang
    draw.text((75, 175), "Today's", fill=COLOR_CRIMSON, font=_font_serif(64, bold=True))
    draw.text((75, 248), "Panchang", fill=COLOR_CRIMSON, font=_font_serif(64, bold=True))

    # Date header
    day_str = p.date.strftime("%d %B %Y").upper()
    draw.text((75, 338), day_str, fill=COLOR_DARK, font=_font_sans(24, bold=True))
    
    paksha_str = f"{p.vara_en.upper()}  •  {p.paksha.upper()} PAKSHA"
    draw.text((75, 372), paksha_str, fill=COLOR_MUTED, font=_font_sans(19, bold=False))

    # 3. Card 1: TITHI
    c1_y = 430
    c_w, c_h = 560, 115
    draw.rounded_rectangle([75, c1_y, 75 + c_w, c1_y + c_h], radius=18, fill=COLOR_CARD_BG, outline=COLOR_CARD_BORDER, width=1)
    
    # Official Siddhanta Moon illustration badge
    _paste_badge_icon(canvas, "icons/Moon.webp", (95, c1_y + 22, 165, c1_y + 92), COLOR_AMBER_CIRCLE, padding=12)
    
    draw.text((185, c1_y + 20), "Tithi", fill=COLOR_MUTED, font=_font_sans(17))
    draw.text((185, c1_y + 44), p.tithi_name, fill=COLOR_CRIMSON, font=_font_serif(26, bold=True))
    draw.text((185, c1_y + 80), f"{p.tithi_name_hi}", fill=COLOR_MUTED, font=_font_hindi(18))

    # 4. Card 2: NAKSHATRA
    c2_y = c1_y + 135
    draw.rounded_rectangle([75, c2_y, 75 + c_w, c2_y + c_h], radius=18, fill=COLOR_CARD_BG, outline=COLOR_CARD_BORDER, width=1)
    
    # Official Siddhanta Nakshatra star illustration badge
    _paste_badge_icon(canvas, "icons/nakshatra.webp", (95, c2_y + 22, 165, c2_y + 92), COLOR_AMBER_CIRCLE, padding=6)
    
    draw.text((185, c2_y + 20), "Nakshatra", fill=COLOR_MUTED, font=_font_sans(17))
    draw.text((185, c2_y + 44), p.nakshatra_name, fill=COLOR_CRIMSON, font=_font_serif(26, bold=True))
    draw.text((185, c2_y + 80), p.nakshatra_name_hi, fill=COLOR_MUTED, font=_font_hindi(18))

    # 5. Sunrise & Sunset Bottom Strip with Official Siddhanta Icons
    sun_y = 1255
    strip_w = 440
    draw.rounded_rectangle([75, sun_y, 75 + strip_w, sun_y + 75], radius=16, fill=(255, 253, 248, 235), outline=COLOR_CARD_BORDER, width=1)
    
    _paste_badge_icon(canvas, "icons/Sunrise.webp", (90, sun_y + 12, 142, sun_y + 64), COLOR_AMBER_CIRCLE, padding=6)
    draw.text((152, sun_y + 16), "Sunrise", fill=COLOR_MUTED, font=_font_sans(15))
    draw.text((152, sun_y + 38), p.sunrise_str, fill=COLOR_DARK, font=_font_sans(20, bold=True))
    
    draw.line([250, sun_y + 15, 250, sun_y + 60], fill=(225, 210, 190), width=1)

    _paste_badge_icon(canvas, "icons/Sunset.webp", (265, sun_y + 12, 317, sun_y + 64), COLOR_AMBER_CIRCLE, padding=6)
    draw.text((327, sun_y + 16), "Sunset", fill=COLOR_MUTED, font=_font_sans(15))
    draw.text((327, sun_y + 38), p.sunset_str, fill=COLOR_DARK, font=_font_sans(20, bold=True))

    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(output_path), exist_ok=True)
    canvas.save(output_path, "PNG")
    return str(output_path)

================================================================================
FILE: .\automation\card_universe.py

"""Siddhanta 6-Card Universe Generator.

Pixel-accurate rendering engine calibrated strictly for Instagram Feed 4:5 portrait (1080 x 1350)
incorporating all agency editorial rules & brand consistency:

Brand Standards:
- Dimensions: CARD_W = 1080, CARD_H = 1350 (4:5 aspect ratio)
- Typography: Maroon serif headlines, dark charcoal body, gold accents
- Frame: Subtle ornamental corner brackets, consistent top brand header & divider (─── ◆ ───)
- Background: Cream-first palette, utilizing rule-based empty background templates where available
- CTA: Standardized restrained editorial footer pill across every card
- Data Authority: No invented marketing claims or hardcoded festival dates
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont

from promo_studio.automation.panchang_engine import DailyPanchang
from promo_studio.automation.template_selector import select_template_for_content

ASSETS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "assets"
TEMPLATES_DIR = ASSETS_DIR / "templates"
FONTS_DIR = ASSETS_DIR / "fonts"
ILLUST_DIR = ASSETS_DIR / "illustrations"
IMAGES_DIR = ASSETS_DIR / "images"

# Strict 4:5 Instagram Feed Dimensions
CARD_W = 1080
CARD_H = 1350

# Palette
COLOR_CRIMSON = (122, 20, 20)           # #7A1414 (Main Titles & Accents)
COLOR_GOLD = (180, 115, 30)             # #B4731E (Subtitles & Gold Accents)
COLOR_DARK = (30, 25, 22)               # #1E1916 (Timings & Headers)
COLOR_MUTED = (112, 102, 94)            # #70665E (Descriptions)
COLOR_CARD_BG = (255, 253, 248, 235)    # Translucent Cream
COLOR_BORDER = (235, 222, 202)          # Soft Cream Border
COLOR_GREEN = (30, 107, 63)             # #1E6B3F (Abhijit)
COLOR_RED = (138, 28, 20)               # #8A1C14 (Rahu)
COLOR_AMBER = (180, 115, 30)            # #B4731E (Choghadiya)
WHITE = (255, 255, 255)


def _font_serif(size: int, bold: bool = True, italic: bool = False) -> ImageFont.ImageFont:
    if italic:
        f = "LibreBaskerville-Italic.ttf"
    elif bold:
        f = "LibreBaskerville-Bold.ttf"
    else:
        f = "LibreBaskerville-Regular.ttf"
    p = FONTS_DIR / f
    if p.exists():
        try:
            return ImageFont.truetype(str(p), size)
        except Exception:
            pass
    return ImageFont.load_default()


def _font_sans(size: int, bold: bool = True) -> ImageFont.ImageFont:
    f = "DMSans-Bold.ttf" if bold else "DMSans-Medium.ttf"
    p = FONTS_DIR / f
    if p.exists():
        try:
            return ImageFont.truetype(str(p), size)
        except Exception:
            pass
    return ImageFont.load_default()


def _font_hindi(size: int) -> ImageFont.ImageFont:
    p = FONTS_DIR / "NotoSansDevanagari-Regular.ttf"
    if p.exists():
        try:
            return ImageFont.truetype(str(p), size)
        except Exception:
            pass
    return ImageFont.load_default()


def _save_card_image(canvas: Image.Image, output_path: str | Path) -> str:
    """Saves canvas to disk with guaranteed format compatibility.
    
    If saving as .jpg / .jpeg, converts RGBA to RGB with clean cream background
    and quality=95, fulfilling Meta's strict Instagram Graph API image format policy.
    """
    out_p = Path(output_path)
    out_p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    ext = out_p.suffix.lower()
    if ext in (".jpg", ".jpeg"):
        # Convert RGBA to RGB over solid cream background
        rgb_canvas = Image.new("RGB", canvas.size, (255, 252, 246))
        if canvas.mode == "RGBA":
            rgb_canvas.paste(canvas, mask=canvas.split()[3])
        else:
            rgb_canvas.paste(canvas)
        rgb_canvas.save(out_p, "JPEG", quality=95, optimize=True)
    else:
        canvas.save(out_p, "PNG")

    return str(out_p)


def _clean_transparency(img: Image.Image) -> Image.Image:
    """Removes near-white/cream corner background for crisp overlay."""
    img = img.convert("RGBA")
    corner_r, corner_g, corner_b, _ = img.getpixel((0, 0))
    if corner_r > 190 and corner_g > 190 and corner_b > 170:
        getter = getattr(img, "get_flattened_data", img.getdata)
        datas = list(getter())
        tol_sq = 35 * 35
        new_data = []
        for r, g, b, a in datas:
            dist_sq = (r - corner_r) ** 2 + (g - corner_g) ** 2 + (b - corner_b) ** 2
            if dist_sq < tol_sq:
                alpha = int(255 * (dist_sq / tol_sq))
                new_data.append((r, g, b, min(a, alpha)))
            else:
                new_data.append((r, g, b, a))
        img.putdata(new_data)
    return img


def _paste_icon_badge(
    canvas: Image.Image,
    icon_path: Path,
    box: tuple[int, int, int, int],
    badge_fill: tuple[int, int, int] | tuple[int, int, int, int],
    padding: int = 12,
    badge_outline: tuple[int, int, int] | None = None,
) -> None:
    """Draws a circular badge and composites an illustration centered inside."""
    x1, y1, x2, y2 = box
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(canvas)
    draw.ellipse(box, fill=badge_fill, outline=badge_outline, width=2 if badge_outline else 0)

    if not icon_path.exists():
        return

    try:
        icon = Image.open(icon_path)
        icon = _clean_transparency(icon)
        inner_w = (x2 - x1) - 2 * padding
        inner_h = (y2 - y1) - 2 * padding
        w, h = icon.size
        scale = min(inner_w / w, inner_h / h)
        nw, nh = max(1, int(w * scale)), max(1, int(h * scale))
        icon = icon.resize((nw, nh), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)

        cx = (x1 + x2) // 2
        cy = (y1 + y2) // 2
        canvas.alpha_composite(icon, (cx - nw // 2, cy - nh // 2))
    except Exception:
        pass


def _draw_brand_header(canvas: Image.Image, draw: ImageDraw.ImageDraw, y: int = 42) -> None:
    """Draws the official Siddhanta Jyotish Panchang header with ornamental divider."""
    logo_path = ASSETS_DIR / "logo.png"
    if logo_path.exists():
        try:
            logo = Image.open(logo_path).convert("RGBA")
            logo = logo.resize((70, 70), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)
            canvas.alpha_composite(logo, (60, y))
        except Exception:
            pass

    font_title = _font_serif(34, bold=True)
    font_sub = _font_serif(20, bold=False)

    text_x = 60 + 85
    draw.text((text_x, y + 2), "Siddhanta", fill=COLOR_CRIMSON, font=font_title)
    draw.text((text_x, y + 40), "Jyotish Panchang", fill=COLOR_GOLD, font=font_sub)

    # Ornamental divider: ─── ◆ ───
    div_y = y + 84
    draw.line([60, div_y, 500, div_y], fill=(210, 180, 130, 140), width=2)
    draw.line([580, div_y, 1020, div_y], fill=(210, 180, 130, 140), width=2)
    # Diamond at center
    cx, cy = 540, div_y
    draw.polygon([(cx, cy - 6), (cx + 6, cy), (cx, cy + 6), (cx - 6, cy)], fill=COLOR_GOLD)


def _draw_card_frame(draw: ImageDraw.ImageDraw) -> None:
    """Draws subtle ornamental corner mandala brackets for 1080x1350 canvas."""
    c_color = (205, 175, 125, 120)
    d_color = COLOR_GOLD

    # Top-Left Bracket
    draw.line([(35, 55), (35, 30), (60, 30)], fill=c_color, width=2)
    draw.polygon([(35, 28), (37, 30), (35, 32), (33, 30)], fill=d_color)

    # Top-Right Bracket
    draw.line([(1045, 55), (1045, 30), (1020, 30)], fill=c_color, width=2)
    draw.polygon([(1045, 28), (1047, 30), (1045, 32), (1043, 30)], fill=d_color)

    # Bottom-Left Bracket
    draw.line([(35, 1295), (35, 1320), (60, 1320)], fill=c_color, width=2)
    draw.polygon([(35, 1318), (37, 1320), (35, 1322), (33, 1320)], fill=d_color)

    # Bottom-Right Bracket
    draw.line([(1045, 1295), (1045, 1320), (1020, 1320)], fill=c_color, width=2)
    draw.polygon([(1045, 1318), (1047, 1320), (1045, 1322), (1043, 1320)], fill=d_color)


def _draw_cta_pill(canvas: Image.Image, draw: ImageDraw.ImageDraw, text: str, y: int = 1245) -> None:
    """Draws an elegant, restrained editorial footer pill replacing giant billboard buttons."""
    pill_box = [60, y, 1020, y + 62]
    # Subtle cream background with warm maroon border and refined typography
    draw.rounded_rectangle(pill_box, radius=18, fill=(255, 250, 242, 230), outline=(190, 155, 115), width=2)

    # Left subtle icon
    ph_x, ph_y = 95, y + 17
    draw.rounded_rectangle([ph_x, ph_y, ph_x + 18, ph_y + 28], radius=4, outline=COLOR_CRIMSON, width=2)
    draw.line([ph_x + 6, ph_y + 21, ph_x + 12, ph_y + 21], fill=COLOR_CRIMSON, width=1)

    # Label & subtle arrow
    font_btn = _font_sans(22, bold=True)
    draw.text((540, y + 31), text, fill=COLOR_CRIMSON, font=font_btn, anchor="mm")
    draw.text((985, y + 30), "›", fill=COLOR_GOLD, font=_font_serif(30, bold=True), anchor="mm")


def _prepare_card_canvas(theme: str, festival_name: str = "") -> tuple[Image.Image, ImageDraw.ImageDraw]:
    """Prepares a 1080x1350 canvas with cream-first atmosphere and template selection."""
    canvas = Image.new("RGBA", (CARD_W, CARD_H), (255, 252, 246, 255))

    # Check for empty template from rule-based selector
    t_path = select_template_for_content(theme, festival_name=festival_name)
    if t_path and t_path.exists():
        try:
            t_img = Image.open(t_path).convert("RGBA")
            scale = max(CARD_W / t_img.width, CARD_H / t_img.height)
            nw, nh = int(t_img.width * scale), int(t_img.height * scale)
            t_img = t_img.resize((nw, nh), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)
            crop_x = (nw - CARD_W) // 2
            crop_y = (nh - CARD_H) // 2
            cropped = t_img.crop((crop_x, crop_y, crop_x + CARD_W, crop_y + CARD_H))
            
            # Subtle opacity blend so cream-first legibility is preserved
            alpha_val = 80 if theme in ("panchang", "plan_day") else 140
            cropped.putalpha(alpha_val)
            canvas.alpha_composite(cropped, (0, 0))
        except Exception:
            pass
    else:
        # Fallback gentle atmosphere gradients
        draw_bg = ImageDraw.Draw(canvas)
        if theme == "chakra":
            cx, cy = 350, 750
            for r in range(350, 40, -25):
                a = max(2, int(18 * (1.0 - r / 350.0)))
                draw_bg.ellipse([cx - r, cy - r, cx + r, cy + r], fill=(255, 235, 190, a))
        elif theme == "kundli":
            cx, cy = 540, 620
            for r in (240, 290, 340):
                draw_bg.ellipse([cx - r, cy - r, cx + r, cy + r], outline=(220, 195, 155, 40), width=1)
        elif theme == "transit":
            for x in range(600, CARD_W):
                alpha = int(140 * ((x - 600) / 480.0))
                draw_bg.line([(x, 0), (x, CARD_H)], fill=(20, 24, 45, alpha))

    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(canvas)
    _draw_card_frame(draw)
    return canvas, draw

# 1. DAILY PANCHANG (1080 x 1350)

        draw.text((100, m_y + 54), p.abhijit_muhurta, fill=COLOR_DARK, font=_font_sans(28, bold=True))
        draw.text((100, m_y + 90), "शुभ कार्यों की शुरुआत हेतु श्रेष्ठ", fill=COLOR_MUTED, font=_font_hindi(18))

        draw.line([540, m_y + 16, 540, m_y + 104], fill=(225, 210, 190), width=2)

        draw.text((580, m_y + 18), "अशुभ काल (राहुकाल)", fill=COLOR_RED, font=_font_hindi(21))
        draw.text((580, m_y + 54), p.rahu_kaal, fill=COLOR_DARK, font=_font_sans(28, bold=True))
        draw.text((580, m_y + 90), "नए कार्य व समझौते टालें", fill=COLOR_MUTED, font=_font_hindi(18))

        # Sunrise & Sunset Strip

        s_h = 100

        _paste_icon_badge(canvas, ILLUST_DIR / "icons" / "Sunrise.webp", (85, s_y + 16, 155, s_y + 84), badge_fill=(254, 243, 220), padding=8)
        draw.text((175, s_y + 18), "सूर्योदय", fill=COLOR_MUTED, font=_font_hindi(19))

        draw.line([540, s_y + 14, 540, s_y + 86], fill=(225, 210, 190), width=2)

        _paste_icon_badge(canvas, ILLUST_DIR / "icons" / "Sunset.webp", (565, s_y + 16, 635, s_y + 84), badge_fill=(254, 243, 220), padding=8)
        draw.text((655, s_y + 18), "सूर्यास्त", fill=COLOR_MUTED, font=_font_hindi(19))
        draw.text((655, s_y + 48), p.sunset_str, fill=COLOR_DARK, font=_font_sans(28, bold=True))

        # Daily Spiritual Focus Footer Pill
        sp_y = 805
        draw.rounded_rectangle([60, sp_y, 1020, sp_y + 95], radius=18, fill=(255, 250, 238), outline=(225, 195, 140), width=2)
        _paste_icon_badge(canvas, ILLUST_DIR / "icons" / "diya.webp", (85, sp_y + 16, 150, sp_y + 80), badge_fill=(254, 243, 220), padding=6)
        draw.text((170, sp_y + 14), "दैनिक आध्यात्मिक संकल्प", fill=COLOR_GOLD, font=_font_hindi(20))
        focus_text = p.remedy if p.remedy else "प्रातःकाल सूर्य देव को तांबे के लोटे से अर्घ्य दें।"

        draw.text((540, 1205), "“शुभ समय पर किया गया कर्म कल्याणकारी होता है।”", fill=COLOR_CRIMSON, font=_font_hindi(22), anchor="mm")
        _draw_cta_pill(canvas, draw, "सिद्धांत पंचांग ऐप में देखें", y=1245)

        return _save_card_image(canvas, output_path)

    # Title & Sub-header (English default)
    draw.text((60, 160), "Today's Panchang", fill=COLOR_CRIMSON, font=_font_serif(56, bold=True))

    # Standardized bottom CTA pill

    return _save_card_image(canvas, output_path)

# 2. PLAN YOUR DAY (1080 x 1350)
# ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
def render_card_2_plan_day(p: DailyPanchang, output_path: str | Path) -> str:
    canvas, draw = _prepare_card_canvas("plan_day")
    _draw_brand_header(canvas, draw, y=42)

    # Title & Sub-header

    # 3 Cards — height 155px each, cleanly spaced
    c_h = 155

    # Card 1: Abhijit Muhurta
    c1_y = 295
    draw.rounded_rectangle([60, c1_y, 1020, c1_y + c_h], radius=18, fill=COLOR_CARD_BG, outline=(50, 130, 80), width=2)

    draw.text((225, c1_y + 104), "Midday solar window auspicious for important beginnings.", fill=COLOR_MUTED, font=_font_sans(19))

    # Card 2: Rahu Kaal
    c2_y = 475
