    draw.text((225, c2_y + 22), "Rahu Kaal", fill=COLOR_RED, font=_font_sans(24, bold=True))
    draw.text((225, c2_y + 58), p.rahu_kaal, fill=COLOR_DARK, font=_font_sans(30, bold=True))
    draw.text((225, c2_y + 104), "Inauspicious period — avoid major commitments or travel start.", fill=COLOR_MUTED, font=_font_sans(19))

    # Card 3: Choghadiya - Data-driven from authentic dump
    c3_y = 655
    draw.rounded_rectangle([60, c3_y, 1020, c3_y + c_h], radius=18, fill=COLOR_CARD_BG, outline=(200, 140, 45), width=2)
    draw.ellipse([85, c3_y + 20, 195, c3_y + 130], fill=(180, 115, 30))
    draw.ellipse([108, c3_y + 43, 172, c3_y + 107], outline=WHITE, width=3)
    draw.line([140, c3_y + 75, 140, c3_y + 56], fill=WHITE, width=3)
    draw.line([140, c3_y + 75, 156, c3_y + 75], fill=WHITE, width=3)
    draw.text((225, c3_y + 22), f"Day Choghadiya ({p.choghadiya_name})", fill=COLOR_AMBER, font=_font_sans(24, bold=True))
    draw.text((225, c3_y + 58), p.choghadiya_time, fill=COLOR_DARK, font=_font_sans(30, bold=True))
    draw.text((225, c3_y + 104), f"Auspicious {p.choghadiya_name} interval favorable for action & commerce.", fill=COLOR_MUTED, font=_font_sans(19))

    # Daily Muhurta Note
    draw.text((540, 1205), "“Right timing brings the right results.”", fill=COLOR_CRIMSON, font=_font_serif(24, italic=True), anchor="mm")

    # Standardized bottom CTA pill
    _draw_cta_pill(canvas, draw, "View All Daily Muhurtas in Siddhanta", y=1245)

    return _save_card_image(canvas, output_path)


# ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 3. FESTIVAL (1080 x 1350)
# ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
def render_card_3_festival(p: DailyPanchang, output_path: str | Path) -> str:
    fest_title = p.festival_name.split("(")[0].strip() if (p.is_festival and p.festival_name) else "Jivitputrika Vrat"
    canvas, draw = _prepare_card_canvas("festival", festival_name=fest_title)
    _draw_brand_header(canvas, draw, y=42)

    # Title & Sub-headlines
    draw.text((60, 160), fest_title, fill=COLOR_CRIMSON, font=_font_serif(56, bold=True))
    date_str = p.date.strftime("%d %B %Y").upper()
    paksha_str = f"{date_str}  •  {p.paksha.upper()} PAKSHA • {p.tithi_name.upper()}"
    draw.text((60, 232), paksha_str, fill=COLOR_GOLD, font=_font_sans(21, bold=True))

    # Left Column: 3 concise Shastric points
    if "jivitputrika" in fest_title.lower() or "jitiya" in fest_title.lower():
        pts = [
            ("icons/diya.webp", "Nirjala Mahavrat", "24-hour sacred waterless fast"),
            ("icons/L Otus.webp", "Lord Jimutavahana", "Worship for progeny longevity"),
            ("icons/family.webp", "Parana Muhurta", "Next day post-sunrise puja"),
        ]
    elif "navratri" in fest_title.lower():
        pts = [
            ("icons/diya.webp", "Ghatasthapana", "Invoking Divine Mother Durga"),
            ("icons/L Otus.webp", "Shailaputri Puja", "First day sacred worship"),
            ("icons/family.webp", "Abhijit Muhurta", p.abhijit_muhurta),
        ]
    else:
        pts = [
            ("icons/diya.webp", "Sacred Observance", "Traditional Vedic fast and puja"),
            ("icons/L Otus.webp", "Presiding Deity", p.deity),
            ("icons/family.webp", "Auspicious Timing", p.abhijit_muhurta),
        ]

    for idx, (icon_rel, pt_head, pt_sub) in enumerate(pts):
        b_y = 320 + idx * 140
        _paste_icon_badge(canvas, ILLUST_DIR / icon_rel, (60, b_y, 140, b_y + 80), badge_fill=(254, 243, 220), padding=10)
        draw.text((165, b_y + 12), pt_head, fill=COLOR_DARK, font=_font_sans(24, bold=True))
        draw.text((165, b_y + 44), pt_sub, fill=COLOR_MUTED, font=_font_sans(19))

    # Right Column: Maa Durga Riding Lion Illustration (positioned cleanly on right)
    durga_path = ILLUST_DIR / "festivals" / "durga.webp"
    if durga_path.exists():
        try:
            durga = Image.open(durga_path)
            durga = _clean_transparency(durga)
            scale = 580 / durga.height
            nw, nh = int(durga.width * scale), int(durga.height * scale)
            durga = durga.resize((nw, nh), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)
            canvas.alpha_composite(durga, (520, 300))
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Observance note
    obs_text = p.festival_note if p.festival_note else "May this sacred observance bring health, peace, and spiritual grace."
    if len(obs_text) > 75:
        obs_text = obs_text[:72] + "..."
    draw.text((540, 1205), f"“{obs_text}”", fill=COLOR_CRIMSON, font=_font_serif(22, italic=True), anchor="mm")

    # Standardized bottom CTA pill
    _draw_cta_pill(canvas, draw, "Explore Festival Details in Siddhanta", y=1245)

    return _save_card_image(canvas, output_path)


# ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 4. KUNDLI (1080 x 1350)
# ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
def render_card_4_kundli(p: DailyPanchang, output_path: str | Path) -> str:
    canvas, draw = _prepare_card_canvas("kundli")
    _draw_brand_header(canvas, draw, y=42)

    # Title & Subtitle per user feedback
    draw.text((60, 160), "Your Kundli", fill=COLOR_CRIMSON, font=_font_serif(56, bold=True))
    draw.text((60, 232), "A deeper understanding of your unique journey.", fill=COLOR_MUTED, font=_font_serif(22, bold=False))

    # Authentic North Indian D1 Lagna Kundli Chart (scaled for 1350 canvas: 480px size centered)
    ch_sz = 480
    ch_x1 = (CARD_W - ch_sz) // 2
    ch_y1 = 290
    ch_x2, ch_y2 = ch_x1 + ch_sz, ch_y1 + ch_sz

    # Background card for chart
    draw.rounded_rectangle([ch_x1 - 12, ch_y1 - 12, ch_x2 + 12, ch_y2 + 12], radius=20, fill=(255, 253, 248, 245), outline=(210, 175, 120), width=2)
    draw.rectangle([ch_x1, ch_y1, ch_x2, ch_y2], outline=(200, 160, 100), width=2)

    # Diagonals
    draw.line([ch_x1, ch_y1, ch_x2, ch_y2], fill=(200, 160, 100), width=2)
    draw.line([ch_x1, ch_y2, ch_x2, ch_y1], fill=(200, 160, 100), width=2)

    # Inner Diamond
    mid_x = (ch_x1 + ch_x2) // 2
    mid_y = (ch_y1 + ch_y2) // 2
    draw.polygon([(mid_x, ch_y1), (ch_x2, mid_y), (mid_x, ch_y2), (ch_x1, mid_y)], outline=(200, 160, 100), width=2)

    # Central Lagna emblem in House 1
    draw.ellipse([mid_x - 36, mid_y - 36, mid_x + 36, mid_y + 36], fill=(254, 243, 220), outline=(210, 175, 120), width=2)
    draw.text((mid_x, mid_y), "Lagna", fill=COLOR_CRIMSON, font=_font_serif(20, bold=True), anchor="mm")

    # House Numbers & Planets
    f_p = _font_serif(26, bold=True)
    f_num = _font_sans(16, bold=False)

    # House 1 (top center diamond)
    draw.text((mid_x, mid_y - 75), "1", fill=COLOR_MUTED, font=f_num, anchor="mm")
    draw.text((mid_x, mid_y - 110), "Ju", fill=COLOR_GOLD, font=f_p, anchor="mm")

    # House 2 (top left triangle)
    draw.text((ch_x1 + 160, ch_y1 + 55), "2", fill=COLOR_MUTED, font=f_num)
    draw.text((ch_x1 + 90, ch_y1 + 80), "Ve", fill=(34, 139, 34), font=f_p)

    # House 12 (top right triangle)
    draw.text((ch_x2 - 180, ch_y1 + 55), "12", fill=COLOR_MUTED, font=f_num)
    draw.text((ch_x2 - 110, ch_y1 + 80), "Ma", fill=COLOR_CRIMSON, font=f_p)

    # House 4 (left center diamond)
    draw.text((ch_x1 + 140, mid_y), "4", fill=COLOR_MUTED, font=f_num)
    draw.text((ch_x1 + 60, mid_y), "Mo", fill=(25, 25, 112), font=f_p)

    # House 10 (right center diamond)
    draw.text((ch_x2 - 150, mid_y), "10", fill=COLOR_MUTED, font=f_num)
    draw.text((ch_x2 - 70, mid_y), "Sa", fill=(30, 60, 140), font=f_p)

    # House 7 (bottom diamond)
    draw.text((mid_x, ch_y2 - 75), "7", fill=COLOR_MUTED, font=f_num, anchor="mm")
    draw.text((mid_x - 45, ch_y2 - 110), "Me", fill=(34, 139, 34), font=f_p)
    draw.text((mid_x + 45, ch_y2 - 110), "Su", fill=COLOR_CRIMSON, font=f_p)

    # 4 Feature Badges in a Row below Chart using exact Siddhanta terminology
    b_y = 820
    badge_w = 215
    badge_h = 160
    badges_data = [
        ("D1 Lagna", "Birth Chart", "icons/kundli.webp"),
        ("D9 Navamsha", "Soul Destiny", "icons/Vargas.webp"),
        ("Dashas", "Vimshottari", "icons/Overview.webp"),
        ("Yogas", "Planetary Yogas", "icons/yogas.webp"),
    ]

    for i, (b_title, b_sub, b_icon) in enumerate(badges_data):
        bx = 60 + i * (badge_w + 35)
        draw.rounded_rectangle([bx, b_y, bx + badge_w, b_y + badge_h], radius=16, fill=COLOR_CARD_BG, outline=COLOR_BORDER, width=2)
        _paste_icon_badge(canvas, ILLUST_DIR / b_icon, (bx + 75, b_y + 16, bx + 140, b_y + 81), badge_fill=(254, 243, 220), padding=8)
        draw.text((bx + badge_w // 2, b_y + 104), b_title, fill=COLOR_CRIMSON, font=_font_sans(20, bold=True), anchor="mm")
        draw.text((bx + badge_w // 2, b_y + 132), b_sub, fill=COLOR_MUTED, font=_font_sans(16), anchor="mm")

    # Subtle quote note
    draw.text((540, 1205), "“The birth chart is the cosmic map of your karmic potential.”", fill=COLOR_CRIMSON, font=_font_serif(22, italic=True), anchor="mm")

    # Standardized bottom CTA pill
    _draw_cta_pill(canvas, draw, "Generate Your Free Kundli in Siddhanta", y=1245)

    return _save_card_image(canvas, output_path)

# 5. CHAKRA (1080 x 1350)
# ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
def render_card_5_chakra(p_or_path: any, maybe_path: str | Path | None = None) -> str:
    output_path = maybe_path if maybe_path is not None else p_or_path
    canvas, draw = _prepare_card_canvas("chakra")
    _draw_brand_header(canvas, draw, y=42)

    # Title & Subtitle updated per user feedback (no health claims!)
    draw.text((60, 160), "Balance Your Inner Energy", fill=COLOR_CRIMSON, font=_font_serif(56, bold=True))
    draw.text((60, 232), "Explore the seven chakras through Jyotish insight.", fill=COLOR_MUTED, font=_font_serif(22, bold=False))

    # Top Right Circular Gauge: Overall Chakra Balance (compacted for 1350 height)
    g_cx, g_cy = 850, 420
    g_r = 65
    draw.text((g_cx, g_cy - 92), "Chakra Balance", fill=COLOR_DARK, font=_font_sans(19, bold=True), anchor="mm")

    # Gauge ring
    draw.ellipse([g_cx - g_r, g_cy - g_r, g_cx + g_r, g_cy + g_r], outline=(230, 220, 205), width=10)
    draw.arc([g_cx - g_r, g_cy - g_r, g_cx + g_r, g_cy + g_r], start=-90, end=180, fill=(46, 139, 87), width=10)
    draw.arc([g_cx - g_r, g_cy - g_r, g_cx + g_r, g_cy + g_r], start=180, end=230, fill=(218, 140, 30), width=10)
    draw.text((g_cx, g_cy), "75%", fill=COLOR_DARK, font=_font_sans(32, bold=True), anchor="mm")
    draw.text((g_cx, g_cy + 88), "Balanced", fill=COLOR_DARK, font=_font_sans(19, bold=True), anchor="mm")

    # Center: Meditating Yogi with glowing 7 chakras (scaled down cleanly, no river ghat)
    yogi_path = IMAGES_DIR / "chakra_meditating.webp"
    if yogi_path.exists():
        try:
            yogi = Image.open(yogi_path)
            yogi = _clean_transparency(yogi)
            scale = 550 / yogi.height
            nw, nh = int(yogi.width * scale), int(yogi.height * scale)
            yogi = yogi.resize((nw, nh), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)
            canvas.alpha_composite(yogi, (50, 470))
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Right Column: 7 Chakras List (compacted)
    chakras = [
        ("Sahasrara", (153, 50, 204)),
        ("Ajna", (65, 105, 225)),
        ("Vishuddha", (0, 191, 255)),
        ("Anahata", (46, 139, 87)),
        ("Manipura", (230, 175, 20)),
        ("Svadhisthana", (255, 127, 80)),
        ("Muladhara", (220, 20, 60)),
    ]

    list_x = 730
    list_y = 570
    draw.rounded_rectangle([list_x - 20, list_y - 15, 1020, list_y + 310], radius=18, fill=COLOR_CARD_BG, outline=COLOR_BORDER, width=2)

    for i, (name, col) in enumerate(chakras):
        cy = list_y + i * 42
        draw.ellipse([list_x - 5, cy + 4, list_x + 17, cy + 26], fill=col)
        draw.text((list_x + 30, cy + 5), name, fill=COLOR_DARK, font=_font_sans(19, bold=True))

    # Subtle quote note
    draw.text((540, 1205), "“Harmony in energy centers brings peace to body and mind.”", fill=COLOR_CRIMSON, font=_font_serif(22, italic=True), anchor="mm")

    # Standardized bottom CTA pill
    _draw_cta_pill(canvas, draw, "Explore Your Chakras in Siddhanta", y=1245)

    return _save_card_image(canvas, output_path)

# 6. JYOTISH INSIGHT (1080 x 1350)
# ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
def render_card_6_jyotish(p: DailyPanchang, output_path: str | Path) -> str:
    canvas, draw = _prepare_card_canvas("transit")
    _draw_brand_header(canvas, draw, y=42)

    # Pill badge
    draw.rounded_rectangle([60, 155, 280, 198], radius=12, fill=(255, 248, 235), outline=(220, 190, 140), width=2)
    draw.text((170, 176), "JYOTISH INSIGHT", fill=COLOR_GOLD, font=_font_sans(19, bold=True), anchor="mm")

    # Determine planet and sign dynamically from Gochar snapshot
    planet_name = "Jupiter"
    target_sign = "Cancer"
    date_range = "CURRENT VEDIC GOCHARA"
    motion_info = ""

    if p.gochar:
        planet_name = p.gochar.spotlight_planet or "Jupiter"
        target_sign = p.gochar.spotlight_sign or "Cancer"
        date_range = p.gochar.spotlight_date_range or "CURRENT VEDIC GOCHARA"
        if "Retrograde" in p.gochar.spotlight_motion:
            motion_info = " • RETROGRADE (वक्री)"

    # Map sign to Roman numeral, Hindi name, and classical Shastric qualities
    ZODIAC_DATA = {
        "Aries": ("I", "MESHA", "Courage & Leadership", "Initiative & fresh beginnings"),
        "Taurus": ("II", "VRISHABHA", "Material Stability", "Grounded growth & perseverance"),
        "Gemini": ("III", "MITHUNA", "Intellect & Expression", "Communication & philosophical study"),
        "Cancer": ("IV", "KARKA", "Emotional Depth & Intuition", "Inner wisdom & nurturing strength"),
        "Leo": ("V", "SIMHA", "Authority & Vitality", "Confidence & noble pursuits"),
        "Virgo": ("VI", "KANYA", "Analytical Discernment", "Detail-oriented mastery & service"),
        "Libra": ("VII", "TULA", "Harmony & Balance", "Diplomacy & refined creativity"),
        "Scorpio": ("VIII", "VRISHCHIKA", "Deep Intuition & Transformation", "Occult wisdom & emotional resilience"),
        "Sagittarius": ("IX", "DHANU", "Dharma & Higher Guidance", "Spiritual expansion & ethical triumph"),
        "Capricorn": ("X", "MAKARA", "Karmic Endurance", "Professional duty & long-term foundation"),
        "Aquarius": ("XI", "KUMBHA", "Universal Insight & Gains", "Community collaboration & vision"),
        "Pisces": ("XII", "MEENA", "Transcendence & Peace", "Moksha orientation & meditative clarity"),
    }

    clean_sign = target_sign.split()[0]
    roman, sign_hi, trait_head, trait_sub = ZODIAC_DATA.get(clean_sign, ("VII", "TULA", "Refined Harmony", "Auspicious planetary alignment"))

    # Planet-specific wisdom traits
    PLANET_TRAITS = {
        "Venus": ("Creative Harmony & Aesthetic Balance", "Deepens relationship understanding, artistic pursuits & grace"),
        "Mercury": ("Analytical Intellect & Commerce", "Sharpening business communication, logic & negotiations"),
        "Mars": ("Courage & Decisive Energy", "Action-oriented clarity, vitality & protection"),
        "Jupiter": ("Wisdom, Ethics & Expansion", "Higher learning, mentor blessings & spiritual clarity"),
        "Saturn": ("Patience, Duty & Karmic Restructuring", "Endurance, long-term discipline & grounded humility"),
    }

    if planet_name in PLANET_TRAITS:
        trait_head, trait_sub = PLANET_TRAITS[planet_name]

    # Title & Subtitle - dynamic from active transit
    draw.text((60, 215), f"{planet_name} Transit in {clean_sign}", fill=COLOR_CRIMSON, font=_font_serif(50, bold=True))
    draw.text((60, 285), f"{date_range}{motion_info}", fill=COLOR_DARK, font=_font_sans(22, bold=True))

    # Right side: Dynamic Zodiac Symbol in Gold Circle matching the transit sign
    draw.ellipse([870, 185, 995, 305], fill=(254, 243, 220), outline=(210, 175, 120), width=2)
    draw.text((932, 232), roman, fill=COLOR_CRIMSON, font=_font_serif(40, bold=True), anchor="mm")
    draw.text((932, 272), sign_hi, fill=COLOR_GOLD, font=_font_sans(13, bold=True), anchor="mm")

    # Left Column: 3 concise Shastric transit insights from Gochar snapshot
    fav_str = ", ".join(p.gochar.favorable_rashis[:2]) if (p.gochar and p.gochar.favorable_rashis) else "Cancer & Scorpio"
    pts = [
        ("icons/Overview.webp", trait_head, trait_sub),
        ("icons/family.webp", f"Favorable Alignment: {fav_str}", "Supportive planetary momentum & positive opportunities"),
        ("icons/strength.webp", f"Vedic Transit Guidance", f"Mindful alignment with {planet_name}'s cosmic energy"),
    ]

    for idx, (icon_rel, pt_head, pt_sub) in enumerate(pts):
        b_y = 350 + idx * 135
        _paste_icon_badge(canvas, ILLUST_DIR / icon_rel, (60, b_y, 140, b_y + 80), badge_fill=(254, 243, 220), padding=10)
        draw.text((165, b_y + 10), pt_head, fill=COLOR_DARK, font=_font_sans(24, bold=True))
        draw.text((165, b_y + 44), pt_sub, fill=COLOR_MUTED, font=_font_sans(19))

    # Decorative clean celestial Jupiter sphere
    c_cy = 920
    draw.ellipse([420, c_cy - 90, 660, c_cy + 90], outline=(225, 195, 140, 110), width=2)
    draw.ellipse([480, c_cy - 40, 600, c_cy + 40], fill=(218, 165, 82), outline=(180, 120, 40), width=2)

    # Subtle quote note
    draw.text((540, 1205), "“When Guru moves, wisdom finds a new direction.”", fill=COLOR_CRIMSON, font=_font_serif(22, italic=True), anchor="mm")

    # Standardized bottom CTA pill
    _draw_cta_pill(canvas, draw, "Track Planetary Transits in Siddhanta", y=1245)

    return _save_card_image(canvas, output_path)




================================================================================
FILE: .\automation\daily_worker.py

"""Master Daily Cosmic Blueprint Worker.

Runs the complete automated pipeline for Siddhanta Social Publishing:
1. Computes today's Siddhanta Panchang & detects festivals via authentic app rules (festival_resolver)
2. Uses intelligent Daily Content Selection (1 Primary Post per day from the 6-Card Universe)
3. Renders strict 1080 x 1350 (4:5 Feed Portrait) visual card using rule-based background templates
4. Prompts Gemini AI as Composer & Writer (concise, focused captions & hashtags for the chosen card)
5. Uploads to ImgBB and publishes to Instagram Graph API (or runs dry-run)

Can be executed locally or via GitHub Actions.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import datetime as dt
import json
import logging
import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from promo_studio.automation.panchang_engine import DailyPanchang
from promo_studio.automation.panchang_data_client import get_authoritative_daily_panchang
from promo_studio.automation.card_universe import (
    render_card_1_panchang,
    render_card_2_plan_day,
    render_card_3_festival,
    render_card_4_kundli,
    render_card_5_chakra,
    render_card_6_jyotish,
)
from promo_studio.automation.ai_blueprint import generate_cosmic_caption, get_devotional_fallback_copy
from promo_studio.automation.instagram_carousel import InstagramCarouselPublisher
from promo_studio.ai.gemini import GeminiProvider
from promo_studio.core.image_host import ImgBBHost

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s")
log = logging.getLogger("daily_worker")


def get_credential(name: str, fallback_settings_key: str = None) -> str:
    """Gets credential from ENV or local PromoStudio keyring/db."""
    val = os.environ.get(name)
    if val:
        return val.strip()

    try:
        from promo_studio.config.secrets import get_secret
        sec = get_secret(name)
        if sec:
            return sec.strip()
    except Exception:
        pass

    if fallback_settings_key:
        try:
            from promo_studio.data.db import Database
            from promo_studio.config.settings import Settings
            db_path = os.path.expandvars(r"%APPDATA%\PromoStudio\promo_studio.db")
            if os.path.exists(db_path):
                s = Settings(Database(db_path))
                v = s.get(fallback_settings_key)
                if v:
                    return v.strip()
        except Exception:
            pass

    return ""


def select_daily_post_type(panchang: DailyPanchang) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """Intelligently selects the single primary post type from the 6-Card Universe.
    
    Priority Hierarchy:
    1. Festival: If today has an active festival, festival card is paramount.
    2. Solar/Lunar Eclipse or Major Planetary Transit: On transit days, Jyotish insight.
    3. Weekday Content Schedule:
       - Tuesday & Thursday: Plan Your Day (Muhurtas, Rahu Kaal, Choghadiya)
       - Saturday: Jyotish Insight or Kundli (Shani / Dasha focus)
       - Sunday: Panchang (Ravivar / Surya focus)
       - Wednesday: Chakra Balance (Mercury / Anahata & Vishuddha balance)
       - Monday: Daily Panchang (Soma / Chandra devotion)
       - Friday: Kundli (Venus / Lagna harmony)
    
    Returns:
        (post_type, display_title)
    """
    # 1. Festival Check
    if panchang.is_festival and panchang.festival_name:
        return "festival", f"Festival Alert: {panchang.festival_name}"

    # 2. Weekday-based Intelligent Selection
    weekday = panchang.date.weekday()  # Monday is 0, Sunday is 6

    if weekday in (1, 3):  # Tuesday, Thursday
        return "plan_day", "Plan Your Day Wisely"
    elif weekday == 5:     # Saturday
        return "kundli", "Your Kundli & Dasha Journey"
    elif weekday == 2:     # Wednesday
        return "chakra", "Balance Your Inner Energy (Chakras)"
    elif weekday == 4:     # Friday
        return "jyotish", "Jyotish Transit Insight"
    else:                  # Monday, Sunday
        return "panchang", "Today's Panchang Overview"


def render_selected_card(post_type: str, panchang: DailyPanchang, out_path: Path) -> Path:
    """Renders the single selected card at 1080 x 1350."""
    if post_type == "festival":
        render_card_3_festival(panchang, out_path)
    elif post_type == "plan_day":
        render_card_2_plan_day(panchang, out_path)
    elif post_type == "kundli":
        render_card_4_kundli(panchang, out_path)
    elif post_type == "chakra":
        render_card_5_chakra(out_path)
    elif post_type == "jyotish":
        render_card_6_jyotish(panchang, out_path)
    else:  # default "panchang"
        render_card_1_panchang(panchang, out_path)
    return out_path


def run_daily_worker(dry_run: bool = False, format_mode: str = "single", target_date: dt.date = None, post_type_override: str = None):
    log.info("========== Starting Siddhanta Daily Social Worker (4:5 Feed Mode) ==========")

    # 1. Credentials
    gemini_key = get_credential("GEMINI_API_KEY")
    ig_user_id = get_credential("IG_USER_ID", "ig.user_id")
    ig_token = get_credential("IG_ACCESS_TOKEN")
    img_host_key = get_credential("IMAGE_HOST_KEY")
    gemini_model = os.environ.get("GEMINI_MODEL", "gemini-3.8-flash")

    if not dry_run:
        missing = []
        if not gemini_key: missing.append("GEMINI_API_KEY")
        if not ig_user_id: missing.append("IG_USER_ID")
        if not ig_token: missing.append("IG_ACCESS_TOKEN")
        if not img_host_key: missing.append("IMAGE_HOST_KEY")
        if missing:
            log.error("Missing required credentials: %s", ", ".join(missing))
            sys.exit(1)

    # 2. Authentic Panchang Calculation (Ingests Flutter dumps with fallback)
    panchang = get_authoritative_daily_panchang(target_date)
    log.info("Calculated Panchang: %s | Tithi: %s | Vara: %s | Festival: %s",
             panchang.date_formatted, panchang.tithi_name, panchang.vara_sa, panchang.festival_name or "None")

    # 3. Intelligent Content Selection
    if post_type_override:
        post_type = post_type_override
        display_title = f"Manual Override: {post_type}"
    else:
        post_type, display_title = select_daily_post_type(panchang)
    
    log.info("Selected Primary Post for Today: [%s] '%s'", post_type.upper(), display_title)

    # 4. Render 1080 x 1350 Primary Card
    out_dir = Path("output") / panchang.date.strftime("%Y%m%d")
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    
    primary_card_path = out_dir / f"primary_{post_type}.jpg"
    render_selected_card(post_type, panchang, primary_card_path)
    log.info("Rendered primary post card (1080x1350 JPEG) to %s", primary_card_path)

    # Also render all 6 cards for reference/download package (and Hindi variant)
    render_card_1_panchang(panchang, out_dir / "card1_panchang.png", lang="en")
    render_card_1_panchang(panchang, out_dir / "card1_panchang_hindi.png", lang="hi")
    render_card_2_plan_day(panchang, out_dir / "card2_plan_day.png")
    render_card_3_festival(panchang, out_dir / "card3_festival.png")
    render_card_4_kundli(panchang, out_dir / "card4_kundli.png")
    render_card_5_chakra(out_dir / "card5_chakra.png")
    render_card_6_jyotish(panchang, out_dir / "card6_jyotish.png")

    # 5. AI Copywriting (Gemini as Writer/Composer tailored to the chosen card)
    log.info("Invoking Gemini AI as Composer for post type '%s'...", post_type)
    copy_data = None
    if gemini_key:
        try:
            gemini = GeminiProvider(gemini_key, gemini_model)
            copy_data = generate_cosmic_caption(panchang, gemini, api_key=gemini_key, post_type=post_type)
        except Exception as e:
            log.warning("Gemini AI failed: %s. Using devotional fallback copy.", e)
            copy_data = get_devotional_fallback_copy(panchang, post_type=post_type)
    else:
        copy_data = get_devotional_fallback_copy(panchang, post_type=post_type)

    raw_caption = copy_data.get("instagram_caption", "")
    hashtags = " ".join(f"#{tag.lstrip('#')}" for tag in copy_data.get("hashtags", []))
    final_caption = f"{raw_caption}\n\n.\n.\n{hashtags}".strip()

    # Save outputs
    (out_dir / "caption.txt").write_text(final_caption, encoding="utf-8")
    meta = {
        "date": panchang.date_formatted,
        "post_type": post_type,
        "title": display_title,
        "tithi": panchang.tithi_name,
        "nakshatra": panchang.nakshatra_name,
        "festival": panchang.festival_name,
        "image_file": primary_card_path.name,
        "image_dimensions": "1080x1350",
        "hashtags": copy_data.get("hashtags", []),
    }
    (out_dir / "post_metadata.json").write_text(json.dumps(meta, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")

    log.info("--- GENERATED CAPTION ---\n%s\n-------------------------", final_caption)

    if dry_run:
        log.info("[DRY RUN] Primary 1080x1350 card & caption generated successfully!")
        return True

    # 6. Image Hosting Upload (ImgBB)
    img_host = ImgBBHost(img_host_key)
    log.info("Uploading primary 1080x1350 card to image host...")
    card_url = img_host.upload(str(primary_card_path))
    log.info("Uploaded Image: %s", card_url)

    # 7. Instagram Publishing via Graph API (Single 4:5 Portrait Feed Post)
    log.info("Publishing single 4:5 Portrait post to Instagram...")
    ig_publisher = InstagramCarouselPublisher(ig_user_id, ig_token)
    res = ig_publisher.publish_single(card_url, final_caption)

    if res.ok:
        log.info("SUCCESS! Post live on Instagram: %s (Post ID: %s)", res.url, res.post_id)
        return True
    else:
        log.error("Failed to publish to Instagram: [%s] %s", res.error_code, res.error_message)
        sys.exit(1)


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Siddhanta Daily Social Automation (4:5 Feed Mode)")
    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Generate 1080x1350 card & caption without posting")
    parser.add_argument("--format", choices=["single", "carousel"], default="single", help="Publish mode (default single 4:5)")
    parser.add_argument("--date", type=str, default=None, help="Target date YYYY-MM-DD")
    parser.add_argument("--type", choices=["panchang", "plan_day", "festival", "kundli", "chakra", "jyotish"], default=None, help="Force specific card type")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    target_date = None
    if args.date:
        target_date = dt.date.fromisoformat(args.date)

    run_daily_worker(dry_run=args.dry_run, format_mode=args.format, target_date=target_date, post_type_override=args.type)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()




================================================================================
FILE: .\automation\festival_resolver.py
================================================================================
"""Siddhanta App Festival Resolver.

Decoupled festival engine that queries the authentic Siddhanta app rules
from 'panchang_master_app/assets/rules/' to ensure 100% data authority and
agreement between the app and social automation.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
from pathlib import Path

log = logging.getLogger("festival_resolver")

APP_RULES_DIR = Path(r"D:\AI My Projects\Production\panchang_master_app\assets\rules")
LOCAL_RULES_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "assets" / "rules"

_CACHED_FESTIVALS: list[dict] | None = None


def load_siddhanta_festival_rules() -> list[dict]:
    """Loads festival rules from the Siddhanta app codebase or local fallback."""
    global _CACHED_FESTIVALS
    if _CACHED_FESTIVALS is not None:
        return _CACHED_FESTIVALS

    rules: list[dict] = []
    sources = [
        APP_RULES_DIR / "festivals_smartha.json",
        APP_RULES_DIR / "festivals_solar.json",
        LOCAL_RULES_DIR / "festivals_smartha.json",
    ]

    for p in sources:
        if p.exists():
            try:
                data = json.loads(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                f_list = data.get("festivals", [])
                rules.extend(f_list)
                log.info("Loaded %d festival rules from %s", len(f_list), p)
                break
            except Exception as e:
                log.warning("Could not parse festival rules from %s: %s", p, e)

    _CACHED_FESTIVALS = rules
    return rules


def resolve_festival(
    masa_name: str,
    paksha: str,
    tithi_index: int,
    tithi_name: str = ""
) -> tuple[bool, str, str, str]:
    """Resolves today's festival against Siddhanta app rules.
    
    Returns:
        (is_festival, festival_name_en, festival_name_hi, festival_observance_note)
    """
    rules = load_siddhanta_festival_rules()

    # Tithi in paksha (1..15, with 30 being Amavasya and 15 being Purnima)
    t_in_paksha = (tithi_index % 15) + 1
    if tithi_index == 29:
        t_in_paksha = 30
    elif tithi_index == 14:
        t_in_paksha = 15

    m_lower = masa_name.lower()
    p_lower = paksha.lower()

    for r in rules:
        r_masa = str(r.get("masa", "")).lower()
        r_masa_amanta = str(r.get("masa_amanta", "")).lower()
        r_paksha = str(r.get("paksha", "")).lower()
        r_tithi = r.get("tithi")

        # Match Masa (both Purnimanta and Amanta conventions)
        masa_matches = (r_masa == m_lower) or (r_masa_amanta == m_lower) or (not r_masa)
        paksha_matches = (r_paksha == p_lower) or (not r_paksha)
        tithi_matches = (r_tithi == t_in_paksha) or (r_tithi == (tithi_index + 1))

        if masa_matches and paksha_matches and tithi_matches:
            name_obj = r.get("name", {})
            name_en = name_obj.get("en", r.get("id", "Festival"))
            name_hi = name_obj.get("hi", name_en)

            # Guidelines / observance summary from rule
            notes = []
            for g in r.get("guidelines", []):
                notes.append(f"{g.get('label')}: {g.get('value')}")
            note_str = " • ".join(notes) if notes else "Auspicious Vedic observance."

            return True, name_en, name_hi, note_str

    # Built-in Shastric core festivals if external JSON rules did not match
    known_shastric = {
        # Jivitputrika (Ashwin/Bhadrapada Krishna Ashtami/Navami)
        ("bhadrapada", "krishna", 8): ("Jivitputrika Vrat (Jitiya)", "जीवित्पुत्रिका व्रत", "Nirjala Mahavrat for children's long life & protection"),
        ("bhadrapada", "krishna", 9): ("Jivitputrika Vrat (Parana)", "जितिया पारण", "Breaking of the sacred fast after sunrise puja"),
        ("ashwina", "krishna", 8): ("Jivitputrika Vrat (Jitiya)", "जीवित्पुत्रिका व्रत", "Nirjala Mahavrat for children's long life & protection"),
        ("ashwina", "krishna", 9): ("Jivitputrika Vrat (Parana)", "जितिया पारण", "Breaking of the sacred fast after sunrise puja"),
        # Navratri Day 1
        ("ashwina", "shukla", 1): ("Sharad Navratri Begins (Day 1)", "शारदीय नवरात्रि प्रतिपदा", "Ghatasthapana & worship of Maa Shailputri"),
        # Dussehra
        ("ashwina", "shukla", 10): ("Vijayadashami (Dussehra)", "विजयादशमी", "Triumph of Dharma over Adharma • Shami Puja"),
        # Diwali
        ("kartika", "krishna", 30): ("Diwali (Deepavali)", "दीपावली", "Maha Lakshmi Puja & festival of lights"),
        ("kartik", "krishna", 30): ("Diwali (Deepavali)", "दीपावली", "Maha Lakshmi Puja & festival of lights"),
    }

    key = (m_lower, p_lower, t_in_paksha)
    if key in known_shastric:
        en, hi, desc = known_shastric[key]
        return True, en, hi, desc

    return False, "", "", ""




================================================================================
FILE: .\automation\instagram_carousel.py
================================================================================
"""Instagram Carousel and Single-Image publisher via Graph API.

Supports:
- 2-Slide Carousels (Slide 1 Hook + Slide 2 Actionable Windows)
- Single 4:5 Portrait Card (Combined Daily Cosmic Blueprint)
- Automatic fallback to single card if carousel creation is rejected.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import time
import requests

from promo_studio.adapters.base import PostResult
from promo_studio.adapters.graph import GraphError, call

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class InstagramCarouselPublisher:
    def __init__(self, ig_user_id: str, token: str, version: str = "v25.0",
                 http=requests, poll_attempts: int = 6, poll_interval: float = 3.0):
        self.ig_user_id = ig_user_id.strip()
        self.token = token.strip()
        self.version = version
        self.http = http
        self.poll_attempts = poll_attempts
        self.poll_interval = poll_interval
        self.host = "https://graph.instagram.com"

    def _url(self, path: str) -> str:
        return f"{self.host}/{self.version}/{path}"

    @property
    def _headers(self) -> dict:
        return {"Authorization": f"Bearer {self.token}"}

    def _wait_ready(self, container_id: str) -> bool:
        for attempt in range(self.poll_attempts):
            try:
                res = call(self.http, "GET", self._url(container_id), headers=self._headers,
                           params={"fields": "status_code"})
                status = res.get("status_code")
                if status == "FINISHED":
                    return True
                if status in ("ERROR", "EXPIRED"):
                    log.warning("Container %s failed with status %s", container_id, status)
                    return False
            except GraphError:
                pass
            time.sleep(self.poll_interval)
        return True  # Try publishing anyway if poll timed out

    def publish_single(self, image_url: str, caption: str) -> PostResult:
        """Publishes single image post to Instagram."""
        try:
            container = call(self.http, "POST", self._url(f"{self.ig_user_id}/media"),
                             headers=self._headers,
                             json={"image_url": image_url, "caption": caption})["id"]
        except GraphError as exc:
            return exc.to_result("instagram")

        self._wait_ready(container)

        try:
            media_id = call(self.http, "POST", self._url(f"{self.ig_user_id}/media_publish"),
                            headers=self._headers, json={"creation_id": container})["id"]
            permalink = self._get_permalink(str(media_id))
            return PostResult("instagram", True, post_id=str(media_id), url=permalink)
        except GraphError as exc:
            return exc.to_result("instagram")

    def publish_carousel(self, image_urls: list[str], caption: str) -> PostResult:
        """Publishes 2-slide carousel to Instagram, with fallback to first image on error."""
        if len(image_urls) < 2:
            return self.publish_single(image_urls[0], caption)

        item_containers = []
        try:
            for url in image_urls:
                item_res = call(self.http, "POST", self._url(f"{self.ig_user_id}/media"),
                                headers=self._headers,
                                json={"image_url": url, "is_carousel_item": True})
                item_id = item_res["id"]
                self._wait_ready(item_id)
                item_containers.append(item_id)

            carousel_res = call(self.http, "POST", self._url(f"{self.ig_user_id}/media"),
                                headers=self._headers,
                                json={
                                    "media_type": "CAROUSEL",
                                    "children": item_containers,
                                    "caption": caption
                                })
            carousel_container_id = carousel_res["id"]
            self._wait_ready(carousel_container_id)

            media_id = call(self.http, "POST", self._url(f"{self.ig_user_id}/media_publish"),
                            headers=self._headers,
                            json={"creation_id": carousel_container_id})["id"]
            permalink = self._get_permalink(str(media_id))
            return PostResult("instagram", True, post_id=str(media_id), url=permalink)

        except Exception as exc:
            log.warning("Carousel publish error: %s. Falling back to single image.", exc)
            return self.publish_single(image_urls[0], caption)

    def _get_permalink(self, media_id: str) -> str:
        try:
            body = call(self.http, "GET", self._url(media_id), headers=self._headers, params={"fields": "permalink"})
            return body.get("permalink") or "https://www.instagram.com/"
        except Exception:
            return "https://www.instagram.com/"




================================================================================
FILE: .\automation\panchang_data_client.py
================================================================================
"""Cross-Repository Data Ingestor for Siddhanta Social Automator.

Implements Approach 1 of the Cross-Repository Architecture:
Seamlessly feeds authentic Flutter engine dumps (`daily_panchang.json`,
`muhurta_data.json`, `festivals_YYYY.json`, `festival_rules_catalog.json`)
into the Python Automator with a 3-tier fallback strategy:

1. Local Production Path: Direct read from `D:\\AI My Projects\\Production\\panchang_master_app\\` (Instant & offline).
2. Checked-Out / Local Folder: `panchang-data/` or project assets.
3. GitHub API (Cloud Action): Reads from `tshashwat7/panchang` branch `data` via `PANCHANG_REPO_PAT`.
4. Graceful Fallback: Internal mathematical calculation engine if all external sources are unavailable.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import datetime as dt
import json
import logging
import os
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from promo_studio.automation.panchang_engine import (
    DailyPanchang,
    GocharSnapshot,
    VARA_INFO,
    calculate_daily_panchang as _calculate_fallback,
)

log = logging.getLogger("panchang_data_client")

# Paths for local development and checked-out data
LOCAL_PROD_DIR = Path(r"D:\AI My Projects\Production\panchang_master_app")
DOWNLOADS_DIR = Path(r"C:\Users\91962\Downloads")
REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent
LOCAL_WORKSPACE_DATA_DIRS = [
    DOWNLOADS_DIR,
    LOCAL_PROD_DIR,
    REPO_ROOT / "data" / "panchang-data",
    Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "data" / "panchang-data",
]
GITHUB_REPO = os.environ.get("PANCHANG_REPO", "tshashwat7/panchang")
GITHUB_BRANCH = os.environ.get("PANCHANG_BRANCH", "data")


def fetch_cross_repo_file(filename: str) -> dict | None:
    """Fetches a JSON dump by checking local Downloads, production disk, workspace folder, or GitHub API."""
    # 1. Local disk checks (Downloads, Production directory, workspace folder)
    for ws_dir in LOCAL_WORKSPACE_DATA_DIRS:
        ws_p = ws_dir / filename
        if ws_p.exists():
            try:
                log.info("Ingesting authentic data from: %s", ws_p)
                return json.loads(ws_p.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            except Exception as e:
                log.warning("Could not read file %s: %s", ws_p, e)

    # 3. GitHub API (Cloud / GitHub Actions)
    pat = os.environ.get("PANCHANG_REPO_PAT") or os.environ.get("DATA_BRANCH_TOKEN")
    if pat:
        import urllib.request
        import urllib.error

        url = f"https://api.github.com/repos/{GITHUB_REPO}/contents/{filename}?ref={GITHUB_BRANCH}"
        headers = {
            "Authorization": f"Bearer {pat.strip()}",
            "Accept": "application/vnd.github.raw+json",
            "User-Agent": "Siddhanta-Automator-Client",
        }
        try:
            req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=headers)
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=15) as resp:
                data = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
                log.info("Successfully fetched %s from GitHub API (%s@%s)", filename, GITHUB_REPO, GITHUB_BRANCH)
                return data
        except Exception as e:
            log.warning("Failed to fetch %s via GitHub API: %s", filename, e)

    return None


def get_authoritative_daily_panchang(target_date: dt.date | None = None) -> DailyPanchang:
    """Constructs a DailyPanchang object using the official Siddhanta Flutter engine dumps.
    
    If the dump exists for target_date (or today), uses 100% authentic data from the app.
    Otherwise gracefully falls back to the internal astronomical calculator.
    """
    if target_date is None:
        ist_tz = dt.timezone(dt.timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))
        target_date = dt.datetime.now(ist_tz).date()

    daily_json = fetch_cross_repo_file("daily_panchang.json")
    muhurta_json = fetch_cross_repo_file("muhurta_data.json")

    # If daily_json is found and matches the target date, construct from Flutter dump
    if daily_json and "today" in daily_json:
        today_data = daily_json["today"]
        json_date_str = today_data.get("date", "")

        # Check if the dump date matches or if target_date is today
        if json_date_str == target_date.isoformat():
            log.info("Using authentic Flutter engine export for date: %s", json_date_str)
            return _build_panchang_from_dump(today_data, muhurta_json, target_date, daily_json)

    log.info("Cross-repo dump not matching %s; falling back to internal calculator.", target_date)
    return _calculate_fallback(target_date)


def _build_panchang_from_dump(
    t: dict,
    m_json: dict | None,
    target_date: dt.date,
    daily_root: dict
) -> DailyPanchang:
    """Maps authentic Flutter JSON dump schema directly into DailyPanchang."""
    # Weekday & Deity
    weekday_idx = target_date.weekday()
    vara_info = VARA_INFO.get(weekday_idx, VARA_INFO[6])

    # Check for Schema 3.5 nested hindi and english sections
    hi_block = t.get("hindi", {})
    en_block = t.get("english", {})

    # Core timings
    sunrise = hi_block.get("sunrise") or en_block.get("sunrise") or t.get("sunrise", "6:15 AM")
    sunset = hi_block.get("sunset") or en_block.get("sunset") or t.get("sunset", "6:00 PM")

    # Abhijit
    abhijit_str = "12:03 PM - 12:51 PM"
    if "shubh_muhurta" in hi_block and "abhijit" in hi_block["shubh_muhurta"]:
        abhijit_str = hi_block["shubh_muhurta"]["abhijit"]
    elif "auspicious_muhurta" in en_block and "abhijit" in en_block["auspicious_muhurta"]:
        abhijit_str = en_block["auspicious_muhurta"]["abhijit"]
    else:
        abhijit_obj = t.get("abhijit_muhurta", {})
        if isinstance(abhijit_obj, dict) and "start" in abhijit_obj and "end" in abhijit_obj:
            abhijit_str = f"{abhijit_obj['start']} - {abhijit_obj['end']}"

    # Rahu Kaal
    rahu_str = "4:42 PM - 6:12 PM"
    if "ashubh_muhurta" in hi_block and "rahu_kaal" in hi_block["ashubh_muhurta"]:
        rahu_str = hi_block["ashubh_muhurta"]["rahu_kaal"]
    elif "inauspicious_muhurta" in en_block and "rahu_kaal" in en_block["inauspicious_muhurta"]:
        rahu_str = en_block["inauspicious_muhurta"]["rahu_kaal"]
    else:
        rahu_obj = t.get("rahu_kaal", {})
        if isinstance(rahu_obj, dict) and "start" in rahu_obj and "end" in rahu_obj:
            rahu_str = f"{rahu_obj['start']} - {rahu_obj['end']}"

    # Tithi & Paksha
    tithi_name = en_block.get("tithi_name") or t.get("tithi_name") or t.get("tithi", "Dashami")
    tithi_name_hi = hi_block.get("tithi_name") or t.get("tithi_name_hi") or t.get("tithi_hi", tithi_name)
    paksha_raw = en_block.get("paksha") or t.get("paksha", "shukla").capitalize()
    paksha_raw = paksha_raw.capitalize()
    paksha_hi = hi_block.get("paksha") or ("शुक्ल पक्ष" if paksha_raw.lower() == "shukla" else "कृष्ण पक्ष")

    # Nakshatra
    nakshatra_name = en_block.get("nakshatra") or t.get("nakshatra", "Punarvasu")
    nakshatra_name_hi = hi_block.get("nakshatra") or t.get("nakshatra_hi", nakshatra_name)

    # Masa
    masa_name = en_block.get("masa") or t.get("masa_purnimanta") or t.get("masa", "Ashwin")
    masa_name_hi = hi_block.get("masa") or t.get("masa_hi", "आश्विन")

    # Festivals
    is_festival = t.get("is_festival_today", False)
    festivals_today = t.get("festivals_today", [])
    if festivals_today and not is_festival:
        is_festival = True
    festival_name = t.get("primary_festival_en") or (festivals_today[0] if festivals_today else "")
    festival_note = ""

    # Check upcoming festivals if today has none
    if not is_festival and "upcoming_festivals" in daily_root:
        for uf in daily_root["upcoming_festivals"]:
            if uf.get("date") == target_date.isoformat() or uf.get("days_away") == 0:
                is_festival = True
                festival_name = uf.get("name_en", uf.get("name", ""))
                break

    # Rashi
    moon_rashi = en_block.get("moon_rashi") or "Gemini"
    moon_rashi_hi = hi_block.get("chandra_rashi") or "मिथुन"
    sun_rashi = en_block.get("sun_rashi") or "Virgo"
    sun_rashi_hi = hi_block.get("surya_rashi") or "कन्या"

    # Gochara transits
    transits_data = daily_root.get("transits", {}).get("positions", {})
    major_transits = daily_root.get("transits", {}).get("major_transits", {})

    jup_sign = transits_data.get("jupiter", {}).get("rashi_en", "Cancer")
    sat_sign = transits_data.get("saturn", {}).get("rashi_en", "Pisces")
    rahu_sign = transits_data.get("rahu", {}).get("rashi_en", "Aquarius")
    ketu_sign = transits_data.get("ketu", {}).get("rashi_en", "Leo")

    # Detect active spotlight planet (e.g. Venus retrograde, Mercury, Mars, or Jupiter/Saturn)
    # Check if there's a retrograde planet or upcoming/recent ingress
    spot_planet = "Jupiter"
    spot_planet_hi = "गुरु (बृहस्पति)"
    spot_sign = jup_sign
    spot_sign_hi = "कर्क"
    spot_date = "01 JUN 2026 – 31 OCT 2026"
    spot_motion = "Direct"

    # Prioritize Venus or Mercury if they have special motion, else major ingress
    if "venus" in transits_data and transits_data["venus"].get("is_retrograde"):
        v = transits_data["venus"]
        spot_planet = "Venus"
        spot_planet_hi = "शुक्र देव"
        spot_sign = v.get("rashi_en", "Libra")
        spot_sign_hi = v.get("rashi_hi", "तुला")
        spot_motion = "Retrograde (वक्री)"
        spot_date = "CURRENT VEDIC GOCHARA"
    elif "mercury" in transits_data and transits_data["mercury"].get("is_retrograde"):
        m = transits_data["mercury"]
        spot_planet = "Mercury"
        spot_planet_hi = "बुध देव"
        spot_sign = m.get("rashi_en", "Libra")
        spot_sign_hi = m.get("rashi_hi", "तुला")
        spot_motion = "Retrograde (वक्री)"
        spot_date = "CURRENT VEDIC GOCHARA"
    elif "jupiter" in major_transits:
        j = major_transits["jupiter"]
        spot_planet = j.get("planet_en", "Jupiter")
        spot_planet_hi = j.get("planet_hi", "गुरु")
        spot_sign = j.get("current_rashi_en", jup_sign)
        spot_sign_hi = j.get("current_rashi_hi", "कर्क")
        ing = j.get("ingress_date", "2026-06-01")
        egr = j.get("egress_date", "2026-10-31")
        spot_date = f"{ing} – {egr}"
        spot_motion = j.get("motion_en", "Direct")

    gochar_snapshot = GocharSnapshot(
        sun_sign=f"{sun_rashi} ({sun_rashi_hi})",
        moon_sign=f"{moon_rashi} ({moon_rashi_hi})",
        jupiter_sign=jup_sign,
        saturn_sign=sat_sign,
        rahu_sign=rahu_sign,
        ketu_sign=ketu_sign,
        favorable_rashis=["Cancer", "Scorpio", "Gemini", "Libra"],
        caution_rashis=["Pisces", "Virgo", "Sagittarius"],
        dasha_spotlight_title="विंशोत्तरी महादशा (Vimshottari Dasha)",
        dasha_spotlight_desc="Karmic timing framework revealing the planetary influences currently active in your life.",
        spotlight_planet=spot_planet,
        spotlight_planet_hi=spot_planet_hi,
        spotlight_sign=spot_sign,
        spotlight_sign_hi=spot_sign_hi,
        spotlight_date_range=spot_date.upper(),
        spotlight_motion=spot_motion,
    )

    # Content theme
    content_theme = "festival_alert" if is_festival else ("saturday_shani" if weekday_idx == 5 else ("sunday_numerology" if weekday_idx == 6 else "daily_panchang"))

    # Numerology
    def sum_digits(n: int) -> int:
        while n > 9:
            n = sum(int(d) for d in str(n))
        return n

    root_num = sum_digits(target_date.day)
    destiny_num = sum_digits(target_date.day + target_date.month + target_date.year)

    date_formatted = target_date.strftime("%A, %d %B %Y")
    date_formatted_hi = f"{t.get('vara_hi', vara_info['name_hi'])}, {target_date.day} {masa_name_hi} {target_date.year}"

    # Extract first prime auspicious Choghadiya from dump (Labh, Amrit, or Shubh)
    chog_name = "Labh"
    chog_time = "9:17 AM – 10:46 AM"
    day_chogs = (m_json or {}).get("choghadiya", {}).get("day", [])
    if not day_chogs and "choghadiya" in t:
        day_chogs = t["choghadiya"].get("day", [])

    for c_item in day_chogs:
        if c_item.get("is_auspicious") or c_item.get("quality_en") in ("Auspicious", "Best"):
            c_n = c_item.get("name_en", "Labh")
            c_s = c_item.get("start", "")
            c_e = c_item.get("end", "")
            if c_s and c_e:
                chog_name = c_n
                chog_time = f"{c_s} – {c_e}"
                break

    tithi_end_val = en_block.get("tithi_end") or hi_block.get("tithi_end") or t.get("tithi_end", "")
    nak_end_val = en_block.get("nakshatra_end") or hi_block.get("nakshatra_end") or t.get("nakshatra_end", "")

    return DailyPanchang(
        date=target_date,
        date_formatted=date_formatted,
        date_formatted_hi=date_formatted_hi,
        weekday_index=weekday_idx,
        vara_sa=t.get("vara", vara_info["name_sa"]),
        vara_en=t.get("day_of_week", vara_info["name_en"]),
        vara_hi=t.get("vara_hi", vara_info["name_hi"]),
        deity=vara_info["deity"],
        deity_hi=vara_info["deity_hi"],
        color=vara_info["color"],
        significance=vara_info["significance"],
        remedy=vara_info["remedy"],
        paksha=paksha_raw,
        paksha_hi=paksha_hi,
        tithi_index=9,
        tithi_name=f"{paksha_raw} {tithi_name}",
        tithi_name_hi=f"{'शुक्ल' if paksha_raw == 'Shukla' else 'कृष्ण'} {tithi_name_hi}",
        masa_name=masa_name,
        masa_name_hi=masa_name_hi,
        nakshatra_index=6,
        nakshatra_name=nakshatra_name,
        nakshatra_name_hi=nakshatra_name_hi,
        moon_rashi=moon_rashi,
        moon_rashi_hi=t.get("rashi_hi", "मिथुन"),
        sun_rashi="Kanya (Virgo)",
        sun_rashi_hi="कन्या",
        sunrise_str=sunrise,
        sunset_str=sunset,
        abhijit_muhurta=abhijit_str,
        rahu_kaal=rahu_str,
        choghadiya_name=chog_name,
        choghadiya_time=chog_time,
        tithi_end=tithi_end_val,
        nakshatra_end=nak_end_val,
        gochar=gochar_snapshot,
        content_theme=content_theme,
        is_festival=is_festival,
        festival_name=festival_name,
        festival_note=festival_note,
        root_number=root_num,
        destiny_number=destiny_num,
        numerology_focus=f"Root {root_num} • Destiny {destiny_num}",
    )

@dataclass
class GocharSnapshot:
    sun_sign: str
    moon_sign: str
    jupiter_sign: str
    saturn_sign: str
    rahu_sign: str
    ketu_sign: str
    favorable_rashis: list[str]
    caution_rashis: list[str]
    dasha_spotlight_title: str
    dasha_spotlight_desc: str
    # Dynamic spotlight transit (can be Mercury, Venus, Mars, Jupiter, Saturn, etc.)
    spotlight_planet: str = "Jupiter"
    spotlight_planet_hi: str = "गुरु (बृहस्पति)"
    spotlight_sign: str = "Cancer"
    spotlight_sign_hi: str = "कर्क"
    spotlight_date_range: str = "14 MAY 2026 – 1 JUNE 2027"
    spotlight_motion: str = "Direct"


@dataclass
class DailyPanchang:
    date: dt.date
    date_formatted: str
    date_formatted_hi: str
    weekday_index: int
    vara_sa: str
    vara_en: str
    vara_hi: str
    deity: str
    deity_hi: str
    color: str
    significance: str
    remedy: str
    
    # Core Panchang
    paksha: str               # "Shukla" or "Krishna"
    paksha_hi: str            # "शुक्ल पक्ष" or "कृष्ण पक्ष"
    tithi_index: int          # 0..29
    tithi_name: str           # e.g. "Shukla Dashami" or "Ekadashi"
    tithi_name_hi: str        # e.g. "शुक्ल नवमी"
    masa_name: str            # e.g. "Ashwina"
    masa_name_hi: str         # e.g. "आश्विन"
    nakshatra_index: int      # 0..26
    nakshatra_name: str       # e.g. "Rohini"
    nakshatra_name_hi: str    # e.g. "रोहिणी"
    moon_rashi: str           # e.g. "Vrishabha (Taurus)"
    moon_rashi_hi: str        # e.g. "वृषभ"
    sun_rashi: str            # e.g. "Kanya (Virgo)"
    sun_rashi_hi: str         # e.g. "कन्या"
    
    # Auspicious & Inauspicious Timings
    sunrise_str: str          # e.g. "06:16 AM"
    sunset_str: str           # e.g. "06:04 PM"
    abhijit_muhurta: str      # e.g. "11:45 AM - 12:33 PM"
    rahu_kaal: str            # e.g. "04:30 PM - 06:00 PM"
    choghadiya_name: str = "Labh"
    choghadiya_time: str = "9:17 AM – 10:46 AM"
    tithi_end: str = ""
    nakshatra_end: str = ""
    
    # Universal Planetary Transits & Dasha Spotlight
    gochar: GocharSnapshot | None = None
    
    # Rotation & Content Flags
    content_theme: str = "daily_panchang"
    is_festival: bool = False
    festival_name: str = ""
    festival_note: str = ""
    
    # Astro-Numerology (Sunday focus)
    root_number: int = 1
    destiny_number: int = 1
    numerology_focus: str = ""


# ── Astronomical Helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _julian_day(year: int, month: int, day: int, hour: float = 0.0) -> float:
    if month <= 2:
        year -= 1
        month += 12
    A = math.floor(year / 100)
    B = 2 - A + math.floor(A / 4)
    return math.floor(365.25 * (year + 4716)) + math.floor(30.6001 * (month + 1)) + day + hour / 24.0 + B - 1524.5


def _lahiri_ayanamsha(jd: float) -> float:
    t = (jd - 2451545.0) / 36525.0
    return 23.85833 + (50.290966 * t * 100) / 3600.0


def _sun_longitude(jd: float) -> float:
    """Mean and true ecliptic longitude of the Sun (Meeus Ch. 25)."""
    T = (jd - 2451545.0) / 36525.0
    L0 = 280.46646 + 36000.76983 * T + 0.0003032 * T * T
    M = 357.52911 + 35999.05029 * T - 0.0001537 * T * T
    M_rad = math.radians(M)
    C = (1.914602 - 0.004817 * T - 0.000014 * T * T) * math.sin(M_rad)
    C += (0.019993 - 0.000101 * T) * math.sin(2 * M_rad)
    C += 0.000289 * math.sin(3 * M_rad)
    return (L0 + C) % 360.0


def _moon_longitude(jd: float) -> float:
    """Lunar ecliptic longitude (Meeus Ch. 47 truncated)."""
    T = (jd - 2451545.0) / 36525.0
    L_prime = 218.3164477 + 481267.88123421 * T - 0.0015786 * T * T
    D = 297.8501921 + 445267.1114034 * T - 0.0018819 * T * T
    M = 357.5291092 + 35999.0502909 * T - 0.0001536 * T * T
    M_prime = 134.9633964 + 477198.8675055 * T + 0.0087414 * T * T
    F = 93.2720950 + 483202.0175233 * T - 0.0036539 * T * T

    # Principal periodic terms in longitude (degrees)
    l_sum = (
        6.288774 * math.sin(math.radians(M_prime))
        + 1.274027 * math.sin(math.radians(2 * D - M_prime))
        + 0.658314 * math.sin(math.radians(2 * D))
        + 0.213618 * math.sin(math.radians(2 * M_prime))
        - 0.185116 * math.sin(math.radians(M))
        - 0.114332 * math.sin(math.radians(2 * F))
        + 0.058793 * math.sin(math.radians(2 * D - 2 * M_prime))
        + 0.057066 * math.sin(math.radians(2 * D - M - M_prime))
        + 0.053322 * math.sin(math.radians(2 * D + M_prime))
        + 0.045758 * math.sin(math.radians(2 * D - M))
        - 0.040923 * math.sin(math.radians(M - M_prime))
    )
    return (L_prime + l_sum) % 360.0


def _approx_sun_times(date: dt.date, lat: float = 28.6139, lon: float = 77.2090) -> tuple[dt.time, dt.time]:
    """Computes approximate sunrise and sunset for India/IST."""
    # Standard solar declination calculation
    day_of_year = date.timetuple().tm_yday
    declination = 23.45 * math.sin(math.radians(360 / 365 * (day_of_year - 81)))
    lat_rad = math.radians(lat)
    dec_rad = math.radians(declination)
    
    # Zenith angle for atmospheric refraction: 90°50' = 90.833°
    cos_omega = (math.cos(math.radians(90.833)) - math.sin(lat_rad) * math.sin(dec_rad)) / (math.cos(lat_rad) * math.cos(dec_rad))
    cos_omega = max(-1.0, min(1.0, cos_omega))
    omega = math.degrees(math.acos(cos_omega))
    daylight_hours = (2 * omega) / 15.0

    # Solar noon in IST (82.5° E is IST meridian)
    # Longitude offset: (82.5 - lon) * 4 minutes
    time_offset_min = (82.5 - lon) * 4.0
    # Equation of time approx (minutes)
    B = math.radians(360 / 365 * (day_of_year - 81))
    eot_min = 9.87 * math.sin(2 * B) - 7.53 * math.cos(B) - 1.5 * math.sin(B)
    solar_noon_min = 12 * 60 + time_offset_min - eot_min

    sunrise_min = solar_noon_min - (daylight_hours / 2.0) * 60
    sunset_min = solar_noon_min + (daylight_hours / 2.0) * 60

    sunrise_h, sunrise_m = int(sunrise_min // 60), int(sunrise_min % 60)
    sunset_h, sunset_m = int(sunset_min // 60), int(sunset_min % 60)

    return dt.time(sunrise_h, sunrise_m), dt.time(sunset_h, sunset_m)


def _fmt_time(t: dt.time) -> str:
    h = t.hour % 12 or 12
    am_pm = "AM" if t.hour < 12 else "PM"
    return f"{h:02d}:{t.minute:02d} {am_pm}"


def _fmt_window(start: dt.datetime, end: dt.datetime) -> str:
    return f"{_fmt_time(start.time())} - {_fmt_time(end.time())}"


# ── Festival & Special Tithi Detection ───────────────────────────────────────

SPECIAL_FESTIVALS = {
    # (Masa_index, Paksha, Tithi_number_in_paksha): ("Festival Name", "Observance Note")
    (0, "Shukla", 1): ("Chaitra Navratri Day 1 / Ugadi / Gudi Padwa", "Hindu New Year begins. Great auspiciousness."),
    (0, "Shukla", 9): ("Ram Navami", "Appearance day of Bhagwan Sri Ram."),
    (4, "Shukla", 5): ("Nag Panchami", "Dedicated to Serpent deities and spiritual protection."),
    (4, "Shukla", 15): ("Raksha Bandhan", "Celebration of sibling bonds and protection."),
    (5, "Krishna", 8): ("Jivitputrika Vrat (Jitiya)", "Sacred 24-hour nirjala fast by mothers for children's longevity and protection."),
    (5, "Krishna", 9): ("Jivitputrika Vrat (Jitiya Parana)", "Mothers break the sacred Jitiya fast with divine blessings of Lord Jimutavahana."),
    (5, "Shukla", 4): ("Ganesh Chaturthi", "Welcoming Lord Ganesha, remover of all obstacles."),
    (6, "Shukla", 1): ("Sharad Navratri Day 1", "Worship of Maa Durga begins."),
    (6, "Shukla", 10): ("Vijayadashami / Dussehra", "Triumph of dharma over adharma."),
    (7, "Krishna", 13): ("Dhanteras", "Welcoming Lord Dhanvantari & Goddess Lakshmi."),
    (7, "Krishna", 30): ("Deepawali / Diwali", "Maha Lakshmi Puja & festival of lights."),
    (10, "Krishna", 14): ("Maha Shivratri", "The Great Night of Lord Shiva. All-night vigil & meditation."),
    (11, "Shukla", 15): ("Holi", "Festival of colors, devotion of Prahlada, victory of faith.")
}


def _detect_festival(masa_idx: int, paksha: str, tithi_in_paksha: int) -> tuple[bool, str, str]:
    # Check fixed named festivals
    key = (masa_idx, paksha, tithi_in_paksha)
    if key in SPECIAL_FESTIVALS:
        name, note = SPECIAL_FESTIVALS[key]
        return True, name, note

    # Recurring high-significance Tithis
    if tithi_in_paksha == 11:
        return True, f"{paksha} Ekadashi Vrat", "Supreme day of Lord Vishnu devotion, fasting, and spiritual purification."
    if tithi_in_paksha == 13:
        return True, f"{paksha} Pradosh Vrat", "Evening twilight dedicated to Lord Shiva; dissolves karmic hurdles."
    if paksha == "Shukla" and tithi_in_paksha == 15:
        return True, "Purnima (Full Moon)", "Peak spiritual energy, Satyanarayan Puja, and gratitude meditation."
    if paksha == "Krishna" and tithi_in_paksha == 15:
        return True, "Amavasya (New Moon)", "Ancestral blessings (Pitru Tarpan), self-reflection, and inner stillness."

    return False, "", ""


# ── Main Panchang Calculator ────────────────────────────────────────────────

def calculate_daily_panchang(target_date: dt.date | None = None) -> DailyPanchang:
    """Calculates full Daily Cosmic Blueprint Panchang for given date (default today IST)."""
    if target_date is None:
        ist_tz = dt.timezone(dt.timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))
        target_date = dt.datetime.now(ist_tz).date()

    # Sunrise reference for India (06:00 IST = 00:30 UTC)
    sunrise_time, sunset_time = _approx_sun_times(target_date)
    sunrise_dt = dt.datetime.combine(target_date, sunrise_time)
    sunset_dt = dt.datetime.combine(target_date, sunset_time)
    
    # Julian day at sunrise UTC (IST is UTC+5:30)
    sunrise_utc_hour = (sunrise_time.hour + sunrise_time.minute / 60.0) - 5.5
    jd = _julian_day(target_date.year, target_date.month, target_date.day, sunrise_utc_hour)
    ayanamsha = _lahiri_ayanamsha(jd)

    # Tropical & Sidereal (Nirayana) Positions
    sun_trop = _sun_longitude(jd)
    moon_trop = _moon_longitude(jd)
    sun_sidereal = (sun_trop - ayanamsha) % 360.0
    moon_sidereal = (moon_trop - ayanamsha) % 360.0

    # Tithi Calculation (0 to 29)
    diff = (moon_sidereal - sun_sidereal) % 360.0
    tithi_index = int(diff // 12.0)
    paksha = "Shukla" if tithi_index < 15 else "Krishna"
    tithi_in_paksha = (tithi_index % 15) + 1
    tithi_base_name = TITHI_NAMES[tithi_index]
    tithi_name = f"{paksha} {tithi_base_name}" if tithi_base_name not in ("Purnima", "Amavasya") else tithi_base_name

    # Nakshatra Calculation (0 to 26)
    nakshatra_index = int((moon_sidereal % 360.0) // (360.0 / 27.0))
    nakshatra_name = NAKSHATRA_NAMES[nakshatra_index]

    # Rashi Calculation (0 to 11)
    sun_rashi_idx = int(sun_sidereal // 30.0)
    moon_rashi_idx = int(moon_sidereal // 30.0)
    sun_rashi = RASHI_NAMES[sun_rashi_idx]
    moon_rashi = RASHI_NAMES[moon_rashi_idx]

    # Hindu Lunar Month (Amanta system based on Sun sign + alignment)
    # Sun sign in Aries (0) -> Chaitra/Vaishakha
    masa_idx = (sun_rashi_idx) % 12
    masa_name = MASA_NAMES[masa_idx]

    # Weekday & Deity
    weekday_idx = target_date.weekday()  # 0=Monday, 6=Sunday
    vara = VARA_INFO[weekday_idx]

    # Abhijit Muhurta: approx 48 minutes centered on solar noon
    daylight_duration = sunset_dt - sunrise_dt
    solar_noon = sunrise_dt + daylight_duration / 2
    abhijit_start = solar_noon - dt.timedelta(minutes=24)
    abhijit_end = solar_noon + dt.timedelta(minutes=24)
    abhijit_str = _fmt_window(abhijit_start, abhijit_end)

    # Rahu Kaal: 1/8th of daylight duration
    rahu_start_part, rahu_end_part = RAHU_KAAL_PARTS[weekday_idx]
    part_duration = daylight_duration / 8
    rahu_start = sunrise_dt + part_duration * rahu_start_part
    rahu_end = sunrise_dt + part_duration * rahu_end_part
    rahu_str = _fmt_window(rahu_start, rahu_end)

    # Festival / Vrat Check
    is_fest, fest_name, fest_note = _detect_festival(masa_idx, paksha, tithi_in_paksha)

    # Determine 7-day content rotation theme
    if is_fest:
        content_theme = "festival_alert"
    elif weekday_idx == 5:  # Saturday
        content_theme = "saturday_shani"
    elif weekday_idx == 6:  # Sunday
        content_theme = "sunday_numerology"
    else:  # Monday - Friday
        content_theme = "daily_panchang"

    # Numerology (Sunday calculation or daily root number)
    def sum_digits(n: int) -> int:
        while n > 9:
            n = sum(int(d) for d in str(n))
        return n

    root_num = sum_digits(target_date.day)
    destiny_num = sum_digits(target_date.day + target_date.month + target_date.year)

    # Planetary Transits (Gochar) & Classical Shastra Rashi Analysis
    T = (jd - 2451545.0) / 36525.0
    L_jup = (34.351519 + 3034.905661 * T) % 360.0 - ayanamsha
    L_sat = (50.077444 + 1222.113794 * T) % 360.0 - ayanamsha
    L_rahu = (259.183275 - 1934.134925 * T) % 360.0 - ayanamsha

    jup_rashi_idx = int((L_jup % 360.0) // 30.0)
    sat_rashi_idx = int((L_sat % 360.0) // 30.0)
    rahu_rashi_idx = int((L_rahu % 360.0) // 30.0)
    ketu_rashi_idx = (rahu_rashi_idx + 6) % 12

    # Favorable and caution rashis from Jupiter (2,5,7,9,11) and Saturn (3,6,11)
    favorable_rashis, caution_rashis = [], []
    for r_idx in range(12):
        dist_jup = ((jup_rashi_idx - r_idx + 12) % 12) + 1
        dist_sat = ((sat_rashi_idx - r_idx + 12) % 12) + 1
        name = RASHI_NAMES[r_idx].split()[0]
        if dist_jup in (2, 5, 7, 9, 11) or dist_sat in (3, 6, 11):
            if name not in favorable_rashis:
                favorable_rashis.append(name)
        elif dist_sat in (12, 1, 2, 4, 8):
            if name not in caution_rashis:
                caution_rashis.append(name)

    # Rotating Dasha Educational Spotlight
    dasha_idx = (target_date.timetuple().tm_yday) % len(VIMSHOTTARI_MAHADASHAS)
    d_title, d_desc = VIMSHOTTARI_MAHADASHAS[dasha_idx]

    gochar_snapshot = GocharSnapshot(
        sun_sign=sun_rashi,
        moon_sign=moon_rashi,
        jupiter_sign=RASHI_NAMES[jup_rashi_idx],
        saturn_sign=RASHI_NAMES[sat_rashi_idx],
        rahu_sign=RASHI_NAMES[rahu_rashi_idx],
        ketu_sign=RASHI_NAMES[ketu_rashi_idx],
        favorable_rashis=favorable_rashis[:4],
        caution_rashis=caution_rashis[:3],
        dasha_spotlight_title=d_title,
        dasha_spotlight_desc=d_desc
    )

    tithi_name_hi = f"{'शुक्ल' if paksha == 'Shukla' else 'कृष्ण'} {TITHI_NAMES_HI[tithi_index]}" if tithi_base_name not in ("Purnima", "Amavasya") else ("पूर्णिमा" if tithi_base_name == "Purnima" else "अमावस्या")
    nakshatra_name_hi = NAKSHATRA_NAMES_HI[nakshatra_index]
    masa_name_hi = MASA_NAMES_HI[masa_idx]
    paksha_hi = "शुक्ल पक्ष" if paksha == "Shukla" else "कृष्ण पक्ष"
    moon_rashi_hi = RASHI_NAMES_HI[moon_rashi_idx]
    sun_rashi_hi = RASHI_NAMES_HI[sun_rashi_idx]
    vara_hi = vara.get("name_hi", vara["name_sa"])
    deity_hi = vara.get("deity_hi", vara["deity"])
    date_formatted_hi = f"{vara_hi}, {target_date.day} {masa_name_hi} {target_date.year}"

    date_formatted = target_date.strftime("%A, %d %B %Y")

    return DailyPanchang(
        date=target_date,
        date_formatted=date_formatted,
        date_formatted_hi=date_formatted_hi,
        weekday_index=weekday_idx,
        vara_sa=vara["name_sa"],
        vara_en=vara["name_en"],
        vara_hi=vara_hi,
        deity=vara["deity"],
        deity_hi=deity_hi,
        color=vara["color"],
        significance=vara["significance"],
        remedy=vara["remedy"],
        paksha=paksha,
        paksha_hi=paksha_hi,
        tithi_index=tithi_index,
        tithi_name=tithi_name,
        tithi_name_hi=tithi_name_hi,
        masa_name=masa_name,
        masa_name_hi=masa_name_hi,
        nakshatra_index=nakshatra_index,
        nakshatra_name=nakshatra_name,
        nakshatra_name_hi=nakshatra_name_hi,
        moon_rashi=moon_rashi,
        moon_rashi_hi=moon_rashi_hi,
        sun_rashi=sun_rashi,
        sun_rashi_hi=sun_rashi_hi,
        sunrise_str=_fmt_time(sunrise_time),
        sunset_str=_fmt_time(sunset_time),
        abhijit_muhurta=abhijit_str,
        rahu_kaal=rahu_str,
        gochar=gochar_snapshot,
        content_theme=content_theme,
        is_festival=is_fest,
        festival_name=fest_name,
        festival_note=fest_note,
        root_number=root_num,
        destiny_number=destiny_num,
        numerology_focus=f"Root {root_num} • Destiny {destiny_num} (Surya Solar Resonance)"
    )

"""Rule-based Background Template Selector for Siddhanta.

Picks the most appropriate, text-free background template based on:
- Active Festival (Navratri, Diwali, Shivratri, Holi, Saraswati Puja, Generic)
- Solar / Lunar Eclipse (Grahan)
- Planetary Transit / Jyotish Insight
- Daily Panchang / Sunrise Riverside
- Muhurta / Sandstone Pillar with Flowers
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
from pathlib import Path
from PIL import Image

ASSETS_TEMPLATES = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "assets" / "templates"
DOWNLOADS_DIR = Path(r"C:\Users\91962\Downloads")

# Template Registry mapping logical themes/festivals to candidate filenames
TEMPLATE_CANDIDATES = {
    "navratri": [
        "navratri_festival.png",
        "durga_festival.png"
    ],
    "diwali": [
        "Diwali Ghat Glow Template.png",
        "diwali_ghat.png"
    ],
    "shivratri": [
        "Shivratri Festive Diptych.png",
        "shivratri.png"
    ],
    "holi": [
        "Holi Festive Diptych.png",
        "holi.png"
    ],
    "saraswati": [
        "saraswati_festival.png",
        "vasant_panchami.png"
    ],
    "eclipse": [
        "Celestial Eclipse Ghat Mandala.png",
        "eclipse_mandala.png"
    ],
    "generic_festival": [
        "Generic festival.png",
        "Golden Temple Sunrise with Diyas and Lotuses.png"
    ],
    "transit": [
        "Celestial Temple Astrology Poster Template.png",
        "transit_template.png"
    ],
    "panchang": [
        "Panchang Temple Riverside Template.png",
        "panchang_template.png",
        "Golden Temple Sunrise with Diyas and Lotuses.png"
    ],
    "plan_day": [
        "plan_day_template.png",
        "Panchang Temple Riverside Template.png"
    ]
}


ASSETS_TEMPLATES = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "assets" / "templates"
DOWNLOADS_DIR = Path(r"C:\Users\91962\Downloads")
REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent

SEARCH_DIRS = [
    ASSETS_TEMPLATES,
    REPO_ROOT / "assets" / "templates",
    REPO_ROOT / "assets",
    REPO_ROOT / "promo_studio" / "assets" / "templates",
    REPO_ROOT / "promo_studio" / "assets",
    DOWNLOADS_DIR,
]


def find_template_file(theme_key: str) -> Path | None:
    """Finds existing template file in project assets, root directory, or Downloads."""
    candidates = TEMPLATE_CANDIDATES.get(theme_key, [])
    for fname in candidates:
        for sdir in SEARCH_DIRS:
            p = sdir / fname
            if p.exists():
                return p

    # Fallback pattern match
    for sdir in SEARCH_DIRS:
        if sdir.exists():
            for candidate in sdir.glob("*.png"):
                if theme_key.lower() in candidate.name.lower():
                    return candidate

    return None


def select_template_for_content(
    post_type: str,
    festival_name: str = "",
    is_eclipse: bool = False
) -> Path | None:
    """Selects the best template path based on festival name and post type."""
    fn_lower = festival_name.lower()

    if is_eclipse:
        found = find_template_file("eclipse")
        if found:
            return found

    if post_type == "festival" or festival_name:
        if "navratri" in fn_lower or "durga" in fn_lower:
            return find_template_file("navratri")
        elif "diwali" in fn_lower or "deepavali" in fn_lower or "dhanteras" in fn_lower:
            return find_template_file("diwali")
        elif "shivratri" in fn_lower or "shiva" in fn_lower:
            return find_template_file("shivratri")
        elif "holi" in fn_lower:
            return find_template_file("holi")
        elif "saraswati" in fn_lower or "vasant" in fn_lower or "basant" in fn_lower:
            return find_template_file("saraswati")
        else:
            # Other festivals: Jivitputrika, Janmashtami, Gudi Padwa, Chhath, etc.
            return find_template_file("generic_festival")

    if post_type == "transit":
        return find_template_file("transit")

    if post_type == "plan_day":
        return find_template_file("plan_day")

    if post_type == "panchang":
        return find_template_file("panchang")

    return None

================================================================================

"""Secrets: environment first, then OS keyring; falls back to a .env file on save."""
from __future__ import annotations

import os

from promo_studio.config.settings import app_home

SERVICE = "promo_studio"


def get_secret(name: str) -> str | None:
    value = os.environ.get(name)
    if value:
        return value
    try:
        import keyring

        return keyring.get_password(SERVICE, name)
    except Exception:
        return None


def set_secret(name: str, value: str) -> str:
    """Store a secret. Returns where it went: 'keyring' or '.env'."""
    os.environ[name] = value
    try:
        import keyring

        keyring.set_password(SERVICE, name, value)
        return "keyring"
    except Exception:
        pass
    env_file = app_home() / ".env"
    lines = env_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if env_file.exists() else []
    lines = [ln for ln in lines if not ln.startswith(f"{name}=")]
    lines.append(f"{name}={value}")
    env_file.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    return ".env"


def mask(value: str | None) -> str:
    if not value:
        return "(not set)"
    return "*" * max(len(value) - 4, 0) + value[-4:]




================================================================================
FILE: .\config\settings.py

"""App home folder, default settings and typed access to stored settings."""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
from pathlib import Path


def app_home() -> Path:
    override = os.environ.get("PROMO_STUDIO_HOME")
    if override:
        home = Path(override)
    elif os.name == "nt":
        home = Path(os.environ.get("APPDATA", str(Path.home()))) / "PromoStudio"
    else:
        home = Path.home() / ".promo_studio"
    home.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return home


def load_env() -> None:
    """Load .env files if python-dotenv is installed (app home first, then cwd)."""
    try:
        from dotenv import load_dotenv
    except ImportError:
        return
    load_dotenv(app_home() / ".env")
    load_dotenv()


# Model names change often: keep them in settings, never hard-coded in logic.
DEFAULTS: dict[str, str] = {
    "ai.provider": "gemini",
    "ai.model": "gemini-2.5-flash",
    "ai.ollama_model": "llama3.1:8b",
    "ai.ollama_url": "http://localhost:11434",
    "ai.variants": "3",
    "ai.calls_per_minute": "10",
    "hashtags.max.x": "2",
    "hashtags.max.instagram": "5",
    "hashtags.max.facebook": "3",
    "hashtags.max.reddit": "0",
    "theme": "system",
    "meta.api_version": "v25.0",
    "post.max_parallel": "4",
    "post.retries": "2",
    "ig.user_id": "siddhanta.app",
    "fb.page_id": "",
    "ig.token_expires": "",
}


class Settings:
    """Thin wrapper over the settings table with defaults."""

    def __init__(self, db):
        self.db = db

    def get(self, key: str) -> str:
        value = self.db.get_setting(key)
        return value if value is not None else DEFAULTS.get(key, "")

    def get_int(self, key: str, fallback: int = 0) -> int:
        try:
            return int(self.get(key))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return fallback

    def set(self, key: str, value: str) -> None:
        self.db.set_setting(key, str(value))

    def hashtag_caps(self) -> dict[str, int]:
        return {p: self.get_int(f"hashtags.max.{p}") for p in ("x", "instagram", "facebook", "reddit")}

================================================================================
FILE: .\core\context.py
