"""Builds AI providers and services from stored settings (used by the UI)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import datetime as dt

from promo_studio.adapters.facebook import FacebookAdapter
from promo_studio.adapters.instagram import InstagramAdapter
from promo_studio.ai.base import AIError, AIProvider
from promo_studio.ai.gemini import GeminiProvider
from promo_studio.ai.ollama import OllamaProvider
from promo_studio.config.secrets import get_secret
from promo_studio.config.settings import Settings
from promo_studio.core.draft_service import DraftService
from promo_studio.core.image_host import ImageHost, ImgBBHost
from promo_studio.core.image_service import ImageService
from promo_studio.core.limiter import RateLimiter
from promo_studio.core.publisher import Publisher


class AppContext:
    def __init__(self, db):
        self.db = db
        self.settings = Settings(db)
        self._limiter: RateLimiter | None = None
        self._limiter_cpm = 0

    def make_provider(self) -> AIProvider:
        s = self.settings
        provider = s.get("ai.provider")
        if provider == "ollama":
            return OllamaProvider(s.get("ai.ollama_model"), s.get("ai.ollama_url"))
        if provider == "gemini":
            return GeminiProvider(get_secret("GEMINI_API_KEY") or "", s.get("ai.model"))
        raise AIError(f"Unknown AI provider: {provider}")

    def _get_limiter(self) -> RateLimiter:
        cpm = self.settings.get_int("ai.calls_per_minute", 10)
        if self._limiter is None or cpm != self._limiter_cpm:
            self._limiter, self._limiter_cpm = RateLimiter(cpm), cpm
        return self._limiter

    def make_draft_service(self) -> DraftService:
        return DraftService(self.make_provider(), self._get_limiter(), self.settings.hashtag_caps())

    # ---- publishing ------------------------------------------------------
    def make_image_service(self) -> ImageService:
        return ImageService()

    def make_image_host(self) -> ImageHost | None:
        key = get_secret("IMAGE_HOST_KEY")
        return ImgBBHost(key) if key else None

    def make_adapters(self) -> dict:
        s, version, adapters = self.settings, self.settings.get("meta.api_version"), {}
        ig_id, ig_token = s.get("ig.user_id").strip(), get_secret("IG_ACCESS_TOKEN")
        if ig_id and ig_token:
            adapters["instagram"] = InstagramAdapter(ig_id, ig_token, version)
        page_id, page_token = s.get("fb.page_id").strip(), get_secret("FB_PAGE_TOKEN")
        if page_id and page_token:
            adapters["facebook"] = FacebookAdapter(page_id, page_token, version)
        return adapters

    def make_publisher(self) -> Publisher:
        s = self.settings
        return Publisher(self.db, self.make_adapters(), self.make_image_service(), self.make_image_host(),
                         max_parallel=s.get_int("post.max_parallel", 4), retries=s.get_int("post.retries", 2))

    def token_warning(self) -> str | None:
        """A friendly warning when the Instagram token is close to expiring."""
        raw = self.settings.get("ig.token_expires")
        try:
            days = (dt.date.fromisoformat(raw) - dt.date.today()).days
        except ValueError:
            return None
        if days < 0:
            return "Your Instagram token has expired. Refresh or regenerate it in Settings."
        return f"Your Instagram token expires in {days} day(s). Refresh it in Settings." if days <= 7 else None




================================================================================
FILE: .\core\draft_service.py
================================================================================
"""Turns a profile + angle into validated, per-platform draft variants."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from promo_studio.ai.base import AIError, AIProvider, RateLimitError
from promo_studio.ai.prompts import SYSTEM_PROMPT, build_user_prompt
from promo_studio.core.validators import DEFAULT_CAPS, PLATFORMS, char_count, normalize_hashtags, validate_post


class DraftError(Exception):
    """Drafting failed; the message is safe to show to the user."""


@dataclass
class DraftResult:
    variants: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    warnings: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)


def parse_json(text: str) -> dict:
    """Parse model output, tolerating markdown fences and stray text around the JSON."""
    cleaned = re.sub(r"```(?:json)?", "", text or "").strip()
    start, end = cleaned.find("{"), cleaned.rfind("}")
    if start == -1 or end <= start:
        raise ValueError("no JSON object found")
    data = json.loads(cleaned[start:end + 1])
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        raise ValueError("JSON is not an object")
    return data


class DraftService:
    def __init__(self, provider: AIProvider, limiter=None, hashtag_caps: dict | None = None,
                 retries: int = 3, sleep=time.sleep):
        self.provider, self.limiter = provider, limiter
        self.caps = {**DEFAULT_CAPS, **(hashtag_caps or {})}
        self.retries, self._sleep = retries, sleep

    # ---- public ---------------------------------------------------------
    def generate(self, profile, angle: str, base_text: str = "", note: str = "",
                 n: int = 3, platforms=PLATFORMS) -> DraftResult:
        platforms = tuple(platforms)
        user = build_user_prompt(profile, angle, base_text, note, n, platforms)
        data = None
        for attempt in range(2):  # one repair attempt for invalid JSON
            prompt = user if attempt == 0 else user + "\n\nYour last reply was not valid JSON. Return valid JSON only."
            raw = self._call(SYSTEM_PROMPT, prompt)
            try:
                data = parse_json(raw)
                break
            except ValueError:
                continue
        if data is None:
            raise DraftError("The AI did not return valid JSON twice. Try again or switch provider.")
        variants = self._clean(data, profile, angle, platforms)
        if not variants:
            raise DraftError("The AI reply had no usable variants. Try again.")
        result = DraftResult(variants=variants)
        if len(variants) < n:
            result.warnings.append(f"Asked for {n} variants, got {len(variants)}.")
        return result

    def shorten(self, platform: str, text: str, max_chars: int) -> str:
        """Ask the AI to rewrite `text` so it fits `max_chars`; verified in code before returning."""
        system = ("You shorten marketing copy. Keep the meaning, tone and any links. Never add claims "
                  'or facts. Return ONLY JSON: {"text": "..."}')
        user = f"Platform: {platform}. Rewrite to at most {max_chars} characters (hard limit).\n\nTEXT:\n{text}"
        for attempt in range(2):
            prompt = user if attempt == 0 else (
                user + f"\n\nYour last reply was invalid or longer than {max_chars} characters. Make it shorter.")
            try:
                candidate = str(parse_json(self._call(system, prompt)).get("text", "")).strip()
            except ValueError:
                continue
            if candidate and char_count(platform, candidate) <= max_chars:
                return candidate
        raise DraftError("Could not shorten it enough. Trim it by hand.")

    # ---- internals ------------------------------------------------------
    def _call(self, system: str, user: str) -> str:
        for attempt in range(self.retries):
            if self.limiter:
                self.limiter.acquire()
            try:
                return self.provider.generate(system, user)
            except RateLimitError:
                if attempt == self.retries - 1:
                    raise DraftError("AI rate limit reached. Wait a minute or switch to Ollama in Settings.")
                self._sleep(2 ** attempt)
            except AIError as exc:
                raise DraftError(f"AI provider error: {exc}") from exc
        raise DraftError("AI call failed.")

    def _clean(self, data: dict, profile, angle: str, platforms) -> list[dict]:
        cleaned = []
        for raw in data.get("variants") or []:
            if not isinstance(raw, dict):
                continue
            variant: dict = {"angle": str(raw.get("angle") or angle)}
            for platform in platforms:
                block = raw.get(platform)
                if not isinstance(block, dict):
                    continue
                variant[platform] = self._clean_platform(platform, block, profile)
            if any(p in variant for p in platforms):
                cleaned.append(variant)
        return cleaned

    def _clean_platform(self, platform: str, block: dict, profile) -> dict:
        tags = normalize_hashtags(block.get("hashtags"), self.caps.get(platform, 0))
        if platform == "reddit":
            title = str(block.get("title") or "").strip()
            text = str(block.get("body") or block.get("text") or "").strip()
            subs = [str(s).strip().removeprefix("r/") for s in block.get("suggested_subreddits") or []]
            item = {"title": title, "text": text, "hashtags": [], "subreddits": subs}
        else:
            text = str(block.get("caption") or block.get("text") or "").strip()
            item = {"text": text, "hashtags": tags}
            title = None
        item["problems"] = validate_post(
            platform, text, item["hashtags"], title=title, banned=profile.banned_phrases)
        return item




================================================================================
FILE: .\core\image_host.py
================================================================================
"""Temporary public hosting for images (Instagram fetches the image from a public URL)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import base64
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod

import requests


class ImageHostError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, message: str, retryable: bool = False):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.retryable = retryable


class ImageHost(ABC):
    @abstractmethod
    def upload(self, path: str) -> str:
        """Upload the file and return a public https URL."""


class ImgBBHost(ImageHost):
    """ImgBB free API. Uploads auto-expire, so nothing has to be deleted afterwards."""

    ENDPOINT = "https://api.imgbb.com/1/upload"

    def __init__(self, api_key: str, expiration: int = 3600, http=requests, timeout: int = 60):
        self.api_key, self.expiration, self.http, self.timeout = api_key, expiration, http, timeout

    def upload(self, path: str) -> str:
        if not self.api_key:
            raise ImageHostError("Image host API key is missing. Add it in Settings.")
        try:
            with open(path, "rb") as fh:
                encoded = base64.b64encode(fh.read())
        except OSError as exc:
            raise ImageHostError("Could not read the image file.") from exc
        try:
            response = self.http.request("POST", self.ENDPOINT, params={"key": self.api_key},
                                         data={"image": encoded, "expiration": self.expiration},
                                         timeout=self.timeout)
        except requests.RequestException as exc:  # message deliberately excludes the URL (it holds the key)
            raise ImageHostError(f"Image upload failed ({type(exc).__name__}).", retryable=True) from None
        try:
            body = response.json()
        except ValueError:
            body = {}
        url = (body.get("data") or {}).get("url") if isinstance(body, dict) else None
        if response.status_code >= 400 or not url:
            raise ImageHostError(f"Image host rejected the upload (HTTP {response.status_code}).",
                                 retryable=response.status_code >= 500)
        return url




================================================================================
FILE: .\core\image_service.py
================================================================================
"""Per-platform image preparation with Pillow. Originals are never modified."""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import tempfile
import uuid
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

from PIL import Image, ImageOps


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Rule:
    min_ratio: float | None = None   # width / height
    max_ratio: float | None = None
    max_width: int = 2048
    min_width: int = 0
    max_bytes: int | None = None


# Working defaults: verify against each platform's current image specs.
RULES = {
    "instagram": Rule(min_ratio=0.8, max_ratio=1.91, max_width=1080, min_width=320, max_bytes=8_000_000),
    "facebook": Rule(max_width=2048),
    "x": Rule(max_width=1600, max_bytes=5_000_000),
    "reddit": Rule(max_width=2048),
}
_MARGIN = 0.005  # stay slightly inside the ratio limits so rounding never pushes us out


class ImageService:
    def __init__(self, temp_dir=None):
        self.temp_dir = Path(temp_dir) if temp_dir else Path(tempfile.gettempdir()) / "promo_studio"
        self.temp_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    def check(self, platform: str, path: str) -> list[str]:
        if not path or not os.path.exists(path):
            return ["Image file not found."]
        try:
            with Image.open(path) as im:
                width = im.size[0]
        except Exception:
            return ["The image could not be opened."]
        rule = RULES[platform]
        if width < rule.min_width:
            return [f"Image is {width}px wide; {platform} needs at least {rule.min_width}px."]
        return []

    def prepare(self, platform: str, path: str) -> str:
        rule = RULES[platform]
        with Image.open(path) as opened:
            image = self._flatten(ImageOps.exif_transpose(opened))
        image = self._fit_ratio(image, rule)
        if image.width > rule.max_width:
            height = max(1, round(image.height * rule.max_width / image.width))
            image = image.resize((rule.max_width, height), Image.LANCZOS)
        out = self.temp_dir / f"{platform}_{uuid.uuid4().hex}.jpg"
        quality = 90
        while True:
            image.save(out, "JPEG", quality=quality, optimize=True)
            if not rule.max_bytes or out.stat().st_size <= rule.max_bytes or quality <= 50:
                break
            quality -= 10
        return str(out)

    def discard(self, path: str | None) -> None:
        """Delete a prepared temp file (only inside our temp folder)."""
        try:
            if path and Path(path).parent == self.temp_dir:
                os.remove(path)
        except OSError:
            pass

    @staticmethod
    def _flatten(image: Image.Image) -> Image.Image:
        if image.mode in ("RGBA", "LA") or "transparency" in image.info:
            rgba = image.convert("RGBA")
            background = Image.new("RGB", rgba.size, "white")
            background.paste(rgba, mask=rgba.split()[-1])
            return background
        return image.convert("RGB")

    @staticmethod
    def _fit_ratio(image: Image.Image, rule: Rule) -> Image.Image:
        w, h = image.size
        ratio = w / h
        if rule.min_ratio and ratio < rule.min_ratio:
            new_h = int(w / (rule.min_ratio * (1 + _MARGIN)))
            top = (h - new_h) // 2
            return image.crop((0, top, w, top + new_h))
        if rule.max_ratio and ratio > rule.max_ratio:
            new_w = int(h * rule.max_ratio * (1 - _MARGIN))
            left = (w - new_w) // 2
            return image.crop((left, 0, left + new_w, h))
        return image




================================================================================
FILE: .\core\limiter.py
================================================================================
from __future__ import annotations

import threading
import time


class RateLimiter:
    """Blocks so that no more than `calls_per_minute` calls start in any 60 seconds."""

    def __init__(self, calls_per_minute: int, clock=time.monotonic, sleep=time.sleep):
        self.capacity = max(int(calls_per_minute), 1)
        self._clock, self._sleep = clock, sleep
        self._stamps: list[float] = []
        self._lock = threading.Lock()

    def acquire(self) -> None:
        while True:
            with self._lock:
                now = self._clock()
                self._stamps = [t for t in self._stamps if now - t < 60]
                if len(self._stamps) < self.capacity:
                    self._stamps.append(now)
                    return
                wait = 60 - (now - self._stamps[0])
            self._sleep(max(wait, 0.05))




================================================================================
FILE: .\core\logsafe.py
================================================================================
"""Keep secrets out of log files: redact known keys, tokens and credential-looking values."""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import os
import re

SECRET_ENV_NAMES = (
    "GEMINI_API_KEY", "META_APP_SECRET", "FB_PAGE_TOKEN", "IG_ACCESS_TOKEN", "X_API_KEY", "X_API_SECRET",
    "X_ACCESS_TOKEN", "X_ACCESS_SECRET", "REDDIT_CLIENT_SECRET", "REDDIT_PASSWORD", "IMAGE_HOST_KEY",
)
MASK = "[REDACTED]"
_PATTERNS = [
    (re.compile(r"AIza[0-9A-Za-z_\-]{20,}"), MASK),
    (re.compile(r"(?i)(bearer\s+)[A-Za-z0-9._\-]{8,}"), r"\1" + MASK),
    (re.compile(r"(?i)\b(access_token|refresh_token|token|api_key|apikey|key|password|secret|client_secret)=([^&\s\"']+)"),
     r"\1=" + MASK),
]


def redact(text: str, extra_secrets=()) -> str:
    for secret in extra_secrets:
        if secret and len(secret) >= 6:
            text = text.replace(secret, MASK)
    for pattern, repl in _PATTERNS:
        text = pattern.sub(repl, text)
    return text


class RedactFilter(logging.Filter):
    """Attach to a handler: rewrites the message and any traceback before they are written."""

    @staticmethod
    def _secrets() -> list[str]:
        return [os.environ.get(name, "") for name in SECRET_ENV_NAMES]

    def filter(self, record: logging.LogRecord) -> bool:
        secrets = self._secrets()
        try:
            message = record.getMessage()
        except Exception:
            message = str(record.msg)
        record.msg, record.args = redact(message, secrets), None
        if record.exc_info and not record.exc_text:
            record.exc_text = redact(logging.Formatter().formatException(record.exc_info), secrets)
        return True




================================================================================
FILE: .\core\publisher.py
================================================================================
"""Fans one approved post out to every platform adapter in parallel and records each outcome."""
from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
import logging
import time
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed

from promo_studio.adapters.base import PostPayload, PostResult
from promo_studio.core.image_host import ImageHostError
from promo_studio.core.validators import compose_text

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
OPEN_STATUSES = ("draft", "scheduled", "failed")  # statuses the Publisher will (re)try


class Publisher:
    def __init__(self, db, adapters: dict, image_service, image_host=None, max_parallel: int = 4,
                 retries: int = 2, sleep=time.sleep):
        self.db, self.adapters = db, adapters
        self.image_service, self.image_host = image_service, image_host
        self.max_parallel, self.retries, self._sleep = max(1, max_parallel), max(0, retries), sleep

    # ---- creating a post ---------------------------------------------------
    def create_post(self, profile_id, angle: str, base_text: str, image_path: str | None, drafts: list[dict]) -> int:
        """drafts: [{"platform", "text", "hashtags", "title"?, "extra"?}] with text WITHOUT hashtags."""
        post_id = self.db.create_post(profile_id, angle, base_text, image_path)
        for d in drafts:
            self.db.add_target(post_id, d["platform"], d["text"], d.get("hashtags", []), d.get("title"), d.get("extra"))
        return post_id

    # ---- publishing --------------------------------------------------------
    def publish(self, post_id: int, only=None, on_event=None) -> dict[str, PostResult]:
        emit = on_event or (lambda event: None)
        post = self.db.get_post(post_id)
        image = (post or {}).get("image_path") or None
        targets = [t for t in self.db.list_targets(post_id, OPEN_STATUSES) if not only or t.platform in only]
        results: dict[str, PostResult] = {}
        ready = []
        for target in targets:
            adapter = self.adapters.get(target.platform)
            payload = PostPayload(text=self._final_text(target), image_path=image, title=target.title,
                                  extra=dict(target.extra))
            problems = self._problems(target, adapter, payload, image)
            if problems:
                results[target.platform] = self._blocked(target, problems, emit)
            else:
                ready.append((target, adapter, payload))

        if ready:
            with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=self.max_parallel) as pool:
                futures = {pool.submit(self._run_target, t, a, p, emit): t for t, a, p in ready}
                for future in as_completed(futures):
                    results[futures[future].platform] = future.result()

        status = self._update_post_status(post_id)
        emit({"type": "done", "post_status": status})
        return results

    # ---- internals ---------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def _final_text(target) -> str:
        return target.text if target.platform == "reddit" else compose_text(target.platform, target.text, target.hashtags)

    def _problems(self, target, adapter, payload, image) -> list[str]:
        if adapter is None:
            return ["Not configured. Add this account's credentials in Settings."]
        problems = list(adapter.validate(payload))
        if image:
            problems += self.image_service.check(target.platform, image)
            if adapter.needs_public_image and self.image_host is None:
                problems.append("Image host is not configured. Add its API key in Settings.")
        return problems

    def _blocked(self, target, problems: list[str], emit) -> PostResult:
        message = " ".join(problems)
        self.db.add_result(target.id, False, error_code="VALIDATION", error_message=message)
        self.db.set_target_status(target.id, "failed")
        emit({"type": "status", "platform": target.platform, "status": "blocked", "message": message})
        return PostResult(target.platform, False, error_code="VALIDATION", error_message=message)

    def _run_target(self, target, adapter, payload: PostPayload, emit) -> PostResult:
        fresh = self.db.get_target(target.id)
        if fresh and fresh.status == "posted":  # idempotency: never post the same target twice
            last = self.db.last_result(target.id) or {}
            return PostResult(target.platform, True, post_id=last.get("remote_id"), url=last.get("url"))
        self.db.set_target_status(target.id, "posting")
        emit({"type": "status", "platform": target.platform, "status": "posting"})

        result, attempts = None, 0
        for attempt in range(1 + self.retries):
            attempts += 1
            result = self._attempt(target.platform, adapter, payload)
            if result.ok or not result.retryable or result.needs_check:
                break
            if attempt < self.retries:
                self._sleep(min(2 ** (attempt + 1), 60))

        status = "posted" if result.ok else ("needs_check" if result.needs_check else "failed")
        self.db.add_result(target.id, result.ok, result.post_id, result.url, result.error_code, result.error_message)
        self.db.set_target_status(target.id, status, attempts)
        emit({"type": "status", "platform": target.platform, "status": status, "url": result.url,
              "message": result.error_message, "attempts": attempts})
        return result

    def _attempt(self, platform: str, adapter, base: PostPayload) -> PostResult:
        payload = dataclasses.replace(base)
        prepared = None
        try:
            if base.image_path:
                prepared = self.image_service.prepare(platform, base.image_path)
                if adapter.needs_public_image:
                    payload.image_url = self.image_host.upload(prepared)
                    payload.image_path = None
                else:
                    payload.image_path = prepared
            return adapter.post(payload)
        except ImageHostError as exc:
            return PostResult(platform, False, error_code="NETWORK" if exc.retryable else "VALIDATION",
                              error_message=str(exc), retryable=exc.retryable)
        except (ValueError, OSError) as exc:
            log.warning("Image handling failed for %s: %s", platform, exc)
            return PostResult(platform, False, error_code="VALIDATION", error_message="The image could not be prepared.")
        except Exception:
            log.exception("Unexpected error posting to %s", platform)
            return PostResult(platform, False, error_code="UNKNOWN",
                              error_message="Unexpected error. Details are in the log file.")
        finally:
            self.image_service.discard(prepared)

    def _update_post_status(self, post_id: int) -> str:
        statuses = [t.status for t in self.db.list_targets(post_id)]
        if statuses and all(s == "posted" for s in statuses):
            status = "posted"
        elif "posted" in statuses:
            status = "partial"
        else:
            status = "failed"
        self.db.set_post_status(post_id, status)
        return status




================================================================================
FILE: .\core\textutil.py
================================================================================
"""Small parsing helpers for form fields."""
from __future__ import annotations


def parse_csv(text: str) -> list[str]:
    return [p.strip() for p in (text or "").replace("\n", ",").split(",") if p.strip()]


def parse_lines(text: str) -> list[str]:
    return [ln.strip() for ln in (text or "").splitlines() if ln.strip()]


def parse_links(text: str) -> dict[str, str]:
    """'ios=https://...' per line -> {'ios': 'https://...'}."""
    links = {}
    for line in parse_lines(text):
        key, sep, value = line.partition("=")
        if sep and key.strip() and value.strip():
            links[key.strip()] = value.strip()
    return links


def format_links(links: dict[str, str]) -> str:
    return "\n".join(f"{k}={v}" for k, v in (links or {}).items())




================================================================================
FILE: .\core\validators.py
================================================================================
"""Code-side checks. The AI suggests; these functions decide what is valid."""
from __future__ import annotations

import re

PLATFORMS = ("facebook", "instagram", "x", "reddit")

# Working defaults from the wiki: verify against each platform's current rules.
LIMITS = {"x": 280, "instagram": 2200, "facebook": 63206, "reddit": 40000}
REDDIT_TITLE_LIMIT = 300
INSTAGRAM_MAX_HASHTAGS = 30
X_URL_LENGTH = 23  # X counts every link as a fixed length
DEFAULT_CAPS = {"x": 2, "instagram": 5, "facebook": 3, "reddit": 0}

_URL_RE = re.compile(r"https?://\S+")


def normalize_hashtags(tags, cap: int) -> list[str]:
    """Strip '#', spaces and symbols, lowercase, de-duplicate (order kept), cap the count."""
    if cap <= 0:
        return []
    out: list[str] = []
    for tag in tags or []:
        if not isinstance(tag, str):
            continue
        clean = re.sub(r"[^\w]", "", tag.lstrip("#").replace(" ", "")).lower()
        if clean and clean not in out:
            out.append(clean)
    return out[:cap]


def compose_text(platform: str, text: str, hashtags) -> str:
    """The text as it will be published: body plus hashtags."""
    body = (text or "").strip()
    tags = " ".join(f"#{t}" for t in hashtags or [])
    if not tags:
        return body
    return f"{body}{' ' if platform == 'x' else chr(10) * 2}{tags}"


def char_count(platform: str, text: str, hashtags=()) -> int:
    full = compose_text(platform, text, hashtags)
    if platform == "x":
        full = _URL_RE.sub("x" * X_URL_LENGTH, full)
    return len(full)


def limit_for(platform: str) -> int:
    return LIMITS[platform]


def counter_state(used: int, limit: int) -> str:
    """'ok', 'warn' (>= 90% of the limit) or 'over'."""
    if used > limit:
        return "over"
    return "warn" if used >= 0.9 * limit else "ok"


def find_banned(text: str, phrases) -> list[str]:
    lower = (text or "").lower()
    return [p for p in phrases or [] if p.strip() and p.strip().lower() in lower]


def validate_post(platform: str, text: str, hashtags=(), *, title: str | None = None,
                  banned=(), has_image: bool | None = None) -> list[str]:
    """Return human-readable problems; an empty list means the post is valid.

    has_image=None means "unknown" and skips image rules.
    """
    problems: list[str] = []
    if not (text or "").strip():
        problems.append("Text is empty.")
    used, limit = char_count(platform, text, hashtags), LIMITS[platform]
    if used > limit:
        problems.append(f"Over the limit by {used - limit} characters ({used}/{limit}).")
    if platform == "instagram":
        if has_image is False:
            problems.append("Instagram needs an image.")
        if len(list(hashtags or [])) > INSTAGRAM_MAX_HASHTAGS:
            problems.append(f"More than {INSTAGRAM_MAX_HASHTAGS} hashtags.")
    if platform == "reddit":
        if not (title or "").strip():
            problems.append("Reddit needs a title.")
        elif len(title) > REDDIT_TITLE_LIMIT:
            problems.append(f"Title is over {REDDIT_TITLE_LIMIT} characters.")
    combined = f"{title or ''} {compose_text(platform, text, hashtags)}"
    for phrase in find_banned(combined, banned):
        problems.append(f'Contains banned phrase: "{phrase}".')
    return problems

================================================================================
FILE: .\ui\app.py

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from logging.handlers import RotatingFileHandler

import customtkinter as ctk

from promo_studio import __version__
from promo_studio.config.settings import app_home, load_env
from promo_studio.core.context import AppContext
from promo_studio.core.logsafe import RedactFilter
from promo_studio.data.db import Database
from promo_studio.ui.compose_tab import ComposeTab
from promo_studio.ui.profiles_tab import ProfilesTab
from promo_studio.ui.settings_tab import SettingsTab


class App(ctk.CTk):
    def __init__(self, ctx: AppContext):
        super().__init__()
        self.ctx = ctx
        ctk.set_appearance_mode(ctx.settings.get("theme") or "system")
        self.title(f"Social Promo Studio {__version__}")
        self.geometry("1240x820")
        self.minsize(1000, 680)

        self.tabs = ctk.CTkTabview(self, command=self._on_tab)
        self.tabs.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=8, pady=8)
        for name in ("Compose", "Profiles", "ASO", "Queue", "Settings"):
            self.tabs.add(name)
        self.compose = ComposeTab(self.tabs.tab("Compose"), ctx)
        self.compose.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        ProfilesTab(self.tabs.tab("Profiles"), ctx, on_change=self.compose.refresh_profiles).pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        for name, phase in (("ASO", "phase 4"), ("Queue", "phase 3")):
            ctk.CTkLabel(self.tabs.tab(name), text=f"{name} arrives in {phase} of the roadmap.",
                         text_color="gray50").pack(pady=40)
        SettingsTab(self.tabs.tab("Settings"), ctx).pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        self.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", self._on_close)
        stale = ctx.db.mark_stale_posting()
        notes = [m for m in (
            f"{stale} post(s) were interrupted last time and may or may not have gone out: check the platforms "
            "before posting again." if stale else None, ctx.token_warning()) if m]
        if notes:
            self.compose.status.configure(text=" ".join(notes))

    def _on_close(self) -> None:
        try:
            self.compose.save_draft(announce=False)
        finally:
            self.destroy()

    def _on_tab(self) -> None:
        if self.tabs.get() == "Compose":
            self.compose.refresh_profiles()


def _setup_logging() -> None:
    handler = RotatingFileHandler(app_home() / "app.log", maxBytes=1_000_000, backupCount=5, encoding="utf-8")
    handler.addFilter(RedactFilter())
    handler.setFormatter(logging.Formatter("%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(name)s: %(message)s"))
    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, handlers=[handler])


def run() -> None:
    load_env()
    _setup_logging()
    db = Database(app_home() / "promo_studio.db")
    App(AppContext(db)).mainloop()




================================================================================
FILE: .\ui\compose_tab.py
================================================================================
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import os
import queue
import threading
from tkinter import filedialog

import customtkinter as ctk
from PIL import Image

from promo_studio.ai.base import AIError
from promo_studio.ai.prompts import ANGLES
from promo_studio.core.draft_service import DraftError
from promo_studio.ui.preview_dialog import PreviewDialog
from promo_studio.ui.results_strip import ResultsStrip
from promo_studio.core.validators import (
    PLATFORMS, char_count, compose_text, counter_state, limit_for, normalize_hashtags, validate_post)

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
POSTABLE = ('facebook', 'instagram')  # X arrives in phase 3; Reddit stays copy-only
LABELS = {"facebook": "Facebook", "instagram": "Instagram", "x": "X", "reddit": "Reddit"}
COLORS = {"ok": ("gray35", "gray70"), "warn": "#d98e04", "over": "#d64545"}


class PlatformCard(ctk.CTkFrame):
    """One editable draft per platform, with variant switcher, live counter and checks."""

    def __init__(self, master, platform: str, on_shorten=None):
        super().__init__(master)
        self.platform, self.blocks, self.index = platform, [], 0
        self.on_shorten = on_shorten
        self.banned: list[str] = []
        self.has_image: bool | None = None
        self.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)

        head = ctk.CTkFrame(self, fg_color="transparent")
        head.grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="ew", padx=10, pady=(8, 2))
        self.enabled = ctk.BooleanVar(value=True)
        ctk.CTkCheckBox(head, text=LABELS[platform], variable=self.enabled,
                        font=ctk.CTkFont(weight="bold")).pack(side="left")
        self.next_btn = ctk.CTkButton(head, text=">", width=28, command=lambda: self._step(1))
        self.next_btn.pack(side="right")
        self.pos_label = ctk.CTkLabel(head, text="0 / 0", width=48)
        self.pos_label.pack(side="right")
        self.prev_btn = ctk.CTkButton(head, text="<", width=28, command=lambda: self._step(-1))
        self.prev_btn.pack(side="right")

        row = 1
        self.title_entry = None
        if platform == "reddit":
            self.title_entry = ctk.CTkEntry(self, placeholder_text="Title")
            self.title_entry.grid(row=row, column=0, sticky="ew", padx=10, pady=2)
            self.title_entry.bind("<KeyRelease>", self.refresh)
            row += 1
        self.textbox = ctk.CTkTextbox(self, height=110, wrap="word")
        self.textbox.grid(row=row, column=0, sticky="ew", padx=10, pady=2)
        self.textbox.bind("<KeyRelease>", self.refresh)
        row += 1
        self.tags_entry = None
        if platform != "reddit":
            self.tags_entry = ctk.CTkEntry(self, placeholder_text="hashtags, comma separated (no #)")
            self.tags_entry.grid(row=row, column=0, sticky="ew", padx=10, pady=2)
            self.tags_entry.bind("<KeyRelease>", self.refresh)
            row += 1
        foot = ctk.CTkFrame(self, fg_color="transparent")
        foot.grid(row=row, column=0, sticky="ew", padx=10, pady=(2, 2))
        self.counter = ctk.CTkLabel(foot, text="")
        self.counter.pack(side="left")
        ctk.CTkButton(foot, text="Copy", width=60, command=self.copy).pack(side="right")
        ctk.CTkButton(foot, text="Shorten", width=70, fg_color="gray40",
                      command=lambda: self.on_shorten and self.on_shorten(self)).pack(side="right", padx=6)
        self.problems = ctk.CTkLabel(self, text="", justify="left", anchor="w", wraplength=420,
                                     text_color=COLORS["over"])
        self.problems.grid(row=row + 1, column=0, sticky="ew", padx=10, pady=(0, 8))
        self._update_nav()
        self.refresh()

    # ---- data -----------------------------------------------------------
    def load_variants(self, variants: list[dict]) -> None:
        self.blocks = [v[self.platform] for v in variants if self.platform in v]
        self.index = 0
        self._show()

    def set_context(self, banned: list[str], has_image: bool | None) -> None:
        self.banned, self.has_image = banned, has_image
        self.refresh()

    def parts(self) -> tuple[str, str, list[str]]:
        title = self.title_entry.get().strip() if self.title_entry else ""
        text = self.textbox.get("1.0", "end").strip()
        raw = self.tags_entry.get().replace("#", "") if self.tags_entry else ""
        tags = normalize_hashtags(raw.split(","), 30) if raw.strip() else []
        return title, text, tags

    def final_text(self) -> str:
        title, text, tags = self.parts()
        body = compose_text(self.platform, text, tags)
        return f"{title}\n\n{body}" if title else body

    # ---- ui -------------------------------------------------------------
    def _step(self, delta: int) -> None:
        if self.blocks:
            self.index = (self.index + delta) % len(self.blocks)
            self._show()

    def _show(self) -> None:
        block = self.blocks[self.index] if self.blocks else None
        self.textbox.delete("1.0", "end")
        if block:
            self.textbox.insert("1.0", block["text"])
            if self.title_entry:
                self.title_entry.delete(0, "end")
                self.title_entry.insert(0, block.get("title", ""))
            if self.tags_entry:
                self.tags_entry.delete(0, "end")
                self.tags_entry.insert(0, ", ".join(block["hashtags"]))
        self._update_nav()
        self.refresh()

    def _update_nav(self) -> None:
        total = len(self.blocks)
        self.pos_label.configure(text=f"{self.index + 1 if total else 0} / {total}")
        state = "normal" if total > 1 else "disabled"
        self.prev_btn.configure(state=state)
        self.next_btn.configure(state=state)

    def refresh(self, _event=None) -> None:
        title, text, tags = self.parts()
        used, limit = char_count(self.platform, text, tags), limit_for(self.platform)
        label = f"{used} characters" if limit > 5000 else f"{used} / {limit}"
        self.counter.configure(text=label, text_color=COLORS[counter_state(used, limit)])
        problems = validate_post(self.platform, text, tags, title=title, banned=self.banned,
                                 has_image=self.has_image) if text or title else []
        self.problems.configure(text="\n".join(problems))

    def copy(self) -> None:
        self.clipboard_clear()
        self.clipboard_append(self.final_text())

    def set_text(self, text: str) -> None:
        self.textbox.delete("1.0", "end")
        self.textbox.insert("1.0", text)
        self.refresh()

    def state(self) -> dict:
        title = self.title_entry.get() if self.title_entry else ""
        tags = self.tags_entry.get() if self.tags_entry else ""
        return {"enabled": self.enabled.get(), "title": title, "tags": tags,
                "text": self.textbox.get("1.0", "end").strip()}

    def apply_state(self, data: dict) -> None:
        self.enabled.set(bool(data.get("enabled", True)))
        self.textbox.delete("1.0", "end")
        self.textbox.insert("1.0", data.get("text", ""))
        if self.title_entry:
            self.title_entry.delete(0, "end")
            self.title_entry.insert(0, data.get("title", ""))
        if self.tags_entry:
            self.tags_entry.delete(0, "end")
            self.tags_entry.insert(0, data.get("tags", ""))
        self.refresh()

    def clear(self) -> None:
        self.blocks, self.index = [], 0
        self.apply_state({})
        self._update_nav()


DRAFT_KEY = "draft.compose"


class ComposeTab(ctk.CTkFrame):
    def __init__(self, master, ctx):
        super().__init__(master, fg_color="transparent")
        self.ctx, self.profiles, self.image_path = ctx, [], None
        self.results: queue.Queue = queue.Queue()
        self._gen_id, self._busy, self._last_saved = 0, False, ""
        self._posting, self.current_post_id = False, None
        self.columnconfigure(0, weight=2, uniform="c")
        self.columnconfigure(1, weight=3, uniform="c")
        self.rowconfigure(1, weight=1)

        bar = ctk.CTkFrame(self)
        bar.grid(row=0, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="ew", padx=6, pady=6)
        ctk.CTkLabel(bar, text="App").pack(side="left", padx=(10, 4))
        self.profile_menu = ctk.CTkOptionMenu(bar, values=["(no profiles)"], command=self._on_profile, width=170)
        self.profile_menu.pack(side="left", padx=4)
        ctk.CTkLabel(bar, text="Angle").pack(side="left", padx=(14, 4))
        self.angle_menu = ctk.CTkOptionMenu(bar, values=[a.replace("_", " ") for a in ANGLES], width=170)
        self.angle_menu.pack(side="left", padx=4)
        ctk.CTkLabel(bar, text="Variants").pack(side="left", padx=(14, 4))
        self.variants_menu = ctk.CTkOptionMenu(bar, values=["1", "2", "3", "4", "5"], width=60)
        self.variants_menu.pack(side="left", padx=4)
        ctk.CTkButton(bar, text="Clear all", width=80, fg_color="gray40", command=self.clear_all).pack(side="right", padx=8)
        ctk.CTkButton(bar, text="Save draft", width=90, fg_color="gray40", command=self.save_draft).pack(side="right")

        left = ctk.CTkFrame(self)
        left.grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="nsew", padx=6, pady=(0, 6))
        left.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
        left.rowconfigure(1, weight=1)
        ctk.CTkLabel(left, text="Base idea (optional)", anchor="w").grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="ew", padx=10, pady=(10, 0))
        self.base_text = ctk.CTkTextbox(left, wrap="word")
        self.base_text.grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="nsew", padx=10, pady=4)
        ctk.CTkLabel(left, text="Note to the AI (optional)", anchor="w").grid(row=2, column=0, sticky="ew", padx=10)
        self.note = ctk.CTkEntry(left, placeholder_text="e.g. mention the new sleep sounds")
        self.note.grid(row=3, column=0, sticky="ew", padx=10, pady=4)
        img_row = ctk.CTkFrame(left, fg_color="transparent")
        img_row.grid(row=4, column=0, sticky="ew", padx=10, pady=4)
        ctk.CTkButton(img_row, text="Choose image", width=110, command=self._choose_image).pack(side="left")
        ctk.CTkButton(img_row, text="Clear", width=60, command=self._clear_image,
                      fg_color="gray40").pack(side="left", padx=6)
        self.image_label = ctk.CTkLabel(left, text="No image", height=110)
        self.image_label.grid(row=5, column=0, padx=10, pady=4)
        self.generate_btn = ctk.CTkButton(left, text="Generate with AI", command=self.generate, height=36)
        self.generate_btn.grid(row=6, column=0, sticky="ew", padx=10, pady=(4, 4))
        self.status = ctk.CTkLabel(left, text="", wraplength=380, justify="left", anchor="w")
        self.status.grid(row=7, column=0, sticky="ew", padx=10, pady=(0, 10))

        right = ctk.CTkScrollableFrame(self)
        right.grid(row=1, column=1, sticky="nsew", padx=6, pady=(0, 6))
        right.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
        self.cards = {}
        for i, platform in enumerate(PLATFORMS):
            card = PlatformCard(right, platform, on_shorten=self._shorten)
            card.grid(row=i, column=0, sticky="ew", pady=4)
            self.cards[platform] = card
        bottom = ctk.CTkFrame(self)
        bottom.grid(row=2, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="ew", padx=6, pady=(0, 6))
        bottom.columnconfigure(1, weight=1)
        btns = ctk.CTkFrame(bottom, fg_color="transparent")
        btns.grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="n", padx=8, pady=8)
        ctk.CTkButton(btns, text="Preview all", width=120, fg_color="gray40", command=self.preview).pack(pady=2)
        self.post_btn = ctk.CTkButton(btns, text="Post now", width=120, command=self.post_now)
        self.post_btn.pack(pady=2)
        self.strip = ResultsStrip(bottom, on_retry=self._retry)
        self.strip.grid(row=0, column=1, sticky="ew", padx=8, pady=8)
        top = self.winfo_toplevel()
        top.bind("<Control-Return>", lambda e: self.generate())
        top.bind("<Control-s>", lambda e: self.save_draft())
        top.bind("<Control-Shift-Return>", lambda e: self.post_now())
        self.variants_menu.set(str(self.ctx.settings.get_int("ai.variants", 3)))
        self.refresh_profiles()
        self._restore_draft()
        self.after(100, self._poll)
        self.after(30_000, self._autosave_tick)

    # ---- profiles / image -----------------------------------------------
    def refresh_profiles(self) -> None:
        current = self.profile_menu.get()
        self.profiles = self.ctx.db.list_profiles()
        names = [p.name for p in self.profiles] or ["(no profiles)"]
        self.profile_menu.configure(values=names)
        self.profile_menu.set(current if current in names else names[0])
        self._on_profile()

    def current_profile(self):
        name = self.profile_menu.get()
        return next((p for p in self.profiles if p.name == name), None)

    def _on_profile(self, _value=None) -> None:
        profile = self.current_profile()
        if profile and profile.default_image_path and not self.image_path:
            self._set_image(profile.default_image_path)
        self._sync_cards()

    def _sync_cards(self) -> None:
        profile = self.current_profile()
        banned = profile.banned_phrases if profile else []
        for card in self.cards.values():
            card.set_context(banned, bool(self.image_path))

    def _choose_image(self) -> None:
        path = filedialog.askopenfilename(
            filetypes=[("Images", "*.png *.jpg *.jpeg *.webp"), ("All files", "*.*")])
        if path:
            self._set_image(path)

    def _set_image(self, path: str) -> bool:
        try:
            img = Image.open(path)
            img.load()
        except Exception:
            log.warning("Could not open image %s", path, exc_info=True)
            self.status.configure(text="That file could not be opened as an image. Try a PNG or JPG.")
            return False
        self.image_path = path
        w, h = img.size
        scale = min(150 / w, 110 / h)
        self._thumb = ctk.CTkImage(light_image=img, dark_image=img, size=(int(w * scale), int(h * scale)))
        self.image_label.configure(image=self._thumb, text="")
        self._sync_cards()
        return True

    def _clear_image(self) -> None:
        self.image_path = None
        self.image_label.configure(image=None, text="No image")
        self._sync_cards()

    # ---- background work ---------------------------------------------------
    def generate(self) -> None:
        if self._busy:  # the button doubles as Cancel while a request is running
            self.cancel()
            return
        profile = self.current_profile()
        if not profile:
            self.status.configure(text="Create an app profile first (Profiles tab).")
            return
        angle = self.angle_menu.get().replace(" ", "_")
        base = self.base_text.get("1.0", "end").strip()
        note, n = self.note.get(), int(self.variants_menu.get())
        self._gen_id += 1
        gid = self._gen_id
        self._set_busy(True)
        self.status.configure(text="Asking the AI for drafts... (click Cancel to stop waiting)")

        def work():
            try:
                svc = self.ctx.make_draft_service()
                self.results.put(("draft", gid, "ok", svc.generate(profile, angle, base, note, n)))
            except (DraftError, AIError) as exc:
                self.results.put(("draft", gid, "err", str(exc)))
            except Exception:
                log.exception("Unexpected error while drafting")
                self.results.put(("draft", gid, "err", "Unexpected error. Details are in the log file."))

        threading.Thread(target=work, daemon=True).start()

    def cancel(self) -> None:
        self._gen_id += 1  # any late reply carries an old id and is ignored
        self._set_busy(False)
        self.status.configure(text="Cancelled. Your current cards were not changed.")

    def _set_busy(self, busy: bool) -> None:
        self._busy = busy
        self.generate_btn.configure(text="Cancel" if busy else "Generate with AI",
                                    fg_color=("#b23b3b" if busy else ("#3a7ebf", "#1f538d")))

    def _shorten(self, card: PlatformCard) -> None:
        title, text, tags = card.parts()
        if not text:
            return
        used, limit = char_count(card.platform, text, tags), limit_for(card.platform)
        if used <= limit and limit > 5000:
            self.status.configure(text=f"{LABELS[card.platform]} has no practical length problem.")
            return
        if used <= limit:
            self.status.configure(text=f"{LABELS[card.platform]} already fits ({used}/{limit}).")
            return
        overhead = used - char_count(card.platform, text, ())
        self.status.configure(text=f"Shortening the {LABELS[card.platform]} text...")

        def work():
            try:
                svc = self.ctx.make_draft_service()
                self.results.put(("shorten", card, "ok", svc.shorten(card.platform, text, limit - overhead)))
            except (DraftError, AIError) as exc:
                self.results.put(("shorten", card, "err", str(exc)))
            except Exception:
                log.exception("Unexpected error while shortening")
                self.results.put(("shorten", card, "err", "Unexpected error. Details are in the log file."))

        threading.Thread(target=work, daemon=True).start()

    def _poll(self) -> None:
        try:
            while True:
                kind, ref, status, payload = self.results.get_nowait()
                if kind == "draft":
                    if ref != self._gen_id:
                        continue  # cancelled or superseded
                    self._set_busy(False)
                    if status == "ok":
                        for card in self.cards.values():
                            card.load_variants(payload.variants)
                        msg = f"Got {len(payload.variants)} variant(s). Use < > on each card to browse."
                        self.status.configure(text=" ".join([msg] + payload.warnings))
                    else:
                        self.status.configure(text=payload)
                elif kind == "publish":
                    self._on_publish_event(payload)
                elif status == "ok":  # shorten
                    ref.set_text(payload)
                    self.status.configure(text=f"{LABELS[ref.platform]} text shortened. Please re-read it.")
                else:
                    self.status.configure(text=payload)
        except queue.Empty:
            pass
        self.after(100, self._poll)

    # ---- preview and posting -----------------------------------------------
    def _drafts(self) -> tuple[list[dict], list[dict]]:
        """Returns (drafts to publish, preview entries) for the enabled, non-empty cards."""
        profile = self.current_profile()
        banned = profile.banned_phrases if profile else []
        drafts, entries = [], []
        for platform, card in self.cards.items():
            if not card.enabled.get():
                continue
            title, text, tags = card.parts()
            if not text and not title:
                continue
            problems = validate_post(platform, text, tags, title=title, banned=banned,
                                     has_image=bool(self.image_path))
            if platform in POSTABLE:
                drafts.append({"platform": platform, "text": text, "hashtags": tags, "title": title or None})
            else:
                problems = problems + ["Copy-only for now: use the Copy button and post it yourself."]
            entries.append({"label": LABELS[platform], "text": card.final_text(), "problems": problems})
        return drafts, entries

    def _instagram_preview_image(self, drafts: list[dict]) -> str | None:
        if not self.image_path or not any(d["platform"] == "instagram" for d in drafts):
            return None
        try:
            return self.ctx.make_image_service().prepare("instagram", self.image_path)
        except Exception:
            return None

    def preview(self) -> None:
        drafts, entries = self._drafts()
        if not entries:
            self.status.configure(text="Nothing to preview yet.")
            return
        PreviewDialog(self.winfo_toplevel(), entries, self._instagram_preview_image(drafts))

    def post_now(self) -> None:
        if self._posting:
            return
        profile = self.current_profile()
        if not profile:
            self.status.configure(text="Create an app profile first (Profiles tab).")
            return
        drafts, entries = self._drafts()
        if not drafts:
            self.status.configure(text="Nothing to post: enable Facebook or Instagram and add some text.")
            return
        PreviewDialog(self.winfo_toplevel(), entries, self._instagram_preview_image(drafts),
                      on_confirm=lambda: self._start_publish(profile, drafts))

    def _start_publish(self, profile, drafts: list[dict]) -> None:
        angle = self.angle_menu.get().replace(" ", "_")
        base = self.base_text.get("1.0", "end").strip()
        publisher = self.ctx.make_publisher()
        self.current_post_id = publisher.create_post(profile.id, angle, base, self.image_path, drafts)
        self.strip.reset()
        self._run_publish(publisher, None)

    def _retry(self, platform: str) -> None:
        if self.current_post_id and not self._posting:
            self._run_publish(self.ctx.make_publisher(), {platform})

    def _run_publish(self, publisher, only) -> None:
        self._posting = True
        self.post_btn.configure(state="disabled", text="Posting...")
        post_id = self.current_post_id

        def work():
            try:
                publisher.publish(post_id, only=only,
                                  on_event=lambda e: self.results.put(("publish", None, "event", e)))
            except Exception:
                log.exception("Publishing crashed")
                self.results.put(("publish", None, "event", {
                    "type": "done", "post_status": "failed",
                    "error": "Unexpected error. Details are in the log file."}))

        threading.Thread(target=work, daemon=True).start()

    def _on_publish_event(self, event: dict) -> None:
        if event["type"] == "status":
            self.strip.update_row(event)
            return
        self._posting = False
        self.post_btn.configure(state="normal", text="Post now")
        names = {"posted": "Everything was posted.",
                 "partial": "Some platforms did not post: see the list, use Retry.",
                 "failed": "Nothing was posted: see the list for the reason."}
        self.status.configure(text=event.get("error") or names.get(event["post_status"], ""))

    # ---- draft auto-save ---------------------------------------------------
    def snapshot(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "profile": self.profile_menu.get(), "angle": self.angle_menu.get(),
            "variants": self.variants_menu.get(), "base": self.base_text.get("1.0", "end").strip(),
            "note": self.note.get(), "image": self.image_path or "",
            "cards": {p: c.state() for p, c in self.cards.items()},
        }

    @staticmethod
    def _is_blank(snap: dict) -> bool:
        return not (snap["base"] or snap["note"] or snap["image"]
                    or any(c["text"] or c["title"] for c in snap["cards"].values()))

    def save_draft(self, announce: bool = True) -> None:
        snap = self.snapshot()
        if self._is_blank(snap):
            if announce:
                self.status.configure(text="Nothing to save yet.")
            return
        raw = json.dumps(snap, ensure_ascii=False)
        if raw != self._last_saved:
            self.ctx.settings.set(DRAFT_KEY, raw)
            self._last_saved = raw
        if announce:
            self.status.configure(text="Draft saved.")

    def _autosave_tick(self) -> None:
        try:
            self.save_draft(announce=False)
        except Exception:
            log.exception("Auto-save failed")
        self.after(30_000, self._autosave_tick)

    def _restore_draft(self) -> None:
        raw = self.ctx.settings.get(DRAFT_KEY)
        if not raw:
            return
        try:
            snap = json.loads(raw)
            names = [p.name for p in self.profiles]
            if snap.get("profile") in names:
                self.profile_menu.set(snap["profile"])
            if snap.get("angle") in [a.replace("_", " ") for a in ANGLES]:
                self.angle_menu.set(snap["angle"])
            if snap.get("variants") in ("1", "2", "3", "4", "5"):
                self.variants_menu.set(snap["variants"])
            self.base_text.insert("1.0", snap.get("base", ""))
            self.note.insert(0, snap.get("note", ""))
            if snap.get("image") and os.path.exists(snap["image"]):
                self._set_image(snap["image"])
            for platform, data in (snap.get("cards") or {}).items():
                if platform in self.cards:
                    self.cards[platform].apply_state(data)
            self._last_saved = raw
            self.status.configure(text="Restored your last draft.")
        except (ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError):
            log.warning("Stored draft could not be restored", exc_info=True)
        self._sync_cards()

    def clear_all(self) -> None:
        self.base_text.delete("1.0", "end")
        self.note.delete(0, "end")
        self._clear_image()
        for card in self.cards.values():
            card.clear()
        self.ctx.settings.set(DRAFT_KEY, "")
        self._last_saved = ""
        self.status.configure(text="Cleared.")




================================================================================
