================================================================================
from __future__ import annotations

import customtkinter as ctk
from PIL import Image


class PreviewDialog(ctk.CTkToplevel):
    """Shows exactly what will be published. With `on_confirm` it becomes the Post now confirmation."""

    def __init__(self, master, entries: list[dict], image_path: str | None = None, on_confirm=None):
        super().__init__(master)
        self.title("Confirm and post" if on_confirm else "Preview")
        self.geometry("680x680")
        self.on_confirm = on_confirm
        body = ctk.CTkTextbox(self, wrap="word")
        body.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=12, pady=(12, 6))
        for e in entries:
            body.insert("end", f"{e['label'].upper()}\n", )
            body.insert("end", f"{e['text']}\n")
            for problem in e["problems"]:
                body.insert("end", f"  ! {problem}\n")
            body.insert("end", "\n")
        body.configure(state="disabled")
        if image_path:
            try:
                img = Image.open(image_path)
                img.load()
                scale = min(260 / img.width, 200 / img.height)
                self._thumb = ctk.CTkImage(light_image=img, dark_image=img,
                                           size=(int(img.width * scale), int(img.height * scale)))
                ctk.CTkLabel(self, image=self._thumb, text="").pack(pady=4)
                ctk.CTkLabel(self, text="Instagram crop shown (other platforms keep your original shape).",
                             text_color="gray50").pack()
            except Exception:
                pass
        row = ctk.CTkFrame(self, fg_color="transparent")
        row.pack(pady=10)
        ctk.CTkButton(row, text="Close" if not on_confirm else "Cancel", width=100, fg_color="gray40",
                      command=self.destroy).pack(side="left", padx=6)
        if on_confirm:
            ctk.CTkButton(row, text="Post now", width=120, command=self._confirm).pack(side="left", padx=6)
        self.after(150, self._focus)

    def _focus(self) -> None:
        try:
            self.lift()
            self.focus_force()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _confirm(self) -> None:
        self.destroy()
        if self.on_confirm:
            self.on_confirm()




================================================================================
FILE: .\ui\profiles_tab.py
================================================================================
from __future__ import annotations

from tkinter import filedialog, messagebox

import logging
import sqlite3

import customtkinter as ctk

from promo_studio.core.textutil import format_links, parse_csv, parse_lines, parse_links
from promo_studio.data.models import AppProfile
from promo_studio.data.profile_io import export_profiles, import_profiles

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class ProfilesTab(ctk.CTkFrame):
    def __init__(self, master, ctx, on_change=None):
        super().__init__(master, fg_color="transparent")
        self.ctx, self.on_change, self.current_id = ctx, on_change, None
        self.columnconfigure(1, weight=1)
        self.rowconfigure(0, weight=1)

        left = ctk.CTkFrame(self, width=230)
        left.grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="ns", padx=6, pady=6)
        ctk.CTkButton(left, text="+ New profile", command=self.new_profile).pack(fill="x", padx=8, pady=8)
        self.list_frame = ctk.CTkScrollableFrame(left, width=200)
        self.list_frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=4, pady=4)
        io_row = ctk.CTkFrame(left, fg_color="transparent")
        io_row.pack(fill="x", padx=8, pady=8)
        ctk.CTkButton(io_row, text="Import", width=90, fg_color="gray40", command=self.import_json).pack(side="left", padx=2)
        ctk.CTkButton(io_row, text="Export", width=90, fg_color="gray40", command=self.export_json).pack(side="left", padx=2)

        form = ctk.CTkScrollableFrame(self)
        form.grid(row=0, column=1, sticky="nsew", padx=6, pady=6)
        form.columnconfigure(1, weight=1)
        self.entries: dict[str, ctk.CTkEntry | ctk.CTkTextbox] = {}
        fields = [
            ("name", "App name", "entry"), ("pitch", "One-line pitch", "entry"),
            ("description", "Description", "text"), ("audience", "Target audience", "entry"),
            ("features", "Key features (one per line)", "text"), ("tone", "Tone (e.g. warm, concise)", "entry"),
            ("store_links", "Store links (name=url per line)", "text"),
            ("seed_keywords", "Seed keywords (comma separated)", "entry"),
            ("banned_phrases", "Banned phrases (comma separated)", "entry"),
            ("default_hashtags", "Default hashtags (comma separated)", "entry"),
            ("default_image_path", "Default image path", "entry"),
        ]
        for row, (key, label, kind) in enumerate(fields):
            ctk.CTkLabel(form, text=label, anchor="nw").grid(row=row, column=0, sticky="nw", padx=8, pady=6)
            widget = ctk.CTkEntry(form) if kind == "entry" else ctk.CTkTextbox(form, height=80, wrap="word")
            widget.grid(row=row, column=1, sticky="ew", padx=8, pady=6)
            self.entries[key] = widget
        ctk.CTkButton(form, text="Browse...", width=80, command=self._browse).grid(
            row=len(fields) - 1, column=2, padx=(0, 8))
        buttons = ctk.CTkFrame(form, fg_color="transparent")
        buttons.grid(row=len(fields), column=1, sticky="w", padx=8, pady=12)
        ctk.CTkButton(buttons, text="Save", width=90, command=self.save).pack(side="left", padx=4)
        ctk.CTkButton(buttons, text="Duplicate", width=90, command=self.duplicate).pack(side="left", padx=4)
        ctk.CTkButton(buttons, text="Delete", width=90, fg_color="#b23b3b", hover_color="#8f2e2e",
                      command=self.delete).pack(side="left", padx=4)
        self.status = ctk.CTkLabel(form, text="", anchor="w")
        self.status.grid(row=len(fields) + 1, column=1, sticky="w", padx=8)
        self.reload_list()

    # ---- list ------------------------------------------------------------
    def reload_list(self) -> None:
        for child in self.list_frame.winfo_children():
            child.destroy()
        for profile in self.ctx.db.list_profiles():
            ctk.CTkButton(self.list_frame, text=profile.name, anchor="w", fg_color="transparent",
                          text_color=("gray10", "gray90"), hover_color=("gray75", "gray30"),
                          command=lambda p=profile: self.load(p)).pack(fill="x", pady=1)

    def new_profile(self) -> None:
        self.load(AppProfile(name=""))

    def load(self, p: AppProfile) -> None:
        self.current_id = p.id
        values = {
            "name": p.name, "pitch": p.pitch, "description": p.description, "audience": p.audience,
            "features": "\n".join(p.features), "tone": p.tone, "store_links": format_links(p.store_links),
            "seed_keywords": ", ".join(p.seed_keywords), "banned_phrases": ", ".join(p.banned_phrases),
            "default_hashtags": ", ".join(p.default_hashtags), "default_image_path": p.default_image_path,
        }
        for key, widget in self.entries.items():
            self._set(widget, values[key])
        self.status.configure(text="")

    @staticmethod
    def _set(widget, value: str) -> None:
        if isinstance(widget, ctk.CTkTextbox):
            widget.delete("1.0", "end")
            widget.insert("1.0", value)
        else:
            widget.delete(0, "end")
            widget.insert(0, value)

    @staticmethod
    def _get(widget) -> str:
        return widget.get("1.0", "end").strip() if isinstance(widget, ctk.CTkTextbox) else widget.get().strip()

    def _read(self) -> AppProfile:
        g = {k: self._get(w) for k, w in self.entries.items()}
        return AppProfile(
            id=self.current_id, name=g["name"], pitch=g["pitch"], description=g["description"],
            audience=g["audience"], features=parse_lines(g["features"]), tone=g["tone"],
            store_links=parse_links(g["store_links"]), seed_keywords=parse_csv(g["seed_keywords"]),
            banned_phrases=parse_csv(g["banned_phrases"]), default_hashtags=parse_csv(g["default_hashtags"]),
            default_image_path=g["default_image_path"])

    # ---- actions ---------------------------------------------------------
    def save(self) -> None:
        profile = self._read()
        if not profile.name:
            self.status.configure(text="Give the profile a name.")
            return
        try:
            self.current_id = self.ctx.db.save_profile(profile)
        except sqlite3.IntegrityError:
            self.status.configure(text=f'A profile named "{profile.name}" already exists. Pick another name.')
            return
        except Exception:
            log.exception("Could not save profile")
            self.status.configure(text="Could not save the profile. Details are in the log file.")
            return
        self.status.configure(text="Saved.")
        self.reload_list()
        if self.on_change:
            self.on_change()

    def duplicate(self) -> None:
        profile = self._read()
        profile.id, profile.name = None, (profile.name or "Profile") + " copy"
        self.current_id = None
        self._set(self.entries["name"], profile.name)
        self.status.configure(text="Edit the name and click Save to create the copy.")

    def delete(self) -> None:
        if self.current_id is None:
            return
        if messagebox.askyesno("Delete profile", "Delete this profile? This cannot be undone."):
            self.ctx.db.delete_profile(self.current_id)
            self.new_profile()
            self.reload_list()
            if self.on_change:
                self.on_change()

    def _browse(self) -> None:
        path = filedialog.askopenfilename(filetypes=[("Images", "*.png *.jpg *.jpeg *.webp")])
        if path:
            self._set(self.entries["default_image_path"], path)

    def export_json(self) -> None:
        path = filedialog.asksaveasfilename(defaultextension=".json", initialfile="promo_profiles.json",
                                            filetypes=[("JSON", "*.json")])
        if not path:
            return
        try:
            count = export_profiles(self.ctx.db, path)
            self.status.configure(text=f"Exported {count} profile(s).")
        except OSError:
            log.exception("Export failed")
            self.status.configure(text="Could not write that file. Choose another location.")

    def import_json(self) -> None:
        path = filedialog.askopenfilename(filetypes=[("JSON", "*.json")])
        if not path:
            return
        try:
            created, updated = import_profiles(self.ctx.db, path)
        except ValueError as exc:
            self.status.configure(text=str(exc))
            return
        self.status.configure(text=f"Imported: {created} new, {updated} updated (matched by name).")
        self.reload_list()
        if self.on_change:
            self.on_change()




================================================================================
FILE: .\ui\results_strip.py
================================================================================
from __future__ import annotations

import webbrowser

import customtkinter as ctk

LABELS = {"facebook": "Facebook", "instagram": "Instagram", "x": "X", "reddit": "Reddit"}
TEXT = {"posting": "posting...", "posted": "posted", "failed": "failed", "blocked": "not sent",
        "needs_check": "unconfirmed"}
COLOR = {"posting": ("gray35", "gray70"), "posted": "#2e9e5b", "failed": "#d64545", "blocked": "#d98e04",
         "needs_check": "#d98e04"}


class ResultsStrip(ctk.CTkFrame):
    """One row per platform: live status, a link to the post, and Retry for failures."""

    def __init__(self, master, on_retry):
        super().__init__(master, fg_color="transparent")
        self.on_retry, self.rows = on_retry, {}
        self.columnconfigure(1, weight=1)

    def reset(self) -> None:
        for child in self.winfo_children():
            child.destroy()
        self.rows.clear()

    def update_row(self, event: dict) -> None:
        platform, status = event["platform"], event["status"]
        if platform not in self.rows:
            r = len(self.rows)
            name = ctk.CTkLabel(self, text=LABELS.get(platform, platform), width=80, anchor="w",
                                font=ctk.CTkFont(weight="bold"))
            name.grid(row=r, column=0, sticky="w", padx=(4, 6))
            label = ctk.CTkLabel(self, text="", anchor="w", justify="left", wraplength=520)
            label.grid(row=r, column=1, sticky="w")
            holder = ctk.CTkFrame(self, fg_color="transparent")
            holder.grid(row=r, column=2, sticky="e", padx=4)
            self.rows[platform] = (label, holder)
        label, holder = self.rows[platform]
        for child in holder.winfo_children():
            child.destroy()
        text = TEXT.get(status, status)
        if event.get("message") and status != "posted":
            text += f": {event['message']}"
        label.configure(text=text, text_color=COLOR.get(status, ("gray35", "gray70")))
        if status == "posted" and event.get("url"):
            ctk.CTkButton(holder, text="Open", width=60,
                          command=lambda u=event["url"]: webbrowser.open(u)).pack(side="left", padx=2)
        if status == "failed":
            ctk.CTkButton(holder, text="Retry", width=60, fg_color="#b23b3b", hover_color="#8f2e2e",
                          command=lambda p=platform: self.on_retry(p)).pack(side="left", padx=2)




================================================================================
FILE: .\ui\settings_tab.py
================================================================================
from __future__ import annotations

import datetime as dt
import logging
import threading

import customtkinter as ctk

from promo_studio.adapters.graph import GraphError
from promo_studio.ai.base import AIError
from promo_studio.config.secrets import get_secret, mask, set_secret

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class SettingsTab(ctk.CTkScrollableFrame):
    def __init__(self, master, ctx):
        super().__init__(master, fg_color="transparent")
        self.ctx = ctx
        self.columnconfigure(1, weight=1)
        self.vars: dict[str, ctk.StringVar] = {}
        self.secret_vars: dict[str, ctk.StringVar] = {}
        self.secret_labels: dict[str, ctk.CTkLabel] = {}
        self._r = 0
        s = ctx.settings

        self._section("AI drafting")
        self._option("Provider", "ai.provider", ["gemini", "ollama"])
        self._field("Gemini model name", "ai.model")
        self._secret("Gemini API key", "GEMINI_API_KEY")
        self._field("Ollama model", "ai.ollama_model")
        self._field("Ollama URL", "ai.ollama_url")
        self._field("AI calls per minute", "ai.calls_per_minute", width=80)
        self._field("Default variants (1-5)", "ai.variants", width=80)

        self._section("Hashtag limits per post")
        self._field("X", "hashtags.max.x", width=80)
        self._field("Instagram", "hashtags.max.instagram", width=80)
        self._field("Facebook", "hashtags.max.facebook", width=80)

        self._section("Publishing accounts")
        self._field("Instagram user ID", "ig.user_id")
        self._secret("Instagram access token", "IG_ACCESS_TOKEN")
        self.expiry_label = ctk.CTkLabel(self, text="", anchor="w")
        self.expiry_label.grid(row=self._r, column=1, sticky="w", padx=10)
        self._r += 1
        self._field("Facebook Page ID", "fb.page_id")
        self._secret("Facebook Page access token", "FB_PAGE_TOKEN")
        self._secret("Image host API key (ImgBB)", "IMAGE_HOST_KEY")
        self._field("Meta API version", "meta.api_version", width=100)
        self._field("Max parallel posts", "post.max_parallel", width=80)
        self._field("Auto-retries per platform", "post.retries", width=80)
        self._refresh_expiry()

        self._section("Appearance")
        self._option("Theme", "theme", ["system", "light", "dark"])

        row = ctk.CTkFrame(self, fg_color="transparent")
        row.grid(row=self._r, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="w", padx=10, pady=(16, 4))
        ctk.CTkButton(row, text="Save settings", command=self.save).pack(side="left", padx=4)
        self.buttons = []
        for text, action in (("Test AI", self._test_ai), ("Test Instagram", lambda: self._test("instagram")),
                             ("Test Facebook", lambda: self._test("facebook")),
                             ("Refresh Instagram token", self._refresh_token)):
            b = ctk.CTkButton(row, text=text, fg_color="gray40", command=action)
            b.pack(side="left", padx=4)
            self.buttons.append(b)
        self._r += 1
        self.status = ctk.CTkLabel(self, text="", anchor="w", wraplength=620, justify="left")
        self.status.grid(row=self._r, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="w", padx=10, pady=4)

    # ---- layout helpers ------------------------------------------------------
    def _section(self, text: str) -> None:
        ctk.CTkLabel(self, text=text, font=ctk.CTkFont(size=16, weight="bold"), anchor="w").grid(
            row=self._r, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="w", padx=10, pady=(16, 4))
        self._r += 1

    def _field(self, label: str, key: str, width: int = 300) -> None:
        ctk.CTkLabel(self, text=label, anchor="w").grid(row=self._r, column=0, sticky="w", padx=10, pady=4)
        self.vars[key] = ctk.StringVar(value=self.ctx.settings.get(key))
        ctk.CTkEntry(self, textvariable=self.vars[key], width=width).grid(row=self._r, column=1, sticky="w", padx=10)
        self._r += 1

    def _option(self, label: str, key: str, values: list[str]) -> None:
        ctk.CTkLabel(self, text=label, anchor="w").grid(row=self._r, column=0, sticky="w", padx=10, pady=4)
        self.vars[key] = ctk.StringVar(value=self.ctx.settings.get(key))
        ctk.CTkOptionMenu(self, values=values, variable=self.vars[key]).grid(row=self._r, column=1, sticky="w", padx=10)
        self._r += 1

    def _secret(self, label: str, name: str) -> None:
        ctk.CTkLabel(self, text=label, anchor="w").grid(row=self._r, column=0, sticky="w", padx=10, pady=4)
        self.secret_vars[name] = ctk.StringVar()
        ctk.CTkEntry(self, textvariable=self.secret_vars[name], width=300, show="*",
                     placeholder_text="paste to replace").grid(row=self._r, column=1, sticky="w", padx=10)
        self._r += 1
        self.secret_labels[name] = ctk.CTkLabel(self, text=f"Current: {mask(get_secret(name))}", anchor="w",
                                                text_color="gray50")
        self.secret_labels[name].grid(row=self._r, column=1, sticky="w", padx=10)
        self._r += 1

    # ---- actions ---------------------------------------------------------------
    def save(self) -> None:
        for key, var in self.vars.items():
            self.ctx.settings.set(key, var.get().strip())
        stored = []
        for name, var in self.secret_vars.items():
            if var.get().strip():
                stored.append(f"{name} -> {set_secret(name, var.get().strip())}")
                var.set("")
                self.secret_labels[name].configure(text=f"Current: {mask(get_secret(name))}")
        ctk.set_appearance_mode(self.vars["theme"].get() or "system")
        self.status.configure(text="Settings saved." + (f" Stored: {', '.join(stored)}." if stored else ""))

    def _refresh_expiry(self) -> None:
        raw = self.ctx.settings.get("ig.token_expires")
        try:
            date = dt.date.fromisoformat(raw)
            self.expiry_label.configure(text=f"Token expires {date} ({(date - dt.date.today()).days} days left)")
        except ValueError:
            self.expiry_label.configure(text="Token expiry unknown: click Refresh Instagram token after pasting it.")

    def _background(self, work) -> None:
        for b in self.buttons:
            b.configure(state="disabled")
        self.status.configure(text="Working...")

        def run():
            try:
                msg = work()
            except (AIError, GraphError) as exc:
                msg = f"Problem: {getattr(exc, 'message', None) or exc}"
            except Exception:
                log.exception("Settings action failed")
                msg = "Unexpected error. Details are in the log file."
            self.after(0, lambda: self._finish(msg))

        threading.Thread(target=run, daemon=True).start()

    def _finish(self, msg: str) -> None:
        self.status.configure(text=msg)
        self._refresh_expiry()
        for b in self.buttons:
            b.configure(state="normal")

    def _test_ai(self) -> None:
        self.save()
        self._background(lambda: "AI connection works." if self.ctx.make_provider().health_check()
                         else "The provider replied but the test failed.")

    def _test(self, platform: str) -> None:
        self.save()

        def work():
            adapter = self.ctx.make_adapters().get(platform)
            if adapter is None:
                return f"Add the {platform} ID and access token first, then save."
            if adapter.check_auth():
                return f"{platform.title()} connected as {adapter.last_account}."
            return f"{platform.title()} connection failed: {adapter.last_error}"

        self._background(work)

    def _refresh_token(self) -> None:
        self.save()

        def work():
            adapter = self.ctx.make_adapters().get("instagram")
            if adapter is None:
                return "Add the Instagram user ID and access token first, then save."
            token, seconds = adapter.refresh_token()
            set_secret("IG_ACCESS_TOKEN", token)
            self.secret_labels["IG_ACCESS_TOKEN"].configure(text=f"Current: {mask(token)}")
            expires = dt.date.today() + dt.timedelta(seconds=seconds)
            self.ctx.settings.set("ig.token_expires", expires.isoformat())
            return f"Instagram token refreshed. It now expires on {expires}."

        self._background(work)
